### [93] [String-Monodromy Rigidity from Six-Point Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25025)
*Jianqi Sheng*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 有限粒子一致性将弦散射的特征单值性固定到一个明确程度：在无质量双序恒等模型中，两个精确六点留数生成标量五边形和奇Fay恒等式，迫使四点零核满足 q''=κq，仅剩线性、三角、双曲分支；交叉对称目标结合正无减除色散与非零重谱权重选出三角分支，并确定Veneziano胚。双尺度反例表明模进入是独立输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：弦散射的特征单值性（characteristic monodromy）能否仅由S矩阵一致性导出，而无需预设世界面（worldsheet）。这关系到弦理论中通常依赖世界面几何的高阶约束是否有更基本的S矩阵起源，以及首次性原理推导所缺失的原则。

Method: 在无质量双序恒等模型（massless doubly ordered identity model）中分析两个精确六点留数（six-point residues），由此生成标量五边形（scalar pentagon）与奇Fay恒等式（odd Fay identity）；对四点零核（null kernel）施加约束 q''=κq；结合交叉对称目标（crossing-symmetric target）与相反KLT模块中的正无减除色散关系（positive unsubtracted dispersion）及非零重谱权重；构造双尺度反例检验模进入的独立性。

Result: 有限粒子一致性仅允许三个分支：线性、三角、双曲。交叉对称目标在正无减除色散与非零重谱权重下选出三角分支；矩确定性（moment determinacy）将Veneziano胚固定到尺度（up to scale）。双尺度反例证明模进入（module entrance）是独立输入。

Conclusion: 结果同时给出弦散射的有限到无限刚性机制，以及首次性原理推导所需的精确缺失原则；即特征单值性可由有限多重性分解与一致性约束大幅固定，但模进入仍需额外独立输入。

Abstract: Can the characteristic monodromy of string scattering be derived from S-matrix consistency alone, without assuming a worldsheet? We show that finite-particle consistency fixes it to a sharply defined extent. In a massless doubly ordered identity model, two exact six-point residues generate a scalar pentagon and the odd Fay identity, forcing every analytic four-point null kernel to satisfy $q''=κq$ and leaving only the linear, trigonometric, and hyperbolic branches. This converts finite-multiplicity factorization into an all-orders constraint normally associated with worldsheet geometry. For a crossing-symmetric target in the opposite KLT module, positive unsubtracted dispersion with nonzero heavy spectral weight selects the trigonometric branch, and moment determinacy fixes the Veneziano germ up to scale. A two-scale counterexample proves that module entrance is an independent input. Our result therefore identifies both a finite-to-infinite rigidity mechanism for string scattering and the precise missing principle needed for a first-principles derivation.

</details>


### [94] [Eigenvectors and Eigenvalues of $p$-Curvature Operators for KZ--type Differential Connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25269)
*Vitaly Tarasov,Alexander Varchenko*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究特征 $p$ KZ-型联络的 $p$-曲率算子，给出其特征向量与特征值的 Bethe-ansatz 型构造，特征向量表述为特征 $p$ 超几何积分并延拓为平坦本征截面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征 $p$ 下 KZ-型联络的 $p$-曲率算子是可交换自同态，其谱理论（特征向量、特征值）缺乏显式构造，本文旨在给出类似 Bethe-ansatz 的显式描述。

Method: 采用 Bethe-ansatz 型构造方法，将特征向量表示为特征 $p$ 超几何积分，并利用 Frobenius 邻域上的延拓技巧将其发展为平坦本征截面。

Result: 成功构造出 $p$-曲率算子的特征向量与特征值，特征向量由特征 $p$ 超几何积分给出，且可延拓为对应 Bethe 点 Frobenius 邻域上的平坦本征截面。

Conclusion: 该构造为特征 $p$ KZ-型联络的 $p$-曲率算子提供了显式谱信息，将 Bethe-ansatz 与超几何积分方法推广至特征 $p$ 情形。

Abstract: A KZ-type connection in characteristic $p$ has $p$-curvature operators. These operators are commuting endomorphisms of the connection. We present a Bethe-ansatz-type construction of their eigenvectors and eigenvalues. The eigenvector is constructed as the value of a characteristic $p$ hypergeometric integral and extends to a flat eigensection over the Frobenius neighborhood of the corresponding Bethe point.

</details>

*Kohei Noda*

<details>

Motivation: 先前关于行列式平面库仑气体（等价于随机矩阵/正交多项式系综）配分函数渐近的工作多针对单一或少量特定奇异点类型与特定区域结构，结果分散。本文旨在建立一个统一的框架，把体、半硬、硬、软边等跳变型奇异点与硬壁（中心圆盘、圆盘补集、圆环）纳入同一分析，从而给出适用范围更广的配分函数到 O(1) 的显式渐近展开。

Method: 考虑带光滑径向势加光滑径向扰动的行列式平面库仑气体，允许跳变型奇异点，并设置硬壁区域（中心圆盘、圆盘补集或圆环）；通过渐近分析推导配分函数的显式渐近展开，精确到并包含 1 阶项。

Result: 获得了配分函数精确到并包含 1 阶项的显式渐近展开式，且该结果统一并推广了此前许多相关工作。

Conclusion: 该工作为具有跳变型奇异点与硬壁约束的行列式平面库仑气体配分函数提供了一个统一、普适的 O(1) 级渐近展开，并可涵盖并推广此前诸多分散的结论。

Abstract: We study determinantal planar Coulomb gases with a smooth radial potential, a smooth radial perturbation, jump-type singularities (including the bulk, semi-hard, hard, and soft-edge regimes), and a hard wall region given by a centered disk, a disk complement, or an annulus. We obtain explicit asymptotic expansions of the partition functions up to and including the term of order $1$. Our results unify and generalize many of the earlier works.

</details>

Result: 得到了具有一般短程排斥相互作用的稀薄玻色气体基态能量密度的上界。

Conclusion: 文献[1]的方法可从硬球推广到一般短程排斥势，从而给出该类稀薄玻色气体基态能量密度的上界。

Abstract: We establish an upper bound for the ground state energy density of a dilute Bose gas, interacting through a general short-range, repulsive potential. To reach this goal, we extend the approach recently introduced in [1] for hard spheres.

Result: 证明了亚临界质量间隙与自由边界方形盒子上Lee-Yang间隙的8/15次幂可比较；同时获得了压力的场导数的均匀阶乘界。

Abstract: We prove that the subcritical mass gap is comparable to the Lee-Yang gap over free-boundary square boxes raised to the power $8/15$. Random-current moment bounds, a correlation inequality and a finite block expansion give the upper bound. The lower bound follows from the Lee-Yang product, the susceptibility and the critical magnetization. We also obtain uniform factorial bounds on the field derivatives of the pressure.

</details>

### [99] [Finite Rodriguez-Villegas Approximants to the Riemann $ξ$-Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25564)
*Alisson M. Silva*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了一列有限Rodriguez-Villegas变换，在临界带内局部一致收敛到Riemann ξ函数，其输入为来自Riemann theta核的正对称剖面，该剖面是x(1-x)的Stieltjes函数，其Bernstein多项式给出互反分子与精确有限函数方程，且同一分子可实为费米子超迹，Bernstein多项式为归一化Gibbs迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Riemann ξ函数（及其零点分布）的解析刻画是数论核心问题；有限逼近与函数方程的可计算实现有助于从有限代数/统计力学对象理解其整体结构。作者试图通过Riemann theta核构造一个有限、精确的逼近框架，把ξ函数与Stieltjes函数、Bernstein多项式、超迹和Gibbs迹联系起来。

Method: 1. 以Riemann theta核为起点，经双曲正割核卷积与logistic坐标变换，得到单位区间上的正对称剖面；2. 该剖面表示为x(1-x)的Stieltjes函数；3. 取其Bernstein多项式，导出互反分子与精确有限函数方程；4. 将同一分子实现为费米子超迹，将Bernstein多项式实现为归一化Gibbs迹；5. 构造有限Rodriguez-Villegas变换序列并证其在临界带内局部一致收敛到ξ函数。

Result: 构造的有限Rodriguez-Villegas变换序列在临界带内局部一致收敛到Riemann ξ函数；所涉剖面为x(1-x)的Stieltjes函数；Bernstein多项式产生互反分子与精确有限函数方程；这些分子具有费米子超迹的精确实现，Bernstein多项式为归一化Gibbs迹。

Conclusion: 该工作为Riemann ξ函数提供了一个由有限、精确、具统计力学与超对称代数解释的对象构成的逼近体系，将解析数论中的ξ函数与Stieltjes函数、Bernstein逼近、超迹及Gibbs迹统一起来，可作为研究其函数方程与零点结构的有限模型框架。

Abstract: We construct a sequence of finite Rodriguez-Villegas transforms converging locally uniformly to the Riemann $ξ$-function in the critical strip. The input is a positive symmetric profile on the unit interval obtained from the Riemann theta kernel through convolution with the hyperbolic-secant kernel and the logistic coordinate. The profile is a Stieltjes function of $x(1-x)$. Its Bernstein polynomials produce reciprocal numerators and exact finite functional equations. The same numerators admit an exact realization as fermionic supertraces, while the Bernstein polynomials are normalized Gibbs traces.

</details>


### [100] [Finite images of braid group representations and algebraic solutions of KZ-type equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25805)
*Haru Negami*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文用有限单值群连接群表示与微分方程的代数解，针对Katz-Long-Moody构造，对固定有限像输入分类了使新表示仍具有限像的参数值，并给出相应KZ型方程全体解为代数的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限单值群为群表示与微分方程的代数解之间提供桥梁。Katz-Long-Moody构造把自由群与辫群的半直积表示变换为同群的新表示，并与Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov（KZ）型方程相关，但其有限像参数与代数解条件尚缺系统刻画。

Method: 固定一个具有有限像的输入表示，考察Katz-Long-Moody构造产生的表示在参数变化下的像；分别在半直积整体、其自由群子群和辫群子群上分类使表示具有有限像的参数值，并利用单值群与KZ型方程及Fuchsian系统解的关系进行论证。

Result: 对半直积及其自由群、辫群子群，给出了使像有限的参数值的分类；发现辫群像的有限性与可容许参数无关；由此得到相应正则奇点KZ型方程所有解均为代数解的充要条件；限制到自由群时，进一步刻画了相关Fuchsian系统有限单值性与所有解的代数性。

Conclusion: Katz-Long-Moody构造下的有限单值性可由参数条件精确判定，辫群部分具有参数无关的有限性；该分类为KZ型方程与Fuchsian系统的代数解提供充要条件，并与代数超几何函数的经典问题相联系。

Abstract: Finite monodromy provides a bridge between group representations and algebraic solutions of differential equations. We study this connection for the Katz-Long-Moody construction, which transforms representations of the semidirect product of a free group and a braid group into new representations of the same group and is related to Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov (KZ)-type equations. For a fixed finite-image input, we classify the parameter values for which the resulting representations have finite image, both on the semidirect product and on its free-group and braid-group subgroups. In particular, finiteness of the braid-group image is independent of the admissible parameter. These results give necessary and sufficient conditions for all solutions of the corresponding regular-singular KZ-type equations to be algebraic. On restriction to the free group, they also characterize finite monodromy and algebraicity of all solutions of the associated Fuchsian systems, connecting the classification to classical questions about algebraic hypergeometric functions.

</details>


### [101] [Disentangling Measurement Protocol from Material Physics in Isotypic and Anisotypic SiC/Si Heterojunctions: A Metrology-Driven Study for Betavoltaic Energy Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26277)
*Mikhail V. Dolgopolov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 比较同型(n-SiC/n-Si)与异型(n-SiC/p-Si)异质结，发现二线法测得的同型结构低偏压电流增强主要源于引线与接触压降；采用四线Kelvin传感后该电流大幅下降至体/界面限制水平。KPFM与STS显示位错富集区界面态密度高达5×10¹² cm⁻²eV⁻¹，可解释局部响应增强。异型结呈真实二极管特性，β辐照下开路电压约1.2 V；电荷泵电容测试30分钟后达0.25 V（同型仅0.05 V），能量比约25。结论：异型SiC/Si是β伏特转换的优选平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清同型与异型SiC/Si异质结在电学表征中的真实差异，排除二线测量中引线与接触压降造成的假象，为β伏特（betavoltaic）能量转换平台的选型提供可靠判据。

Method: 综合Kelvin探针力显微镜(KPFM)、扫描隧道谱(STS)、Keithley 2450精密电学测量、四线Kelvin传感以及0.1 F超级电容器储能测试；并建立包含四线Kelvin传感、发生器极性(阴极接LO)与高阻输出关断态的测量-解释框架。

Result: 二线探测下同型结构低偏压电流增强，四线传感消除外引线与接触压降后该电流塌缩至更低的体/界面限制水平；位错富集区界面态密度可达5×10¹² cm⁻²eV⁻¹。异型结呈二极管式I-V特性，β辐照下开路电压约1.2 V；采用专利电荷泵转换器30分钟后异型达0.25 V、同型仅0.05 V，能量比约25。

Conclusion: 在正确测量（四线Kelvin传感等）条件下，异型SiC/Si异质结是β伏特转换的优选平台；同型结构的表观电流增强属测量假象而非本征优势。

Abstract: We compare isotypic (n-SiC/n-Si) and anisotypic (n-SiC/p-Si) heterojunctions using Kelvin probe force microscopy, scanning tunnelling spectroscopy, precision electrical measurements with a Keithley 2450 SourceMeter, and a 0.1 farad supercapacitor charge-storage test. Two-wire probing shows an enhanced low-bias current in the isotypic structures; four-wire Kelvin sensing removes the voltage-drop contribution of external leads and contacts and collapses this current to a much lower bulk and interface limited level. The high density of interface states detected by KPFM and STS, up to five times ten to the twelfth per square centimetre per electronvolt in dislocation-rich regions, provides a plausible origin for the enhanced local response. Anisotypic junctions exhibit a genuine diode-like current-voltage characteristic and an open-circuit voltage of about 1.2 volts under beta irradiation. In the capacitor test with the patented charge-pump converter, the anisotypic structure reaches 0.25 volts versus 0.05 volts for the isotypic one after 30 minutes, giving an energy ratio of about 25. We formulate a measurement-interpretation framework based on four-wire Kelvin sensing, generator polarity with cathode to LO, and high-impedance output-off state. Our results show that anisotypic SiC/Si heterojunctions are the preferred platform for betavoltaic conversion when measured correctly.

</details>


### [102] [Asymptotics of Hankel determinants for potentials with singular edge points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26412)
*Dan Dai,Jia-Hao Du,Chenhao Lu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了具有高阶边缘奇性的酉随机矩阵系综的Hankel行列式的大n渐近性，显式给出了渐近展开中的常数项，并证明了特征值相关核的普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高阶边缘奇性下随机矩阵系综的Hankel行列式渐近行为及其相关常数项，是高阶临界普适性理论中的重要问题。

Method: 采用正交多项式Riemann-Hilbert问题的Deift-Zhou非线性最速下降分析。

Result: 显式计算出渐近展开中的常数项，其涉及与Painlevé I（P_I^{2k}）层级相关的Hamiltonian的正则化积分；同时证明了奇异边缘附近特征值相关核的普适性，并导出了由P_I^{2k}方程特殊解相关函数表示的极限核。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了高阶边缘奇性Hankel行列式的完整大n渐近，包括常数项与边缘普适核，推广了经典边缘奇性的相应结果。

Abstract: We establish the large-$n$ asymptotics of Hankel determinants for unitary random matrix ensembles possessing a higher-order edge singularity. We focus on ensembles where the equilibrium measure is supported on a single interval and the limiting eigenvalue density vanishes to order $2k+\frac{1}{2}$ for $k \in \mathbb{N}$. Notably, we explicitly evaluate the constant term in the asymptotic expansion, which involves a regularized integral of the Hamiltonian associated with the Painlevé I ($P_{\rm I}^{2k}$) hierarchy. As a by-product, we also prove the universality of the eigenvalue correlation kernel near this singular edge and derive a limiting kernel expressed through functions related to a special solution of the $P_{\rm I}^{2k}$ equation. Our method relies on the Deift-Zhou nonlinear steepest descent analysis for the Riemann-Hilbert problem of orthogonal polynomials.

</details>


### [103] [An upper-symbol approach to $Φ^4_d$ limits of Bose gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26518)
*Hao Liang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用相对熵方法，研究环面上大正则玻色气体高温极限下的Φ⁴_d测度推导，统一证明了相对自由能收敛及所有固定阶重整化约化密度矩阵到Hartree对应项的Hilbert–Schmidt收敛，并将二维和三维的相互作用范围指数改进至η<1/2与η<1/26。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在二维和三维环面上从大正则玻色气体严格推导Φ⁴_d测度是数学物理中的重要问题。已有工作（如Jougla–Rougerie、Fröhlich–Knowles–Schlein–Sohinger以及Nam–Zhu–Zhu）分别在二维和三维给出了相关结果，但存在相互作用范围指数限制较严、收敛意义较弱（如仅有限阶弱收敛）等不足，需要一个更简洁且统一的证明框架来改进这些结果。

Method: 建立在作者此前工作的上符号变分方法之上，采用相对熵工具，给出相对自由能收敛以及所有固定阶重整化约化密度矩阵到Hartree对应项的Hilbert–Schmidt收敛的统一且简洁的证明。其中相互作用程为ε=λ^η（λ为逆温度），分析了二维和三维各自适用的η范围。

Result: 在二维中，结果对每个固定的0<η<1/2成立，改进并推广了Jougla–Rougerie（2026）的结果以及Fröhlich–Knowles–Schlein–Sohinger（2025）的局部Φ⁴_2推导；结合经典近似，首次给出了对每个固定指数0<η<1/2均有效的局部Φ⁴_2推导，使多项式指数可从下方任意接近稀薄性阈值。在三维中，适用于0<η<1/26，将Nam–Zhu–Zhu（2025）定理2.8建立的有限多阶弱收敛强化为每个固定阶的Hilbert–Schmidt收敛。

Conclusion: 相对熵方法为Φ⁴_d测度的高温推导提供了统一而简洁的框架，在二维与三维均拓展了相互作用程的有效范围并强化了收敛意义，二维结果达到接近稀薄性阈值的最优指数范围，三维结果将弱收敛提升为Hilbert–Schmidt收敛。

Abstract: We study the high-temperature derivation of $Φ^4_d$ measures from grand-canonical Bose gases on two- and three-dimensional tori. Building on the upper-symbol variational approach of the author's earlier work, we use relative entropy to give a simple, unified proof of relative free-energy convergence and Hilbert--Schmidt convergence of all fixed-order rescaled reduced density matrices to their Hartree counterparts. For interaction ranges $\varepsilon=λ^η$, where $λ$ is the inverse temperature, these results hold for every fixed $0<η<1/2$ in two dimensions and $0<η<1/26$ in three. In two dimensions, we improve the result of Jougla and Rougerie (2026) and the local $Φ^4_2$ derivation of Fröhlich, Knowles, Schlein and Sohinger (2025). Combined with classical approximation, the two-dimensional result gives the first local $Φ^4_2$ derivation valid for every fixed exponent $0<η<1/2$, allowing polynomial exponents arbitrarily close to the diluteness threshold from below. In three dimensions, we strengthen the weak convergence at finitely many orders established by Nam, R.~Zhu and X.~Zhu [Theorem~2.8](2025) to Hilbert--Schmidt convergence at every fixed order.

</details>

### [104] [Eccentricity and Inclination Excitation of Stellar Orbits around merging Black-Hole Binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25109)
*Hao Gao,Hanlun Lei*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个同时包含三体扰动和后牛顿效应的广义框架，研究绕双黑洞（BHB）恒星轨道中由共振驱动的偏心率和/或倾角激发机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辨识并合致密双星的形成通道是引力波天体物理学的核心难题。作者希望通过研究共振驱动的轨道动力学，寻找可用于间接探测并合双黑洞的观测特征。

Method: 建立兼顾第三体扰动与后牛顿效应的广义动力学框架，按四极阶、共面八极阶和空间八极阶配置，分别分析共振捕获、共振逃逸以及偏心率与倾角的激发过程。

Result: 四极阶下，倾角激发由绝热捕获进入四极阶共振驱动，随后的共振逃逸受相空间面积守恒支配；共面八极情形下，拱线进动共振触发显著偏心率增长，其捕获概率依赖初始条件；空间八极情形下呈现两阶段演化路径，先由四极阶共振激发倾角，再由逆 von Zeipel-Lidov-Kozai（ZLK）共振驱动偏心率增长。

Conclusion: 这些共振驱动动力学可能在周围恒星族群上留下可观测的运动学印记，从而为并合双黑洞提供一种新的间接探测手段。

Abstract: Identifying the formation channels of merging compact-object binaries remains a fundamental challenge in gravitational-wave (GW) astrophysics. Here, we formulate a generalized framework incorporating both third-body perturbations and post-Newtonian effects to investigate the resonance-driven excitation of eccentricity and/or inclination for stellar orbits around merging black-hole binaries (BHBs). We show that at the quadrupole level, inclination excitation is driven by adiabatic capture into a quadrupole-order resonance, with subsequent resonance escape governed by the conservation of phase-space area. Furthermore, in the coplanar octupole regime, an apsidal precession resonance triggers robust eccentricity growth, where the capture probability depends on the initial conditions. For spatial octupole configurations, we reveal a distinct two-stage evolutionary pathway: the quadrupole-order resonance first excites the orbital inclination, followed by subsequent eccentricity growth driven by the inverse von Zeipel-Lidov-Kozai (ZLK) resonance. Our results indicate that these resonance-driven dynamics could imprint observable kinematic signatures on surrounding stellar populations, thereby offering a novel indirect probe for merging BHBs.

</details>


### [105] [Early NIR echo and the time variable mass loss history of Type IIn SN 2024kgi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25233)
*Naveen Dukiya,Kuntal Misra,Andrea Pastorello,Andrea Reguitti,Anjasha Gangopadhyay,Aravind P. Ravi,Brian Hsu,Noah Franz,David J. Sand,Nathan Smith,Jennifer E. Andrews,Moira Andrews,K. Azalee Bostroem,Collin Christy,Monalisa Dubey,Joseph R. Farah,Archana Gupta,Estefania Padilla Gonzalez,Emily Hoang,D. Andrew Howell,Curtis McCully,Darshana Mehta,Megan Newsome,Jeniveve Pearson,Nicolas Meza Retamal,Jeonghee Rho,Manisha Shrestha,Bhagya Subrayan,Giacomo Terreran,Lina Tomasella,Stefano Valenti,Giorgio Valerin,Kathryn Wynn*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 对IIn型超新星SN 2024kgi进行了长期测光与光谱监测分析，通过半解析光变曲线模型估计其星周介质（CSM）质量与密度结构，并揭示了尘埃形成与双星相互作用引发的质量损失机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IIn型超新星的光变与光谱演化由星周介质相互作用主导，但CSM的质量、密度分布、尘埃形成时标及其起源仍缺乏系统约束。本文旨在通过对SN 2024kgi的多波段长期观测与建模，厘清CSM性质、抛射物特征和尘埃形成过程。

Method: 开展长期测光与光谱监测；测量峰值r波段绝对星等与热辐射光度；构建适用于CSM相互作用瞬变源的半解析光变曲线模型，引入随时间变化的扩散时标；拟合CSM质量与密度剖面；分析H、He、Ca II三重线等抛射物特征及红翼流量缺失；结合NIR波段数据区分已有尘埃与新生尘埃的贡献。

Result: 峰值r波段绝对星等为-19.81±0.06 mag，热辐射光度约2×10^43 erg/s；光变曲线在第312天出现拐折，衰减率由t^-1.0转为t^-4.5。拟合得CSM质量为2.34^{+11.8}_{-1.6} M_sun，密度剖面ρ_csm ∝ r^-2.74，表明质量损失随时间递增。抛射物特征（宽H、He和Ca II三重线）在第80天出现，早于球对称CSM预期，暗示CSM存在不对称性。拐折后H线红翼出现波长依赖的流量缺失，指示抛射物和/或激波后气体中新生尘埃形成。第40天起观测到NIR辐射超出。

Conclusion: 拐折前的早期NIR超出仅归因于已有尘埃的NIR回响，无其他新生尘埃证据；晚期NIR超出则同时来自已有尘埃和新生尘埃。质量损失持续增加、CSM速度较慢且CSM不对称，支持其由双星相互作用诱导的质量损失机制。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive analysis of the long-term photometric and spectroscopic monitoring campaign of the Type IIn Supernova (SN) 2024kgi. The SN reaches at peak an $r$-band absolute magnitude of $-19.81 \pm 0.06$ mag and a bolometric luminosity of $\sim 2 \times 10^{43}$ erg s$^{-1}$. We observe a break in the lightcurve at day $\sim 312$, after which the luminosity decline changes from $t^{-1.0}$ to $t^{-4.5}$, likely due to the shock sweeping the dense CSM. We introduce a semi-analytical lightcurve modeling approach for CSM-interaction in transients that accounts for variable diffusion time. We hence estimate a CSM mass of $2.34^{+11.8}_{-1.6} M_{\odot}$ and with a density profile $ρ_{csm} \propto r^{-2.74}$, indicating progressively increasing mass loss toward the SN explosion. The ejecta signatures emerge as broad H, He, and Ca II triplet lines, at day $\sim 80$, much earlier than expected from a spherically symmetric CSM, suggesting asymmetry in the CSM. After the lightcurve break, the H lines exhibit a wavelength-dependent deficit in the red-wing flux, indicating new dust formation in the ejecta and/or in the post-shock gas. We observe an NIR excess from day $\sim 40$ onwards. We attribute the early NIR excess before the lightcurve break solely to the NIR echo from pre-existing dust, as there are no other indications for new dust formation. The late-time NIR excess likely has contributions from both the preexisting and newly formed dust. The steady increase in mass loss, slow CSM velocity, and asymmetric CSM favor a binary interaction-induced mass loss.

</details>


### [106] [A Magnetic Unification of Merging Supermassive Black Hole Binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25260)
*Mark J. Avara,David O'Neill,Zoltán Haiman*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 提出一种新型二维伪牛顿引力框架，通过引入随机粘性、相对论拱线进动和磁场效应的物理化描述，可在低计算成本下复现三维GRMHD关键行为，并揭示超大质量双黑洞并合电磁信号主要受磁场强度调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超大质量双黑洞（SMBHB）在含气环境中并合的电磁暂现信号是多信使天文学的重要目标。此前二维模拟预测并合时吸积率与X射线光度骤降后缓慢恢复，而三维GRMHD/MHD模拟仅显示温和下降且恢复迅速，二者存在显著分歧，且三维模拟计算代价高昂。

Method: 构建二维伪牛顿引力框架，加入物理驱动的随机粘性、相对论拱线进动以及大尺度磁场的参数化描述，并对其进行校准以匹配三维GRMHD模拟的关键特征，从而在避免三维GRMHD高成本的前提下研究双星吸积。

Result: 并合信号对磁场强度最为敏感：磁场越强，并合时吸积率下降越不显著。并合后演化由磁化程度与轨道气体的相对论进动共同决定，吸积恢复迅速。低磁化盘中恢复由自相交激波驱动，并合时的变暗之后还伴随第二次明显的亮度下降。大尺度磁场增强导致磁阻吸积盘（MAD）行为，使其在并合前更早、并合后更久地扰动双星系统。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了嵌入共面SMBHB并合的电磁信号特征，并为约束其内禀磁化强度提供了新的观测判据。

Abstract: The detection of an electromagnetic (EM) transient from a supermassive black hole binary (SMBHB) merging within a gaseous environment will constitute a multi-messenger milestone. Numerical simulations of these systems have mostly been performed in 2D with a constant viscosity parameter representing turbulent and magnetic stresses. These simulations predict a steep drop in accretion rate and X-ray luminosity at merger, followed by a slow recovery. However, 3D-GRMHD and 3D-MHD simulations have shown only a modest reduction in accretion rate and a fast post-merger recovery. We present a new 2D pseudo-Newtonian framework with physically motivated prescriptions for stochastic viscosity, relativistic apsidal precession, and large-scale magnetic fields. When suitably calibrated, this 2D framework accurately captures key 3D-GRMHD behavior, demonstrating that it can be used to explore binary accretion without the high computational cost of 3D-GRMHD simulations. We find that the merger signatures are most sensitive to the strength of the magnetic field, with a drop in the accretion rate at merger progressively less pronounced for stronger fields. Post-merger evolution is determined by both magnetization and relativistic precession of orbiting gas, yielding rapid recovery of accretion. For low-magnetization disks, recovery is driven by self-intersection shocks, and the dip at merger is followed by a distinct second dimming. Large-scale magnetic fields of increasing magnitude lead to magnetically arrested disk (MAD) behavior which disturbs the merging system progressively earlier before merger, and longer after. Our results unify the EM signatures of the merger of embedded coplanar SMBHBs, and provide novel observational constraints on their intrinsic magnetization.

</details>


### [107] [Multi-scale variability in the optical afterglow of GRB 251013C from high-cadence LAST observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25342)
*R. Konno,E. O. Ofek,S. Garrappa,O. Teboul,E. Waxman,S. Ben-Ami,D. Kovaleva,A. Krassilchtchikov,D. Polishook,E. Segre,E. A. Zimmerman*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用LAST高频光学观测结合Swift-XRT数据，揭示GRB 251013C光学余辉存在多时标变化，并以刷新外激波与慢冷却同步辐射解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学余辉常被描述为平滑幂律衰减，但耀发与再增亮等偏离携带重要辐射机制信息；GRB 251013C的高频观测为检验这些变化成分的起源提供了契机。

Method: 使用LAST连续光学观测，辅以Swift-XRT公开X射线数据；以叠加变化成分的幂律衰减拟合光学光变曲线，并用同时段光-X射线测量约束宽波段能谱。

Result: 光学光变呈现多时标显著变化：宽时标再增亮（Δt~t）叠加更快涨落（Δt/t<1）；主再增亮上升结构有明显峰前陡化；快变呈非对称快升慢降且持续时间随峰值时间增加；光学通量位于X射线谱外推上，X射线带内与光-X射线测得的谱硬化程度一致。

Conclusion: 主再增亮支持刷新外激波解释；宽波段能谱与峰后衰减符合风介质中慢冷却同步辐射；谱硬化要求新主导的更硬电子群，更快光学变化指示刷新喷出物或激波区内部结构。

Abstract: Context. Optical afterglows of gamma-ray bursts (GRBs) are often described by smooth power-law decays, but deviations such as flares and rebrightenings provide important constraints on the underlying emission processes. Aims. We investigate the temporal variability of the optical afterglow of GRB 251013C using high-cadence observations, and assess the origin of the observed variability. Methods. We analyze continuous optical observations obtained with the Large Array Survey Telescope (LAST), complemented by publicly available X-ray data from Swift-XRT. The optical light curve is modeled using a power-law decay with superposed variability components, and contemporaneous optical and X-ray measurements are used to constrain the broadband spectrum. Results. The optical light curve shows pronounced variability on multiple timescales, including broad rebrightening episodes with $Δt \sim t$ and superposed faster fluctuations with $Δt/t < 1$. The main rebrightening exhibits a structured rise with a clear steepening prior to the peak. The fast variability comprises asymmetric fast-rise, slow-decay features whose durations increase with peak time. The optical flux lies on the extrapolation of the X-ray spectrum, and the spectrum hardens by the same amount whether measured within the X-ray band or from optical to X-ray. Conclusions. We favor a refreshed external-shock interpretation for the main rebrightening. The broadband spectrum and post-peak decay are consistent with slow-cooling synchrotron emission in a wind-like medium. The spectral hardening requires a newly dominant, harder electron population, while the faster optical variability indicates structure within the refreshed ejecta or shocked region.

</details>


### [108] [A Multi-Mission Detection Framework for High-Energy Transients: Application to a 13-year Fermi/GBM and Swift/BAT Sample](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25672)
*Ariel Perera,Barak Zackay,Tejaswi Venumadhav*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 提出多探测器联合触发框架，利用Fermi/GBM与Swift/BAT档案数据联合搜索，可提升暗弱高能暂现源探测灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗弱高能暂现源（如双中子星并合的电磁对应体、高能中微子事件等）探测对时域与多信使天文学日益重要，但单台仪器的探测阈值限制了灵敏度，大量暗弱暂现源无法被单任务分析探测到。

Method: 构建联合探测框架：综合多个探测器的触发信息，基于各探测器信噪比给出候选事件的联合关联概率 p_joint；并将其应用于2013—2025年Fermi/GBM与Swift/BAT的计数率档案数据。

Result: 该方法能有效区分真实符合信号与偶然背景关联，抑制不一致的符合事件，同时提升两台仪器共同观测到的暗弱事件的显著性；在最优条件下，双任务搜索可使短伽马暴的可及体积最多增加60%。

Conclusion: 该框架为广义多任务联合搜索提供了实用基础，有助于更灵敏地探索暗弱高能暂现源天区，并提升未来多信使发现的潜力。

Abstract: The detection of faint high-energy transients is becoming increasingly important in time-domain and multi-messenger astronomy, particularly for identifying electromagnetic counterparts to binary neutron star mergers, high-energy neutrino events, and other weak transient phenomena. At present, the sensitivity of gamma-ray and hard X-ray searches is often limited by the detection threshold of individual instruments, leaving a population of faint transients inaccessible to single-mission analyses. To address this limitation, we develop a joint detection framework that combines trigger information from multiple detectors and assigns each candidate a joint association probability, $p_{\text{joint}}$, as a function of the individual detector signal-to-noise ratios. We apply this framework to archival Fermi/GBM and Swift/BAT rates data from 2013-2025. The method effectively separates coincident signals from accidental background associations, suppressing inconsistent coincidences while enhancing the significance of faint events observed by both instruments. We find that, under optimal conditions, a two-mission search can increase the accessible volume for short gamma-ray bursts by up to $60\%$. This framework provides a practical foundation for generalized multi-mission searches, enabling more sensitive exploration of the faint high-energy transient sky and improving the prospects for future multi-messenger discoveries.

</details>


### [109] [A Brief Review on the Statistical Properties of Fast Radio Bursts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25810)
*Yu Sang,Hai-Nan Lin*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文综述了快速射电暴（FRB）的统计特性，涵盖色散量、红移、能量/光度函数、重复率、宿主星系分布，以及重复暴的能量、通量、等待时间统计与时间序列分析（记忆效应、混沌动力学）、周期活动和偏振性质，并强调观测偏差与选择效应的考量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FRB的辐射机制和祖天体尚未明确，而快速增长样本的统计分析是约束其物理本质的有效途径，因此需要系统整合现有目录的统计结果。

Method: 综述性分析：综合当前FRB目录的统计性质；对重复暴进行能量、通量/流量、等待时间统计及时间序列分析；讨论周期活动的观测发现与理论解释；调查偏振性质以探测磁离子环境。同时纳入观测偏差和选择效应的修正。

Result: 汇总了色散量、红移、能量/光度函数、重复率和宿主星系统计的约束；揭示了重复暴的记忆效应与混沌动力学特征；梳理了周期活动和偏振性质的观测与理论进展。

Conclusion: 稳健的经验趋势为理解FRB物理起源提供约束，但观测偏差和选择效应需谨慎处理；FRB的物理起源仍是待解决的开放问题。

Abstract: Fast radio bursts (FRBs) are millisecond-duration radio transients of extragalactic origin, whose emission mechanisms and progenitors remain unresolved. Statistical analyses of the rapidly growing samples provide a promising means to constrain the underlying physics. In this review, we synthesize the statistical properties derived from current FRB catalogs, encompassing constraints on dispersion measure, redshift, energy/luminosity functions, repetition rate, and host-galaxy demographics. For repeating FRBs, we examine the statistics of energy, fluence/flux, waiting time, and discuss time-series analyses that reveal memory effects and chaotic dynamics. We further discuss the observational discovery and theoretical interpretation of periodic activity, and survey polarization properties that probe the magneto-ionic environments of FRB sources. Throughout, we emphasize robust empirical trends, account for observational biases and selection effects, and outline outstanding open questions regarding the physical origin of these enigmatic bursts.

</details>


### [110] [Kink instability as a particle acceleration mechanism in X-ray binaries and the connection to the outburst cycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25842)
*Emma L. Elley,James H. Matthews,Alex J. Cooper,Fraser J. Cowie,Rob Fender*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文考察扭曲不稳定性(kink instability)能否解释硬态"致密"X射线双星喷流的粒子加速。作者用一维模型评估不稳定性增长率,并将模拟结果标度以估计磁能耗散率,同时考虑辐射冷却效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬态"致密"X射线双星喷流中粒子加速的物理机制尚不明确,作者欲检验扭曲不稳定性是否可作为该加速过程的解释。

Method: 采用一维模型计算多种喷流模型下扭曲不稳定性的预期增长率;将模拟结果标度以估算喷流中磁能的耗散率;并分析辐射冷却对该系统的影响。

Result: 在合理参数空间内,硬态"致密"X射线双星喷流在其加速区内很可能对扭曲不稳定性不稳定。视模型参数而定,喷流可在多种长度尺度上辐射,从对应"失败"喷流情形的尺度到与已分辨的"致密"喷流射电观测相符的尺度。

Conclusion: 扭曲不稳定性可合理解释硬态"致密"X射线双星喷流的粒子加速,并能涵盖从"失败"喷流到可分辨"致密"喷流的观测特征;文中还讨论了该模型在整个爆发周期中的预期演化。

Abstract: We consider whether the kink instability can explain particle acceleration in hard state 'compact' X-ray binary jets. We apply a 1D model to assess the expected growth rate of the instability for a variety of jet models, then scale results from simulations to estimate a dissipation rate for the magnetic energy in the jet. We then consider the effects of radiative cooling on the system. We find that hard state 'compact' X-ray binary jets are likely to be unstable to the kink instability within their acceleration zones across a plausible parameter space. We further find that depending on the parameters of the model, the jets may radiate over a variety of length scales ranging from length scales attributable to a 'failed' jet scenario, to those in keeping with resolved radio observations of 'compact' jets. We discuss the expected evolution of this model through the outburst cycle.

</details>


### [111] [IXPE observation of the new magnetar source EP J223759.5+531421](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25958)
*Roberto Taverna,Lorenzo Marra,Roberto Turolla,Ruth M. E. Kelly,Silvia Zane,Alice Borghese,Francesco Coti Zelati,Paolo Esposito,Gian Luca Israel,Nanda Rea,Haonan Yang,Yilong Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: IXPE对银河系新磁星EP J223759.5+531421的观测探测到约7%的相位能量积分偏振度，两个热辐射成分偏振特性差异显著，热成分在4-7.5 keV脉冲次峰处偏振度可达约63%，偏振角随自转周期振荡且符合旋转矢量模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁星是强磁场中子星，其表面辐射几何与磁场构型尚不清楚。本文旨在利用X射线偏振测量，约束新发现磁星EP J223759.5+531421的表面热辐射区域分布、磁场轴与视线几何，检验是否存在磁凝聚态与大气层等不同辐射机制。

Method: Einstein Probe宽视场X射线望远镜于2026年6月28日发现该磁星后，IXPE于2026年7月6日开始跟踪观测。在2-7.5 keV能段测量吸收后流量约4.5×10^-11 erg cm^-2 s^-1，拟合出温度约0.5 keV与约1 keV的两个热辐射成分。分析相位与能量积分的偏振度、偏振角，并分能段、分相位折叠分析两成分偏振差异，用旋转矢量模型拟合偏振角随自转周期的变化。

Result: 探测到置信度大于4σ、相位与能量积分的偏振度约7%，偏振角约-2°（天北极以东）。两个热成分偏振特性差异明显，较热的成分偏振更强。相位折叠数据中差异更大：4-7.5 keV能段偏振度可达约63%，对应脉冲轮廓次峰，而低能段不超过约30%。偏振角在一个自转周期内从-90°连续振荡到+90°，可被旋转矢量模型很好拟合，得到视线与磁偶极轴相对自转轴的倾角分别约28°和约109°。

Conclusion: 结果表明该磁星具有特定辐射几何：较冷热成分可能来自两个对跖的热斑，或处于磁凝聚态，而较热成分来自一个被大气层覆盖的较小区域。X射线偏振为揭示磁星表面辐射区结构与磁场几何提供了有效手段。

Abstract: We report on the detection of polarized X-ray emission from the new Galactic magnetar EP J223759.5+531421, discovered by the Einstein Probe Wide-field X-ray Telescope on 2026 June 28. The Imaging X-ray Polarimetry Explorer (IXPE) follow-up observation started on 2026 July 6 and detected the source at a (absorbed) flux of $\approx 4.5\times 10^{-11} \, \mathrm{ erg\,cm^2\,s}^{-1}$ ($2$--$7.5\, \mathrm{keV}$ range), confirming the presence of two thermally emitting regions on the star surface, with temperatures $\approx 0.5$ and $\approx 1\, \mathrm{keV}$. A significant (at $> 4σ$ confidence level) phase- and energy-integrated polarization degree of $\approx 7\%$ was detected with the polarization angle $\approx -2^\circ$ East of the celestial North. The two thermal components exhibit quite different polarization properties, with the hotter one being more polarized. The difference is larger in the phase-folded data, with the polarization degree reaching $\sim 63\%$ in the range $4$--$7.5\,\mathrm{keV}$, in correspondence with the secondary peak of the pulse profile, and never exceeding $\sim 30\%$ at lower energies. The polarization angle continuously oscillates from $-90^\circ$ to $+90^\circ$ over one rotational cycle and is well fit by the rotating vector model, with an inclination of the line of sight and of the magnetic dipole axis relative to the star spin axis of $\approx 28^\circ$ and $\approx 109^\circ$, respectively. The data point to an emission geometry in which the cold thermal component likely originates from two antipodal caps, possibly in a magnetically condensed state, while the hotter one comes from a smaller region covered by an atmosphere.

</details>


### [112] [Einstein Probe discovery of the magnetar EP J223759.5+531421](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25990)
*N. Rea,F. Coti Zelati,A. Borghese,Y. L. Wang,H. Yang,X. Mao,C. -Y. Dai,E. Arrigoni,J. Bai,G. Bernardi,M. Burgay,S. Cao,A. Coleiro,D. De Grandis,P. Esposito,H. Feng,Y. -C. Fu,A. Geminardi,D. Götz,S. Guillot,Y. Huang,M. Imbrogno,G. L. Israel,C. Jin,A. Kong,Y. Li,L. Lin,C. -S. Liu,C. Maitra,A. Marino,S. Mereghetti,J. -U. Ness,S. Ng,M. Pilia,A. Possenti,H. Sun,L. Tao,R. Taverna,A. Tiengo,R. Turolla,W. Yuan,S. Zane,B. Zhang,L. -X. Zhang,Z. Zhang,W. -W. Zhu,Y. -C. Zou*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本研究报告了由爱因斯坦探针宽视场X射线望远镜于2026年6月28日发现的新银河系磁星EP J223759.5+531421及其早期爆发演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用爱因斯坦探针WXT监测能力，从最早观测阶段揭示此前处于宁静态的银河系磁星及其爆发过程。

Method: 通过EP、XMM-Newton、NuSTAR、SVOM和IXPE进行后续观测，分析相干X射线脉冲、周期导数、能谱、脉冲 fraction 及多波段对应体。

Result: 探测到P~6 s、Pdot~2.8×10^-12 s/s的脉冲，B_dip~2.6×10^14 G、tau_c~34 kyr；0.5-30 keV光度首月由1.2×10^35降至5.7×10^34 erg/s；未探测到近红外或射电对应体；短X射线暴独立确认其磁星性质。

Conclusion: EP WXT监测具备揭示此前宁静银河系磁星并从最早期阶段跟踪其爆发的潜力。

Abstract: We report the discovery and early outburst evolution of the new Galactic magnetar EP J223759.5+531421, detected by the Einstein Probe Wide-field X-ray Telescope on 2026 June 28. Follow-up observations with Einstein Probe, XMM--Newton, NuSTAR, SVOM, and IXPE revealed coherent X-ray pulsations at P ~ 6s and an average period derivative of Pdot ~ 2.8x10^{-12} s/s. These values imply a nominal polar dipolar magnetic field of B_dip ~ 2.6x10^{14} Gauss and a characteristic age of tau_c ~ 34 kyr, although the structured timing residuals suggest possible torque variability. The rms pulsed fraction is approximately 20-25% below ~7 keV and decreases at higher energies. The broadband spectra require multiple thermal components and a hard power-law tail. Adopting a distance of 3.3 kpc, the 0.5-30 keV luminosity declined from 1.2x10^{35} to 5.7x10^{34} erg/s during the first month, accompanied by a decrease in the inferred thermal-emitting areas. At this distance, the Galactic latitude b=-4.56 deg corresponds to a height of approximately 0.26 kpc below the Galactic plane, which is difficult to reconcile with the nominal characteristic age under a simple midplane-birth scenario even for an extreme proper motion velocity. Short X-ray bursts independently confirm the magnetar nature of the source. No near-infrared or radio counterpart were detected by GTC, Medicina or FAST, respectively, down to K_s>21 mag and S_{1.25 GHz} <2.3 microJy. These observations demonstrate the potential of the Einstein Probe WXT monitoring to uncover previously quiescent Galactic magnetars and follow their outbursts from their earliest observed stages.

</details>


### [113] [KPolaris: GPU-accelerated Polarized Radiative Transfer in General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26298)
*Zelin Zhang,Bin Chen*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: KPolaris：基于Kokkos的GPU加速广义相对论偏振辐射转移开源代码，支持解析与GRMHD等离子体模型、快光/慢光输运，并提供诊断工具，经收敛与交叉验证，GPU加速促进多频与含时偏振黑洞成像研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏振黑洞图像同时编码了磁化等离子体与辐射传播所经弯曲时空的信息，需要能够同时处理两者并支持高效计算的偏振辐射转移工具。

Method: 提出开源GPU加速的广义相对论偏振辐射转移代码KPolaris，基于Kokkos框架构建；支持解析等离子体模型与GRMHD模拟数据，兼容快光与慢光辐射转移；提供发射率加权等离子体图、选定区域发射贡献、选定光线历史、Stokes参数对等离子体参数的敏感性等诊断；通过数值收敛测试并与独立辐射转移代码对比验证精度。

Result: 数值收敛测试与独立辐射转移代码的对比验证了计算精度；GPU加速显著降低了图像生成的计算成本。

Conclusion: KPolaris为偏振黑洞图像的多频与含时研究提供了高效、可验证的通用工具。

Abstract: Polarized black-hole images carry information about both the magnetized plasma and the curved spacetime through which the radiation propagates. We present KPolaris, an open-source GPU-accelerated general-relativistic polarized radiative-transfer code built on the Kokkos framework. It supports analytic plasma models and GRMHD simulation data, with both fast-light and slow-light radiative transfer. The code also provides diagnostics that connect image structure to the emitting plasma and to propagation effects along the rays. These include emissivity-weighted plasma maps, emission contributions from selected regions, selected-ray histories, and sensitivities of the Stokes parameters to plasma parameters. Numerical convergence tests and comparisons with an independent radiative-transfer code assess the accuracy of the calculations. GPU acceleration reduces the computational cost of image generation, facilitating multi-frequency and time-dependent studies of polarized black-hole images.

</details>


### [114] [Dense Dump Codec: Error-Controlled, Random-Access Compression of GRMHD Time Series for Slow-Light Radiative Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26317)
*Zelin Zhang,Zhenyu Zhang,Bin Chen*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出稠密倾泻编解码器（Dense Dump Codec, DDC），一种针对稠密GRMHD时间序列的压缩方法，通过存储精确锚点状态并紧凑表示中间演化，在支持对任意时刻和变量直接访问的同时，将数据体积缩小约10倍，并显著降低慢光辐射转移的Stokes图像误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞影像研究依赖对吸积等离子体随时间演化的模拟。慢光辐射转移要求模拟输出时间间隔足够密集（因为光在传播过程中等离子体状态会发生变化），然而完整保存密集输出代价高昂，稀疏采样又会引入时间插值误差。因此需要一种兼顾高时间分辨率与存储效率的方案。

Method: DDC存储精确的锚点（anchor）状态，并对锚点之间的中间演化进行紧凑编码表示，同时保留对选定时刻和变量的直接访问能力。作者将DDC应用于8组生产序列（涵盖SANE与MAD模拟、多种黑洞自旋、0.1M输出），并将原生DDC读取器集成进偏振慢光辐射转移流程中。

Result: DDC归档体积约为原始稠密数据的1/10，甚至比原始0.5M输出的总量还小约50%。在86、230、345 GHz的测试中，相比使用原始0.5M输入的慢光计算，DDC可将聚合Stokes图像误差降低54%–79%。

Conclusion: DDC使高时间步频（high-cadence）的慢光计算成为可能，无需付出完整保存每个稠密GRMHD状态的高昂存储代价。

Abstract: Black-hole movies provide a unique venue to study the time evolution of accreting plasma. Modeling this evolution with slow-light radiative transfer requires closely spaced simulation outputs, because the plasma changes as light propagates through it. Saving these outputs in full is costly, whereas sparse sampling introduces time-interpolation errors. We present the Dense Dump Codec (DDC), a compression method for dense GRMHD time series. DDC stores exact anchor states and compactly represents the intermediate evolution, while allowing direct access to selected times and variables. Applied to eight production sequences spanning SANE and MAD simulations at several black-hole spins with 0.1M output, DDC archives are about 10 times smaller than the original dense data and even about 50% smaller than the original 0.5M output in aggregate. We further integrate a native DDC reader into polarized slow-light radiative transfer. Tests at 86, 230, and 345 GHz show that DDC may reduce aggregate Stokes-image errors by 54-79% relative to slow-light calculations using original 0.5M input. These results indicate that DDC enables high-cadence slow-light calculations without the prohibitive cost of saving every dense GRMHD state in its original form.

</details>


### [115] [Inelastic scattering effects on polarization predictions for cold and warm atmospheres in the X-rays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26380)
*J. Podgorný,L. Marra,M. Dovčiak,R. Taverna,R. W. Goosmann,M. Gupta,F. Marin,G. Matt,J. Poutanen,A. Różańska,A. Veledina*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文考察非弹性康普顿散射对X射线偏振模型的影响，采用蒙特卡洛方法模拟冷电子与暖电子（≤1 keV）均匀平板中的散射过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有吸积致密天体的X射线偏振模型通常沿用Chandrasekhar-Sobolev的弹性汤姆孙散射偏振假设，忽略了非弹性康普顿散射的效应。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛方法，模拟均匀冷电子与暖电子（最高1 keV）平板中的散射，覆盖平面平行透射与反射两种几何。

Result: 给出了弹性与非弹性散射假设之间在X射线波段产生的偏振差异，涵盖透射和反射几何。

Conclusion: 非弹性康普顿散射导致的偏振差异会影响对吸积致密天体X射线偏振观测的解读。

Abstract: X-ray polarization models of accreting compact objects often assume the so-called Chandrasekhar-Sobolev polarization prescription for elastic Thomson scattering in electron-scattering semi-infinite atmospheres. In this letter, we examine the effects of inelastic Compton scattering. We assume homogeneous slabs of cold and warm (up to 1 keV) electrons. We use a Monte Carlo approach. We discuss the polarization difference arising in the X-rays between the elastic and inelastic scattering assumptions for plane-parallel transmission and reflection, and the role it can play in the interpretation of X-ray polarimetric observations of accreting compact objects.

</details>


### [116] [Neutrino Astronomy at High Energies - An Experimental Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26436)
*Christian Spiering*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 高能中微子天文学是新兴领域，十余年前探测到TeV–PeV弥散地外中微子通量，并初步识别出个别源，标志新观测窗口的开启；本文综述其发展历程、现状与未来趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能中微子天文学仍处于起步阶段，相关探测成果出现时间短，亟需系统梳理该领域的进展与前景，以明确发展方向。

Method: 综述性研究，以时间脉络为主线，回顾实现突破所需巨型探测器四十年的研制历程，并结合当前领域状态进行总结与展望。

Result: 确认TeV–PeV能段弥散地外中微子通量的探测以及首批个别源的识别，标志经过四十年努力建成的大型装置带来了观测上的突破。

Conclusion: 高能中微子天文学已开启观测宇宙的新窗口，但目前仍处初期；文章在总结现状的基础上给出了未来发展的设想。

Abstract: Neutrino astronomy at high energies is an emerging field but still in a state of infancy. It is barely a dozen years ago that a diffuse flux of extraterrestrial neutrinos with energies in the TeV and PeV ranges was detected and even fewer that first individual sources could be identified. A new window to the universe has been opened after four decades of efforts to realize the gigantic instruments that made that breakthrough possible. This review describes the road towards the present, reviews the actual status of the field and sketches future developments.

</details>


### [117] [Self-consistent modeling of energy-dependent synchrotron polarization in blazars with application to Mrk 421](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26574)
*Frederike Apel,Motseothata Tisang,Victor Barbosa Martins,Xavier Rodrigues,Markus Böttcher,Anna Franckowiak*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过扩展AM3模拟代码，自洽计入电子同步辐射冷却与俯仰角依赖，建立含时喷流激波模型，解释耀变体的色偏振现象，无需能量分层喷流假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耀变体X射线偏振度常显著高于光学波段，这种色偏振通常归因于能量分层喷流；然而当X射线由电子同步辐射主导时，磁力线与电子速度间的俯仰角亦可能影响偏振度的频率依赖，但该效应在主流模型中被普遍忽略。

Method: 扩展数值模拟代码AM3，引入电子同步辐射发射的自洽计算，构建俯仰角依赖的电子同步辐射冷却，模拟多种磁场结构（如螺旋场），并将模型应用于Mrk 421与观测对比；同时提出含时喷流激波模型以研究频率依赖的偏振角摆动。

Result: 不同磁场结构产生不同的能量依赖偏振特征；Mrk 421数据可由喷流中的螺旋磁场解释；在喷流激波情形下，X射线偏振角变化约70度，而光学/红外偏振角几乎保持不变。

Conclusion: 对电子时间演化进行自洽处理是建模耀变体偏振同步辐射的关键，可自然解释色偏振特征，无需借助能量分层喷流。

Abstract: Observations of black hole jets closely aligned with our line of sight, known as blazars, have revealed that their X-ray emission can be significantly more polarized than in the optical bands. This chromatic polarization is often attributed to an energy-stratified jet. In blazars with X-ray emission dominated by electron synchrotron, the pitch angle between the magnetic field lines and electron velocity might also affect the frequency dependence of the polarization degree, but this effect is generally neglected in state-of-the-art models. In this work, we model the polarization and spectral energy distribution of blazars, accounting for the system's temporal evolution and pitch-angle-dependent electron synchrotron cooling. Our analysis investigates the impact of the magnetic field structure and electron distribution on polarization and tests whether this can help explain its observed frequency dependence. By extending the numerical simulation code AM3, we incorporate a self-consistent calculation of electron synchrotron emission and simulate various magnetic-field structures. We apply these models to Mrk 421 and compare them with observations. Finally, we present a time-dependent shock-in-jet model to investigate frequency-dependent polarization angle swings. Our results show how different magnetic-field structures affect energy-dependent polarization signatures. In the case of Mrk 421, the data can be explained by a helical field in the jet. In the shock-in-jet scenario, the X-ray polarization angle changes by 70 degrees, while remaining almost constant in the optical/infrared. Our results demonstrate that a self-consistent treatment of the temporal evolution of electrons is essential to model the polarized synchrotron emission from blazars and can naturally explain chromatic polarization signatures without requiring an energy-stratified jet.

</details>


### [118] [Dark-matter relay for ultra-high-energy cosmic rays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26611)
*Mikhail Sekretov*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该文提出暗物质可作为超高频宇宙线（UHECR）的“接力”媒介：暗物质粒子在源附近被UHECR加速，将动量传递给观测者附近的核子，从而解释UHECR与远方blazar的关联，并指出在m_DM ~ 25 MeV铁素暗物质下该效应可观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UHECR的天体物理源尚未确定，即使是最高能量事件也没有确认的近邻宇宙加速器；同时HiRes和Telescope Array发现部分UHECR通量与远方blazar存在令人困惑的关联。作者希望提出一种机制，使UHECR能有效地从远处源传输到观测者。

Method: 提出暗物质接力机制：暗物质粒子在源附近被UHECR加速（boosted），随后将动量传递给观测者附近的核子。由于暗物质—重子散射截面随能量增长，该接力概率不可忽略。通过计算演示该效应，重点考察质量m_DM ~ 25 MeV的铁素暗物质。

Result: 在m_DM ~ 25 MeV的铁素暗物质情形下，该接力效应是可观测的；论文还讨论了该机制的若干可能观测特征。

Conclusion: 暗物质可作为一种有效机制，将UHECR从遥远源传输至观测者附近，从而可能解释UHECR与远方blazar的关联，并为25 MeV量级的铁素暗物质提供可检验的观测signature。

Abstract: Astrophysical sources of ultra-high-energy cosmic rays (UHECRs) remain unidentified, and no nearby cosmic accelerators are established even for the highest-energy events. In addition, puzzling correlations of a fraction of UHECR flux with distant blazars were found by HiRes and, recently, Telescope Array experiments. Here we propose that dark matter can provide a mechanism to effectively transfer UHECRs from distant sources: dark-matter particles are boosted by UHECRs near the source and pass their momentum to baryons close to the observer. The probability of this relay is not negligible because of the growth of dark-matter-baryon cross section with energy. We demonstrate that the effect is observable for fermionic dark matter of mass $m_{\mathrm{DM}} \sim 25$ MeV and discuss possible observational signatures of this mechanism.

</details>


<div id='hep-ex'></div>

# hep-ex [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Search for boosted vector boson scattering $W^{\pm}W^{\pm}H$ production in final states with same-sign leptons and $b$-jets in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}=$13 TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25153)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: ATLAS利用Run 2的140 fb⁻¹数据搜索W±W±Hjj电弱产生过程，未观测到显著超出标准模型的信号，对κ_WW给出95%置信度排除区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该过程对四重WWHH相互作用具有直接敏感性，可用于检验标准模型电弱对称性破缺机制并约束反常规范耦合。

Method: 使用ATLAS探测器在√s=13 TeV下采集的140 fb⁻¹质子-质子对撞数据，选择两个同号轻子、缺失横向动量及大半径喷流（重建为b bbar的Higgs候选）的末态；通过耦合修饰因子κ_WW参数化偏离。

Result: 未观测到显著超出标准模型预期的信号；在95%置信度下对W±W±Hjj产生截面设置上限，排除κ_WW位于观测区间[-2.1, 4.1]（预期区间[-1.9, 3.9]）之外的值。

Conclusion: 在现有数据精度下，W±W±Hjj过程的测量结果与标准模型一致，κ_WW被限制在较宽的允许区间内。

Abstract: A search for the electroweak production of a Higgs boson in association with a same-sign $W$ boson pair and two forward jets ($W^{\pm}W^{\pm}Hjj$) is presented. The full Run 2 proton-proton collision dataset recorded at a center-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV by the ATLAS detector at the Large Hadron Collider is used, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 140 fb$^{-1}$. The search targets events where both $W$ bosons decay leptonically, yielding a final state with two same-sign charged leptons, missing transverse momentum, and a Higgs boson candidate. The high-momentum Higgs-boson candidate decaying into a $b\bar{b}$ pair is reconstructed as a single large-radius jet. This process provides direct sensitivity to the quartic $WWHH$ interaction. Possible deviations from the Standard Model prediction are parameterized through the coupling modifier $κ_{WW}$, where $κ_{WW}=1$ corresponds to the Standard Model. No significant excess over the Standard Model prediction is observed. Upper limits at the 95% confidence level are set on the $W^{\pm}W^{\pm}Hjj$ production cross-section as a function of $κ_{WW}$. Observed (expected) values of $κ_{WW}$ outside the interval $-2.1 < κ_{WW} < 4.1$ ($-1.9 < κ_{WW} < 3.9$) are excluded.

</details>

Zhu,K. Zhu,K. J. Zhu,K. S. Zhu,L. X. Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,J. H. Zou*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于BESIII探测器收集的2712.4±14.3百万ψ(3686)事例，研究ψ(3686)辐射衰变中的ΛΛbar系统，通过模型无关分波分析发现显著的阈增强结构，并观测到新的赝标量共振态η(2600)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究重子-反重子阈动力学以及赝标量轻强子谱，探索ΛΛbar系统在阈附近的产生机制与可能存在的新共振态。

Method: 使用BESIII探测器采集的2712.4±14.3百万ψ(3686)事例，对ψ(3686)辐射衰变产生的ΛΛbar系统进行模型无关的分波分析（partial wave analysis）。

Result: 发现一个显著的阈增强结构，主要由^1S_0（J^{PC}=0^{-+}）和^3P_0（J^{PC}=0^{++}）分波主导；并在^1S_0分波中观测到新的赝标量共振态η(2600)，其质量与先前报道的X(2600)一致，是目前观测到的最重的轻介子。

Conclusion: 该结果深化了对重子-反重子阈动力学和赝标量轻强子谱的理解，并首次观测到最重的轻介子η(2600)（与X(2600)质量一致）。

Abstract: Using $2712.4 \pm 14.3$ million $ψ(3686)$ events collected with the BESIII detector, the $Λ\barΛ$ system produced in $ψ(3686)$ radiative decays is studied. A model-independent partial wave analysis reveals a significant threshold enhancement structure dominated by the $^1S_0$ and $^3P_0$ partial waves, corresponding to $J^{PC} = 0^{-+}$ and $0^{++}$, respectively. In addition, a new pseudoscalar resonance, designated as $η(2600)$, is observed in the $^1S_0$ partial wave with a mass value consistent with the previously reported $X(2600)$ state, which represents the heaviest light meson observed to date. These results enhance our understanding of baryon-antibaryon threshold dynamics and the pseudoscalar light hadron spectroscopy.

</details>

Zhu,X. Zhu,X. Zhu,Y. Zhu,V. Zhukov,J. Zhuo,T. Zies,D. Zuliani,G. Zunica,X. Zuo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 首次在底奇异区发现质量超出夸克模型预言的新共振态，全球显著性超过7倍标准差，为非传统单底强子的首个观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统夸克模型将强子分为介子（夸克-反夸克对）和重子（三个价夸克），但该经典图像无法解释强相互作用的复杂手征动力学及奇异多夸克强子的出现。尽管粲扇区已出现非常规迹象，开放底系统仍是检验这些机制普适性的关键前沿，且此前尚无观测。

Method: 利用欧洲核子研究中心（CERN）大型强子对撞机底物理实验（LHCb）记录的质子-质子对撞数据，在底奇异扇区搜索新共振态，并对观测到的信号进行显著性与性质分析。

Result: 在底奇异扇区观测到一个新共振态，全球显著性超过7倍标准差；其自然宽度较窄，质量相对传统夸克模型预言存在显著亏损。这是非传统单底强子的首次观测。

Conclusion: 该发现为强相互作用的手征动力学以及奇异领域中重夸克自旋对称性提供了关键实验信息，并可能推动底扇区非常规强子谱的系统研究。

Abstract: As the essential building blocks of visible matter, hadrons have traditionally been classified by the quark model as mesons composed of quark-antiquark pairs and baryons built from three valence quarks. Yet, this classical picture does not account for the intricate chiral dynamics of the strong force and the emergence of exotic multi-quark hadrons. While experimental evidence for such unconventional dynamics has surfaced in the charm sector, the open-beauty system remains the long-sought frontier for testing the universality of these mechanisms. Here the observation of a new resonance in the beauty-strange sector with a global significance exceeding seven standard deviations is reported using proton-proton collision data recorded by the Large Hadron Collider beauty (LHCb) experiment at the European Organization for Nuclear Research (CERN). It exhibits a narrow natural width and a substantial mass deficit compared to the conventional quark model predictions. This result marks the first observation of a nonconventional single-beauty hadron, providing crucial insights into the chiral dynamics of the strong interaction and heavy-quark spin symmetry in the exotic domain.

</details>


### [122] [Fast Timing at Colliders: From Scintillator Hodoscopes to MicroPattern Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25358)
*Sebastian N. White*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了对撞机中带电粒子时间测量技术的发展，并以CMS升级所用TOFHIR2C ASIC为例，展示双阈值定时技术在HL-LHC高辐射、高亮度环境下的校准优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对撞机时间测量阵列历经三个时代的演进，其探测器技术受亮度和多重数制约；同时需要在不断变化的运行条件下重新审视信号捕获、处理及校准方法。当前处于高亮度LHC升级时代，幅度游走修正所需时间跨度比目标分辨率大一个数量级，且高辐射环境下运行条件多变，校准不再是"一次性"过程，因此需要更有效的定时与校准方案。

Method: 对带电粒子定时技术的三个时代进行选择性综述，梳理不同信号捕获与处理方法及其对校准的影响；针对HL-LHC时代，采用为CMS升级设计的TOFHIR2C ASIC，将其配置为提供双阈值定时（dual-threshold timing），用以简化幅度游走修正并适应多次校准需求。

Result: 双阈值定时技术能够显著简化对幅度游走的关键修正，该修正通常跨越比目标分辨率大一个数量级的时间范围；TOFHIR2C ASIC具备可配置的双阈值定时能力，可支撑这一适用于新时代的技术。

Conclusion: 双阈值定时是应对HL-LHC高亮度、高辐射环境下带电粒子定时与校准挑战的有效手段，TOFHIR2C ASIC可通过配置实现该能力，体现了适用于新时代定时方案的潜力。

Abstract: We give a select overview of charged particle timing at colliders. Roughly speaking, three eras capture the evolution of timing arrays. While luminosity and multiplicity dictate the detector technologies, there is also a need to review the various approaches to signal capture and processing, and their role in changing conditions for calibration.
  For the current era--the High-Luminosity LHC upgrades--the technique of dual-threshold timing can significantly simplify the crucial correction for amplitude walk.This correction often spans a time range an order of magnitude larger than the target resolution. Furthermore, because operating conditions vary in the high radiation environment, calibration can no longer be thought of as a one-time procedure.
  The TOFHIR2C ASIC designed for a CMS upgrade can be configured to provide dual threshold timing. In this report, we use this capability to demonstrate the power of this technique suitable for our new era.}

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: LHCb利用Run 2数据测量Ω_b^-与Ξ_b^-寿命比r_τ=1.109±0.055±0.010，并给出Ω_b^-寿命为1.751±0.089±0.022 ps，精度较此前世界平均值提高约两倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重夸克展开（HQE）框架对重子寿命比有理论预言，而实验上Ω_b^-寿命的测量精度有限。精确测量Ω_b^-与Ξ_b^-的寿命比可检验理论预言，并改进Ω_b^-寿命的世界平均值。

Method: 使用LHCb实验在LHC Run 2（2015–2018）采集的对应积分亮度6 fb^-1的pp碰撞数据。在两组衰变模式中测量r_τ：(Ω_b^-,Ξ_b^-)→(Ω_c^0π^-,Ξ_c^0π^-)与(Ω_b^-,Ξ_b^-)→(J/ψΩ^-, J/ψΞ^-)，其中(Ω_c^0,Ξ_c^0)→pK^-K^-π^+，(Ω^-,Ξ^-)→(Λ^0K^-,Λ^0π^-)，Λ^0→pπ^-，J/ψ→μ^+μ^-。将测量结果平均并与Run 1（2011–2012）同衰变模式的测量组合。

Result: 组合得到r_τ=1.109±0.055±0.010（统计误差与系统误差）。乘以已知的Ξ_b^-寿命，得到Ω_b^-寿命τ_{Ω_b^-}=1.751±0.089±0.022 ps。

Conclusion: 该测量将Ω_b^-寿命的精度较此前世界平均值提高约两倍，且r_τ值同重夸克展开框架的最新理论预言一致。

Abstract: The lifetime ratio ${r_τ\equivτ_{Ω_b^-}/τ_{Ξ_b^-}}$ between the ${Ω_b^-}$ and ${Ξ_b^-}$ baryons is measured using a sample of $pp$ collision data corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 6 fb$^{-1}$ and collected by the LHCb experiment during LHC Run 2 (2015$-$2018). The ratio $r_τ$ is measured in two sets of decays modes, ${ {(Ω_b^-,Ξ_b^-)\to(Ω_c^0π^-,Ξ_c^0π^-)}}$ and ${(Ω_b^-,Ξ_b^-)\to(J/ψΩ^-, J/ψΞ^-)}$, with ${(Ω_c^0,Ξ_c^0)\to pK^-K^-π^+}$, ${(Ω^-,Ξ^-)\to(Λ^0 K^-,Λ^0π^-)}$, ${Λ^0\to pπ^-}$ and $J/ψ\toμ^+μ^-$. The measured $r_τ$ values are averaged and combined with Run 1 (2011$-$2012) measurements in the same decay modes to obtain ${r_τ = 1.109\pm0.055\pm0.010}$. Multiplying by the known ${Ξ_b^-}$ lifetime results in the ${Ω_b^-}$ lifetime ${τ_{Ω_b^-} = 1.751\pm0.089\pm0.022~{\rm ps}}$, where the uncertainties are statistical and systematic. This measurement improves on the precision of the $Ω_b^-$ lifetime by about a factor of two over the previous world average. The value of $r_τ$ is in agreement with the most recent theoretical predictions from the heavy quark expansion framework.

</details>


### [124] [Spin-spin correlations in $Λ\barΛ$ pair production at the SPD experiment at NICA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25383)
*D. Gubachev,A. Guskov*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文对 NICA 上 SPD 实验在 pp 和 dd 碰撞中测量 ΛΛ̄ 对自旋关联进行可行性研究，基于 SU(6) 和 Burkardt–Jaffe 模型估算关联参数 P_{ΛΛ̄}，并通过全模拟评估探测器适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期 STAR 合作组测量表明 ΛΛ̄ 对的自旋关联对 s s̄ 对的子自旋结构及强子化过程中的自旋转移具有敏感性，加之 CMS 高能测量结果，这为研究部分子到强子转变中的自旋动力学开辟了新途径。作者希望评估 SPD 实验在该方向上的可行性。

Method: 针对 NICA 上 SPD 实验，在 √s_{NN} ≤ 27 GeV 的 pp 和 dd 碰撞条件下开展 ΛΛ̄ 对自旋-自旋关联的可行性研究。在自旋三重态 s s̄ 对假设下，采用 SU(6) 和 Burkardt–Jaffe 模型估算期望关联 P_{ΛΛ̄}，分析过程的运动学性质，并通过全模拟评估探测器对该类测量的适用性。

Result: 给出了在 SU(6) 与 Burkardt–Jaffe 模型下 P_{ΛΛ̄} 的期望估计值，分析了相关过程的运动学特征，并基于全模拟评估了 SPD 探测器用于此类自旋关联测量的适用性。

Conclusion: SPD 实验为研究非微扰与微扰 s s̄ 产生机制的相对贡献提供了机会，并且借助极化束流，可进一步研究初始态极化在自旋相关部分子动力学中的作用。

Abstract: Recent measurements by the STAR collaboration have demonstrated that spin correlations of $Λ\barΛ$ pairs provide sensitivity to the spin structure of $s\bar{s}$ pairs and to spin transfer during hadronization.These results, together with recent CMS measurements at higher energies, open a new avenue for studying spin dynamics in the transition from partons to hadrons.
  A feasibility study of spin-spin correlations in $Λ\barΛ$ pairs at the SPD experiment at NICA is presented for $pp$ and $dd$ collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}}\leq27$~GeV. The expected correlation $P_{Λ\barΛ}$ is estimated within the SU(6) and Burkardt--Jaffe models for a spin-triplet $s\bar{s}$ pair. The kinematic properties of the process are analysed, and the suitability of the detector for such measurements is evaluated using a full simulation. The SPD experiment provides an opportunity to investigate the relative contributions of non-perturbative and perturbative $s\bar{s}$-production mechanisms and, with polarized beams, to study the role of initial-state polarization in spin-dependent partonic dynamics.

</details>

Wilson,S. Tairafune,A. Eguchi,E. Watanabe,J. Xia,K. Terada,R. Asaka,M. Shinoki,T. Yoshida,V. Gousy-Leblanc,M. Nicholson,S. Amanai,R. Shibayama,R. Shimamura*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用Super-Kamiokande 0.401兆吨·年数据，首次将注意力机制CNN应用于核子衰变搜索，给出三核子衰变模式的新部分寿命下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三核子衰变是超出标准模型的重子数破坏过程的重要探针，此前实验约束较弱，需借助更大曝光量与更优事件分类方法提升搜索灵敏度。

Method: 基于Super-Kamiokande五个运行期（SK-I至SK-V）共0.401兆吨·年曝光量，搜索16O(ppp)→13C π+ π+ e+衰变；采用带注意力机制的卷积神经网络（CNN）聚焦切伦科夫环图案的关键区域，以增强事件分类能力，这是该探测器核子衰变搜索中首次使用CNN。

Result: 得到该三核子衰变模式的部分寿命下限为4.2×10^32年，较此前实验约束提升了六个数量级。

Conclusion: 带注意力机制的CNN显著提升了事件分类与搜索灵敏度，结合SK的大统计量数据，可给出远严于以往实验的约束；该方法为后续核子衰变搜索提供了有效途径。

Abstract: We report a new partial lifetime limit of $4.2 \times 10^{32}$ years for the trinucleon decay mode $^{16}\text{O}(ppp) \rightarrow ^{13}\text{C} π^+ π^+ e^+$, obtained from a search conducted using the Super-Kamiokande detector with 0.401 megaton-years of exposure across five operational periods (SK-I: 1996--2001, SK-II: 2002--2005, SK-III: 2006--2008, SK-IV: 2008--2018, SK-V: 2019--2020). This represents an improvement of six orders of magnitude over previous experimental constraints. The analysis utilizes a convolutional neural network (CNN) incorporating an attention mechanism---a computational technique that enables the model to focus on the most relevant regions of Cherenkov ring patterns---to enhance event classification, thereby improving the sensitivity of the search. This is the first application of a CNN to a nucleon decay search in Super-Kamiokande. Furthermore, the large dataset available in Super-Kamiokande (hereafter "SK") strengthens the statistical power of the study, enabling a more stringent constraint than those set by prior experiments.

</details>


### [126] [Feasibility Study of $e^+e^-\to η_cJ/ψ$ Production and Fully-Charmed Tetraquark Searches at STCF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25870)
*Zhuojuan Dong,Xiaorong Zhou,Xiaorui Lv,Zhengyun You,Jin Zhang,Cong Geng*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文评估了在STCF上通过 $e^+e^-\to\eta_c J/\psi$ 通道探测全粲矢量四夸克态 $T_{4c}$ 的预期显著性，在三种基准双电子宽度假设下 ST 显著性可达 $5.1\sigma$–$20.5\sigma$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全粲四夸克态 $T_{4c}$ 是奇特强子研究的重要候选，但其性质（尤其是双电子宽度 $\Gamma_{ee}$）仍不确定；拟建的高亮度超级陶粲装置（STCF）提供了干净的实验环境和阈值扫描条件，因此需要定量评估其探测潜力。

Method: 在 $\sqrt{s}=6.71$–$6.79~\mathrm{GeV}$ 区间进行能量扫描的蒙特卡洛模拟，每点积分亮度 $100~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$；以 $J/\psi$ 轻子衰变的高效率单标记（ST）重建为主要策略，并以 $\eta_c\to K^+K^-\pi^0$ 的双标记（DT）重建作为独立交叉检验；针对尚不确定的 $\Gamma_{ee}$ 设定 $0.25$、$0.5$、$1~\mathrm{eV}$ 三种基准假设。

Result: 对应三种 $\Gamma_{ee}$ 假设，预期 ST 显著性分别为 $5.1\sigma$、$10.6\sigma$ 和 $20.5\sigma$。

Conclusion: 结果表明 STCF 能够对阈值附近的全粲四夸克态提供有意义的探测灵敏度。

Abstract: The proposed high-luminosity Super Tau-Charm Facility (STCF) offers a clean experimental environment for threshold studies of exotic hadrons. In this Letter, we evaluate the expected significance for the fully-charmed vector tetraquark candidate $T_{4c}$ in the $e^+e^- \to η_c J/ψ$ channel at the STCF. Using Monte Carlo simulations of an energy scan from $\sqrt{s}=6.71$ to $6.79~\mathrm{GeV}$ with $100~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ per point, we adopt a high-efficiency single-tag (ST) reconstruction of $J/ψ$ leptonic decays as the primary strategy, with a double-tag (DT) reconstruction of $η_c \to K^+K^-π^0$ retained as an independent cross-check. Because the signal cross section is governed by the still-uncertain dielectron width $Γ_{ee}$, we consider three benchmark hypotheses, $Γ_{ee}=0.25, 0.5, 1~\mathrm{eV}$. The corresponding expected ST significances are $5.1\,σ$, $10.6\,σ$, and $20.5\,σ$, respectively. These results indicate that the STCF can provide meaningful sensitivity to fully-charmed tetraquark states near threshold.

</details>


### [127] [Future Muon Physics Experiments: Muon Beams and Experimental Apparatus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25943)
*Yi Yuan,Leyun Gao,Jian Tang,Qiang Li*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了全球缪子束流设施现状与升级规划，并按三大实验类别（轻子味破坏搜寻、精密测量、缪子散射）总结了所需束流与探测器技术，强调束流强度、时间结构、纯度与相空间控制等关键指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缪子是精度前沿与强度前沿探索基础物理的有力探针，需明确现有及规划中的缪子束流设施能力与实验技术需求，以支撑下一代灵敏度与精度的实现。

Method: 综述性分析：归纳全球缪子束流设施的状态与升级计划；按三类实验归类（1）带电轻子味破坏搜寻：稀有缪子衰变、核内缪子-电子转换、缪子素-反缪子素转换；（2）精密测量：缪子磁偶极矩与电偶极矩、缪子素谱学、缪子原子谱学；（3）缪子散射：MUonE测定强子真空极化、NA64μ 缺失动量搜寻不可见暗扇区、分阶段 PKMu 计划。

Result: 梳理了各类实验对应的束流需求与探测器方案，指出实现下一代灵敏度与精度依赖束流强度、时间结构、纯度、相空间控制，以及束线设计、探测器设计、本底抑制与实验技术。

Conclusion: 缪子实验在味物理、精密测量与暗扇区探测等方面具有重要潜力，其发展高度依赖束流品质与探测技术的协同提升。

Abstract: Muons are powerful probes of fundamental physics at the precision and intensity frontiers. We review the status and planned upgrades of muon-beam facilities worldwide, together with the experimental apparatus required across three experimental categories. Charged-lepton flavor violation (CLFV) searches test charged-lepton flavor conservation through rare muon decays, muon-to-electron conversion in nuclei, and muonium--antimuonium conversion. Precision measurements of the muon magnetic and electric dipole moments, muonium spectroscopy, and muonic-atom spectroscopy test the Standard Model and bound-state QED and determine fundamental constants and nuclear charge and magnetization distributions. Muon-scattering programs include the MUonE determination of hadronic vacuum polarization, NA64$μ$ missing-momentum searches for invisible dark sectors, and the phased PKMu program, which investigates muonphilic dark matter, light mediators, LFV muon--electron processes, and atomic effects in resonant annihilation. We emphasize the beam intensity, timing, purity, and phase-space control, as well as beamline and detector design, background suppression, and experimental techniques needed to achieve next-generation sensitivity and precision.

</details>


### [128] [Improving the CMS High Level Trigger tracking at the HL-LHC with novel and evolved heterogeneous algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26376)
*Mario Masciovecchio*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 在HL-LHC下CMS HLT径迹重建面临算力瓶颈，本文结合mkFit、Patatrack与LST算法并利用ML技术，在异构计算系统上实现并展示最先进的重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC实验中带电粒子径迹重建是事件重建链中计算量最大的任务之一。HL-LHC的预测显示，单线程CPU算法所需的计算资源将超出预期可用资源。因此，实验需要采用新颖且演进的径迹重建算法，运行于包含多核CPU与GPU的异构计算系统中，在保持最佳重建效率的同时最大化计算性能。

Method: 在CMS HLT的HL-LHC场景下，利用mkFit算法的并行化与向量化特性在CPU上执行模式识别，其种子径迹由完全可并行化且硬件无关的Patatrack与LST算法产生，适配异构系统。Patatrack已用于Run 3的CMS像素径迹重建，LST是面向HL-LHC CMS外层径迹器重建的新算法。三者结合并使用机器学习技术（深度神经网络与多目标粒子群优化）抑制重复与误重建径迹。

Result: 展示了CMS HLT在HL-LHC下结合mkFit、Patatrack与LST算法的径迹重建的最先进性能，并给出了进一步改进的前景，重点聚焦于ML技术在CMS径迹重建中的应用。

Conclusion: 通过mkFit、Patatrack与LST的协同以及ML技术的引入，可在异构计算系统上有效应对HL-LHC径迹重建的算力挑战，同时保持高重建效率，并具备进一步优化的空间。

Abstract: Charged particle track reconstruction is one of the heaviest computational tasks in the event reconstruction chain at LHC experiments. Furthermore, projections for the High Luminosity LHC (HL-LHC) show that the required computing resources for single-threaded CPU algorithms will exceed those that are expected to be available. It follows that experiments at the HL-LHC will need to employ novel and evolved track reconstruction algorithms, within heterogeneous computing systems that include many-core CPUs as well as GPUs, in the attempt to maximize the computational performance while retaining the best possible reconstruction efficiency. In the context of the CMS High Level Trigger (HLT) at the HL-LHC, the mkFit algorithm, already in use for the CMS track reconstruction during the LHC Run 3, will exploit its parallelized and vectorized nature on CPUs to perform pattern recognition using seed tracks produced with algorithms that are designed to be fully parallelizable and hardware agnostic, thus suitable for heterogeneous systems: the Patatrack and the Line Segment Tracking (LST) algorithms. Patatrack is an established algorithm, already used for the CMS pixel track reconstruction at HLT during the Run 3 of the LHC, while LST is a novel algorithm, recently integrated in the CMS software, targeting the reconstruction of tracks in the outer tracker of the HL-LHC CMS detector. The state-of-the-art performance for the CMS HLT track reconstruction at the HL-LHC is presented, obtained using the combination of the mkFit, Patatrack, and LST algorithms, that in turn use machine-learning (ML) techniques such as deep neural networks and multi-objective particle swarm optimization to suppress duplicate and misreconstructed tracks. Prospects of further improvements are also presented, with a focus on the usage of ML techniques for track reconstruction at CMS.

</details>


### [129] [An enhanced track reconstruction for the CMS experiment at the HL-LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26383)
*Mario Masciovecchio*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CMS提出phase-2基线径迹重建策略，统一多种算法范式并整合机器学习，结合GPU与CPU优化方法，在HL-LHC极端堆积条件下保持高效率和分辨率，同时改善假径迹率与计算性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HL-LHC的极端堆积条件给事件重建带来巨大挑战，需在保证径迹重建效率和分辨率的同时提升计算性能、降低假径迹率，并扩展对长寿命粒子等稀有信号的灵敏度。

Method: 将多样的算法范式统一，并把机器学习技术整合为连贯流程；采用异构计算策略，结合GPU优化算法（Patatrack、LST）与向量化CPU方法（mkFit），充分利用现代硬件提升吞吐量，并引入全局位移径迹重建。

Result: 该策略在保持高效率和高质量径迹重建的同时，改善了假径迹率和计算性能，降低了资源需求，并扩展了对长寿命粒子等稀有特征信号的灵敏度。

Conclusion: 该phase-2 CMS基线径迹重建策略兼顾在线与离线重建需求，为HL-LHC极端环境下高效径迹重建提供可行方案，并增强物理探测能力。

Abstract: This contribution presents the new phase-2 CMS baseline tracking strategy currently proposed for online event reconstruction, which can be similarly useful in offline reconstruction under the extreme pileup conditions of the High-Luminosity LHC (HL-LHC). By unifying diverse algorithmic paradigms and integrating machine learning (ML) techniques into a coherent sequence, CMS can maintain high-efficiency and high-resolution tracking while improving the fake track rate and the computational performance, a crucial aspect especially for the HL-LHC. The approach combines GPU-optimized algorithms (Patatrack, LST) with vectorized CPU methods (mkFit), exploiting modern hardware to maximize throughput. This heterogeneous strategy reduces the resource requirements and enhances the physics reach by incorporating a global displaced track reconstruction and extending sensitivity to rare, interesting signatures, such as long-lived particles.

</details>


### [130] [Complementary Time- and Distance-Based Methods for Cosmogenic $^{9}$Li/$^{8}$He Background Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26538)
*Cailian Jiang,Qishan Liu,Liangjian Wen,Gaosong Li,Zeyuan Yu,Wanlei Guo,Lei Zhang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出一种距离约束的联合时间拟合方法（DCJ-MuCAT），用于估计低能中微子实验中残余的宇宙成因⁹Li/⁸He本底，在模拟中将统计不确定度较J-MuCAT降低逾40%、较TraDiTS降低逾10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙成因⁹Li和⁸He同位素的β延迟中子衰变信号可模拟逆β衰变信号，是低能中微子实验的重要关联本底。高μ子率下基于"距上次μ子时间"的传统估计方法难以适用，而μ子相关 veto 又进一步减少了残余同位素统计量，亟需更稳健的估计方法。

Method: 将传统时间拟合扩展为事件级、按μ子分类的联合时间拟合（J-MuCAT），使用每个能量损失类别中距最近前序μ子的时间；同时从候选事件到μ子径迹的距离分布（TraDiTS）获得互补估计，利用远距离区域确定并扣除与μ子无关的成分，再以此约束J-MuCAT拟合，构成距离约束的DCJ-MuCAT拟合。

Result: 在带有连续宇宙成因本底 veto 的探测器级模拟中，DCJ-MuCAT相对J-MuCAT将统计不确定度降低逾40%，相对TraDiTS降低逾10%；拟合结果与模拟真值保持一致。适用性研究显示在较宽的μ子率和同位素比例范围内均表现良好。

Conclusion: 该框架为大型中微子探测器中残余⁹Li/⁸He本底的估计提供了一种实用方法。

Abstract: Cosmogenic $^{9}$Li and $^{8}$He isotopes constitute an important correlated background in low-energy neutrino experiments because their $β$-delayed neutron decay signatures can mimic inverse beta decay signals. Conventional estimates based on the time since the last muon become challenging at high muon rates, while muon-related vetoes further reduce the residual isotope statistics. We extend the conventional time fit to an event-level muon-categorized joint time (J-MuCAT) fit, which uses the time to the most recent preceding muon in each energy-loss category. A complementary estimate is obtained from the candidate-to-muon-track distance distribution (TraDiTS). The muon-uncorrelated component is determined from the far-distance region and subtracted. This estimate is then used to constrain the J-MuCAT fit, defining the distance-constrained J-MuCAT (DCJ-MuCAT) fit. In detector-level simulation with successive cosmogenic-background vetoes, DCJ-MuCAT reduces the statistical uncertainty by more than $40\%$ relative to J-MuCAT and by more than $10\%$ relative to the TraDiTS. The fitted results remain consistent with the simulation truth. Applicability studies further show good performance over a broad range of muon rates and isotope fractions. The proposed framework provides a practical approach for estimating residual $^{9}$Li/$^{8}$He backgrounds in large neutrino detectors.

</details>


### [131] [Extending the reconstruction of displaced tracks at CMS during the HL-LHC with Line Segment Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26695)
*Jade Chismar*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Line Segment Tracking (LST)算法中的新对象quadruplet (T4)，用于提升HL-LHC高堆积环境下位移径迹的重建接受度，将径向位移接受范围从40 cm扩展至60 cm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC升级为HL-LHC后，每个束团交叉的质子-质子同时相互作用数（堆积）大幅增加，给带电粒子径迹重建的计算资源带来巨大压力；同时高堆积场景下位移径迹的重建接受度不足，需要高效且可并行化的算法来扩展接受范围。

Method: LST是一种高度可并行化、可在GPU上高效运行的算法，已集成到CMS软件中，仅使用外层径迹探测器命中构建径迹候选。本文引入新的LST对象quadruplet (T4)，定义为具有公共线段的两个triplet的链接对，专门针对位移径迹特征。

Result: 在LST中使用T4后，位移径迹的效率得到增强，相比未使用LST的序列（径向位移约8 cm），接受度提升超过4倍；相比未使用T4的LST，径向位移接受范围从40 cm扩展至60 cm。

Conclusion: 引入T4对象的改进版LST算法在多个物理场景中表现良好，有效扩展了HL-LHC位移径迹的接受度，为高堆积条件下的径迹重建提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The upgrade of the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) to the High-Luminosity LHC (HL-LHC) will greatly increase the average number of simultaneous proton-proton interactions per bunch-crossing (pileup), and will place significant demands on computing resources for the reconstruction of charged-particle tracks. Line Segment Tracking (LST) is a novel, highly parallelizable algorithm that can run efficiently on GPUs and has been integrated into the CMS software to enable the reconstruction of displaced tracks in the high pileup scenarios of the HL-LHC. The LST algorithm is designed to build track candidates using only outer tracker hits, naturally allowing for high acceptance of displaced track signatures. In the HL-LHC tracking reconstruction sequence without LST, displaced tracking ends at a radial displacement of the track origin of approximately 8 cm. The usage of LST in track reconstruction enhanced efficiency for displaced tracks, extending the acceptance in radial displacement from the track origin by more than a factor of 4. In this work, we introduce a new LST object, the quadruplet (T4), defined as a linked pair of triplets with a common line segment, targeting displaced track signatures. We present the performance of this improved implementation of the LST algorithm in several physics scenarios, extending the displaced-track acceptance from 40 cm to 60 cm in radial displacement compared to LST without T4s.

</details>

### [132] [Stochastic Field Theory of HIV Latency: Instanton Dynamics and the Path to Viral Rebound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25136)
*Jose de Jesus Bernal-Alvarado,David Delepine,Georges Delepine*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 将HIV潜伏到活跃感染的随机跃迁建模为Doi-Peliti路径积分，在半经典极限下导出Hamilton-Jacobi方程与瞬子作用量，校准临床数据后预测病毒反弹的平均首次通过时间约为6个月。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于常微分方程（ODE）的确定性模型忽略了小种群极限下的群体噪声，无法刻画灭绝边界及随后的病毒反弹，而临床潜伏到活跃HIV感染的转变本质上是随机过程。

Method: 用Doi-Peliti二次量子化形式将主方程映射为相干态路径积分，验证随机Hamiltonian在经典流形\barφ_i=1上还原平均场ODE；在半经典（大系统尺度）极限下导出八个耦合的Hamilton-Jacobi方程，识别无病毒与地方性固定点并求基本再生数R_0；对单病毒粒子爆发(n=1)的解析可解情形，证明随机Hamiltonian分解为两个双线性项，揭示零能面即瞬子轨迹，再用准稳态约化得到一阶线性ODE并给出瞬子作用量S_inst的闭式解。

Result: 对n=1情形获得瞬子作用量的闭式表达；用临床数据（感染细胞半衰期δ_I^{-1}≈2天、病毒爆发规模N≈10^3、清除率δ_V≈23天^{-1}、再激活率η≈10^{-3}天^{-1}、潜伏库规模L_0≈10^6细胞）校准后，病毒反弹的平均首次通过时间（MFPT）约为6个月，视潜伏库大小可从数天到数年不等。

Conclusion: 该随机路径积分框架克服了确定性ODE模型忽略群体噪声的缺陷，所得反弹时间尺度与临床中断治疗后观测到的反弹时间在定性上一致，为HIV潜伏激活的随机动力学提供了可解析的定量描述。

Abstract: The transition from clinical latency to active HIV infection is a stochastic process. Traditional deterministic models based on ordinary differential equations (ODEs) fail to capture the extinction boundary and the subsequent rebound of the virus because they neglect demographic noise in the small-population limit. We map the resulting Master Equation onto a coherent-state path integral using the Doi-Peliti second-quantization formalism and verify that the stochastic Hamiltonian reproduces the correct mean-field ODEs on the classical manifold $\barφ_i = 1$. In the semiclassical (large system-size) limit we derive eight coupled Hamilton-Jacobi equations, identify the virus-free and endemic fixed points, and obtain the basic reproduction number $R_0$ for the four-species network. For the analytically tractable case of single-virion bursting ($n=1$) we show that the stochastic Hamiltonian \emph{factorizes} into two bilinear terms, exposing a non-trivial zero-energy surface that constitutes the instanton trajectory. A quasi-steady-state reduction then yields a first-order linear ODE whose closed-form solution gives the instanton action $S_{\rm inst}$ in terms of biological parameters. Calibrating to clinical data---infected-cell half-life $δ_I^{-1}\approx 2\,\rm d$, viral burst size $N\approx 10^3$, clearance rate $δ_V\approx 23\,\rm d^{-1}$, reactivation rate $η\approx 10^{-3}\,\rm d^{-1}$, and latent reservoir size $L_0\approx 10^6$ cells---we obtain a mean first-passage time (MFPT) to viral rebound of $\approx 6$ months for a typical patient, with a range of days to years depending on reservoir size. These predictions are qualitatively consistent with observed post-interruption rebound timescales.

</details>


### [133] [Mutation Order and Selection Shape Intratumor Heterogeneity in Tumor Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25838)
*Yichen Chu,Xiaoxia Sheng,Xuanming Zhang,Zicheng Wang*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文建立双驱动突变的多类型分支过程模型，按突变顺序与独立转化事件区分恶性克隆，在逐次指数近似下求得通路克隆大小的点过程极限及组合种群的期望Simpson指数闭式解，揭示突变顺序如何通过通路权重与通路内集中度共同决定瘤内异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癌症进展常需多个驱动突变，但相同驱动可以不同顺序获得；这些顺序路径如何共同塑造肿瘤克隆结构尚不清楚。作者希望刻画突变顺序、突变可获得性、选择与上位效应如何联合决定谱系层面的瘤内异质性。

Method: 构建需两个驱动突变才完成恶性转化的多类型分支过程模型，按突变顺序及建立克隆的独立转化事件对恶性细胞分类。在逐次指数近似下，建立通路特异性克隆大小的点过程极限，并推导组合恶性种群期望Simpson指数的闭式表达式。

Result: 当两种突变顺序产生相同净增长率的恶性细胞时，Simpson指数可分解为有效通路权重（由突变率及前序阶段的生灭动态决定）与通路内集中度项（由中间态到恶性态的增长率之比决定）。当某驱动早期获得时，它会扩增中间谱系、增加独立恶性创始者的来源；而同一驱动最后获得时，则强化早期建立恶性克隆的增长与年龄优势。在加性适应度效应下，这两种相反机制可导致选择优势与克隆集中度之间呈非单调关系。阈值式非加性适应度效应可产生高度集中的恶性种群，而顺序依赖的终末适应度使增长更快的通路渐近主导。

Conclusion: 结果表明，突变顺序、突变可获得性、选择与上位效应共同决定谱系层面的瘤内异质性；驱动突变的效应关键取决于其获得时机。

Abstract: Cancer progression often requires multiple driver mutations, but the same drivers may be acquired in different orders. How these pathways jointly shape tumor clonal structure remains unclear. We develop a multitype branching-process model in which malignant transformation requires two driver mutations, distinguishing malignant cells by mutation order and the independent transformation event that founded their clone. Under a successive exponential approximation, we establish point-process limits for pathway-specific clone sizes and derive a closed-form expression for the limiting expected Simpson's index of the combined malignant population. When both mutation orders yield malignant cells with the same net growth rate, the index decomposes into effective pathway weights, determined by mutation rates and birth-death dynamics at preceding stages, and within-pathway concentration terms, determined by intermediate-to-malignant growth-rate ratios. A driver's effect on heterogeneity thus depends critically on when it is acquired. A strong driver acquired early expands the intermediate lineage and increases the supply of independent malignant founders, whereas the same driver acquired last strengthens the growth and age advantage of early-founded malignant clones. Under additive fitness effects, these opposing mechanisms can produce a non-monotone relationship between selective advantage and clonal concentration. Threshold-like non-additive fitness effects can generate highly concentrated malignant populations, while order-dependent terminal fitness causes the faster-growing pathway to dominate asymptotically. These results show how mutation order, mutational accessibility, selection, and epistasis jointly determine lineage-level intratumor heterogeneity.

</details>


### [134] [How long versus when: worker mortality synchrony is widespread but decoupled from body size in Australian ants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26278)
*Alana Moscardi,Rafael da Silva,Gleycon Silva*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 该研究利用18种澳大利亚蚂蚁、106个队列的15个月野外数据，发现种内死亡率同步现象普遍（集合平均ICC为0.21±0.09），与体大小无关，且仅部分由温度驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群落人口学模型通常将工蚁死亡视为独立事件，但共享的环境暴露可能在物种内同步死亡。作者希望量化种内死亡同步程度，并检验体大小与温度是否为候选关联因素。

Method: 使用18种澳大利亚蚂蚁、106个队列、历时15个月的野外数据集，以组内相关系数（ICC）量化种内死亡同步性；检验体大小（工蚁质量）与ICC的关系（Spearman相关、加权最小二乘WLS回归），并分析温度与死亡脉冲的滞后关联（峰值r与多滞后平均r，含Bonferroni校正和敏感性分析）。

Result: 同步普遍存在：18种中9种的可信区间不含零，集合平均ICC为0.21±0.09，约21%的日死亡方差来自种内共享暴露。体大小无缓冲作用：工蚁质量与ICC无关（Spearman rho=+0.10，p=0.69；WLS beta=+0.05，p=0.35）。温度呈提示性滞后关联：15个可分析物种中13种最高温领先死亡脉冲1-7天，平均峰值r=0.16，但无物种通过Bonferroni校正。用滞后1-7天平均相关替代峰值后，集合水平信号消失（平均r=-0.03，p=0.93），且ICC与温度耦合强度无关（Spearman rho=-0.004，p=0.99）。

Conclusion: 死亡率同步现象普遍、与体大小无关、仅部分由温度驱动。相较于体重，物种特异性的耐热性更可能是解释同步死亡变异的有力候选因素。

Abstract: Colony demographic models often treat worker deaths as independent events, but shared environmental exposure may synchronize mortality within species. Using a 15-month field dataset of 18 Australian ant species and 106 cohorts, we quantified intra-specific mortality synchrony with the intra-class correlation coefficient (ICC) and tested body size and temperature as candidate correlates. Synchrony was widespread: credible intervals excluded zero in 9 of 18 species, and the assemblage mean ICC was 0.21 +/- 0.09, indicating that about 21% of daily mortality variance reflected shared within-species exposure. Body size did not buffer synchrony. Worker mass was unrelated to ICC (Spearman rho = +0.10, p = 0.69; WLS beta = +0.05, p = 0.35), decoupling how long workers live from when they die together. Temperature showed a suggestive lagged association: maximum temperature preceded mortality pulses by 1-7 days in 13 of 15 analyzable species, with mean peak r = 0.16, but no species survived Bonferroni correction. A sensitivity analysis using mean correlation across lags 1-7 days instead of the peak removed the assemblage-level signal (mean r = -0.03, p = 0.93), and ICC was unrelated to temperature-coupling strength (Spearman rho = -0.004, p = 0.99). Mortality synchrony is therefore widespread, size-independent, and only partly thermal.
