Species-specific thermal tolerance, rather than body mass, is a more promising candidate for explaining variation in synchronized mortality.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Towards Adaptive Interaction Strategies for Human Companion Robot via Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25031)
*Cong-Thanh Vu,Yen-Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于深度强化学习(DRL)的人机伴随策略，结合MPPI与CBF控制，使移动机器人动态调整跟随位姿，室内外实验成功率与跟踪精度分别提升至少24%和47%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人机伴随方法将机器人限制在人的固定相对位置（后方、前方或并排），难以适应动态工作空间，缺乏灵活性；同时在真实环境中实现灵活伴随并兼顾安全性、舒适性与社交接受度存在挑战。

Method: 1) 定义"交互空间"刻画人与机器人在环境约束下的关系，作为DRL状态空间的基础，帮助机器人适应环境变化；2) 采用深度强化学习(DRL)构建伴随策略，使机器人根据条件动态调整跟踪位置；3) 将模型预测路径积分(MPPI)控制与控制障碍函数(CBF)结合，构建人机伴随控制器，实现位置与朝向的准确跟踪并避障。

Result: 在室内外真实场景中评估并与现有研究对比：成功率与跟踪精度分别提升至少24%和47%，并提升了人的舒适度；机器人可在人行走速度达1.7 m/s时灵活伴随，动态调整策略而不局限于固定位置，同时尊重人的亲密空间，保证安全、舒适与有效避障。

Conclusion: 所提方法突破了固定相对位置的限制，实现了灵活、安全且社交友好的真实环境人机伴随，验证了DRL与MPPI+CBF结合策略的有效性。

Abstract: In the field of Human-Robot Interaction (HRI), achieving flexibility in human-accompanying within real-world environments holds great potential for various applications but also poses significant challenges. Traditional methods typically restrict robots to fixed positions relative to humans, such as tracking from behind, in front, or side-by-side, which limits robot adaptability in dynamic workspaces. This study introduces a novel human-companioning strategy that uses Reinforcement Learning (DRL) to enable mobile robots to dynamically adjust their tracking positions according to varying conditions. An interaction space is defined to capture the relationship between the human and the robot while considering the environment, which serves as the basis for state spaces in DRL to assist the robot in adapting to environmental changes. A human-robot companion controller is developed by integrating Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control with Control Barrier Functions (CBF), ensuring that the robot accurately follows the target's movement in both position and orientation while avoiding obstacles and enhancing social acceptance and safety. The proposed approach is evaluated in real-world scenarios, both indoors and outdoors, and compared with other studies. The results show that the proposed method improves the success rate and tracking accuracy by at least 24% and 47%, respectively, while enhancing human comfort. Experiments demonstrate the robot's ability to flexibly accompany a person walking at speeds of up to 1.7 m/s, dynamically adjusting its strategy without being confined to a fixed position. Additionally, the robot respects the human's intimate space to ensure safety, comfort, and effective obstacle avoidance.

</details>


### [136] [Cosserat Modeling of Trimmed Helicoid Soft Arms with a Separated-Section Constitutive Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25264)
*Zhihang Qin,Linxin Hou,Zeyu Zhong,Yuchen Sun,Wenci Xin,Yueheng Zhang,Ji Qi,Jie Wang,Muhammad Sunny Nazeer,Yu Jun Tan,Federico Renda,Cecilia Laschi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对修剪螺旋臂提出分离截面本构律，将各螺旋域在其局部坐标系中求解并映射回主干，得到强各向异性有效截面刚度，并嵌入几何精确动态Cosserat模型，实现高精度、可快速求解的软体机器人建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Cosserat杆模型通过在公共截面上叠加材料属性来构造截面刚度，该假设对修剪螺旋臂不成立：其承载螺旋域彼此分离，仅通过稀疏融合交叉点连接，因此需要新的本构描述。

Method: 提出分离截面本构律：各螺旋域在局部坐标系中评估本构响应并拉回主干得到有效主干刚度；用稀疏融合力学刻画相邻域相对运动带来的附加柔度，确定弯曲、扭转、拉伸的通道折减剖面η_c(s/L)；将所得截面律嵌入几何精确动态Cosserat模型，采用GVS离散化并考虑穿线腱驱动。

Result: 有效截面刚度呈强各向异性：弯曲与拉伸约降低一个数量级，而扭转接近有效主干刚度。在103组实测构型上，三个数据集的汇总归一化位置误差分别为7.7%、6.7%和7.8%，单次全臂求解在单核CPU（Intel Xeon，Cascade Lake，2.8 GHz）上约需0.3 s。

Conclusion: 该截面律可支持快速基于模型的规划、状态与载荷估计，以及面向结构化软体机器人的形态—控制协同设计。

Abstract: Cosserat rod models for soft robots usually construct sectional stiffness by summing material properties over a common cross-section. This assumption becomes inaccurate for trimmed helicoid arms, where load-bearing helix domains are separated and connected only through sparse fused crossings. This paper formulates a separated-section constitutive law that evaluates each helix domain in its local frame and pulls its constitutive response back to the backbone, yielding an effective backbone stiffness. Sparse-fusion mechanics captures the additional compliance caused by relative motion between neighboring domains and determines channel-wise reduction profiles $η_c(s/L)$ for bending, torsion, and extension. The resulting effective sectional stiffness is strongly anisotropic: bending and extension are reduced by about one order of magnitude, whereas torsion remains close to the effective backbone stiffness. The resulting sectional law is embedded in a geometrically exact dynamic Cosserat model with GVS discretization and routed-tendon actuation. Across 103 measured configurations, the three datasets give pooled normalized position errors of \SI{7.7}{\percent}, \SI{6.7}{\percent}, and \SI{7.8}{\percent}, while each full-arm solve requires approximately \SI{0.3}{s} on one CPU core (Intel Xeon, Cascade Lake, \SI{2.8}{GHz}), enabling rapid model-based planning, state and load estimation, and morphology--control co-design for architected soft robots.

</details>


### [137] [Beyond the Flat Seafloor: A Closed-Form Two-View Constraint to Aid Sidescan Sonar Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25271)
*Kalin Norman,Joshua G. Mangelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文基于多视图几何形式化侧扫声纳双视图几何约束，证明共享特征位于球面与平面交线上的轨迹内，并通过蒙特卡洛仿真量化模糊轨迹尺寸，给出航测规划建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 侧扫声纳是有人与自主海洋探测的常用传感器，但少有方法利用其几何投影模型；因仅提供一维距离测量，常采用平坦海底等近似假设。本文希望避免类似近似，转而建立基于多视图几何的严格建模。

Method: 提出基于多视图几何的方法，形式化双视图几何约束，推导并证明共享特征被约束在球面与平面相交的轨迹（模糊轨迹）内；结合真实孔径与安装几何，对水面船只与水下潜器进行蒙特卡洛仿真，刻画轨迹尺寸的决定因素，并仿真两种平台的相对轨迹。

Result: 模糊轨迹长度主要受仰角失配支配，在约20度的中等斜交角处达到峰值，而在近于平行和反平行的通过路径下轨迹长度最小；相对轨迹仿真被转化为具体的测量规划指导。

Conclusion: 该工作为侧扫声纳提供了不依赖平坦海底假设的几何约束框架，并给出可操作的航测规划准则。

Abstract: Sidescan sonar is a common sensor for both manned and autonomous marine exploration and mapping, yet very few methods build upon or exploit the geometric projection model of the sensor. As sidescan sonar is limited to a 1D range measurement, many approximations are frequently used, including the long-standing assumption of a flat seafloor. Rather than make similar approximations, this paper focuses on a multi-view geometry based approach and formalizes a two-view geometric constraint and proves that a shared feature is constrained to a locus within the intersection of a sphere and a plane. In addition, we characterize what governs the size of the ambiguity locus through Monte Carlo simulation that is grounded in real aperture and mounting geometry for both a surface vessel and an underwater vehicle. We translate additional simulations of relative trajectories for both vehicle platforms into concrete survey-planning guidance. Our results show that locus length is strongly governed by elevation misalignment, and peaks at a moderate oblique crossing angle of approximately 20 degrees, with minimal locus lengths obtained at near parallel and anti-parallel passes.

</details>


### [138] [Learning to Plan in Human-Robot Collaboration: Multimodal Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25274)
*Afagh Mehri Shervedani,Siyu Li,Natawut Monaikul,Bahareh Abbasi,Barbara Di Eugenio,Miloš Žefran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于强化学习的方法，自动生成机器人在多模态人机协作中的交互策略，并以家庭环境中辅助用户寻找物体的场景进行验证，真人实验显示可用性高、任务完成效果好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向老年人和残障人士的机器人助手需要与用户有效协作，其核心是交互管理器，负责观察任务、推断人的状态与意图并选择最优动作。由于该领域数据稀疏，现有多模态系统策略通常依靠手工设计，随着交互复杂度提升，这种方式难以扩展。

Method: 提出用强化学习自动生成机器人的多模态策略。面向机器人在家庭环境中辅助用户寻找物体的真实场景，系统需处理语言与物理动作等多模态信号以选择最佳动作。与传统对话系统不同，该智能体在基于人类数据构建的模拟器中训练，可处理多种模态；采用简单的高层奖励函数，无需精细调参，并通过设定一些前置条件来加速训练。

Result: 在真实环境中的真人研究评估表明结果令人鼓舞，系统具有较高的可用性，并能够有效完成任务。

Conclusion: 该基于强化学习的方法为多模态人机协作中交互管理器的设计提供了一种可扩展且可解释的替代方案。

Abstract: Robot assistants for older adults and people with disabilities need to perform collaborative tasks with users effectively. The core component of these systems is an interaction manager whose job is to observe and assess the task and infer the state of the human and their intent for the robot to choose the best course of action. Due to the sparseness of the data in this domain, the policy for such multimodal systems is often crafted by hand; as the complexity of interactions grows, this process is not scalable. This paper proposes a reinforcement learning (RL) approach to automatically generate the multimodal policy of the robot. Our system focuses on a realistic scenario where a robot assists a user in locating objects within a home environment, managing multimodal signals, including language and physical actions, to select the best action. In contrast to traditional dialog systems, our agent is trained with a simulator that uses human data and can deal with multiple modalities. We use a simple high-level reward function that needs no fine-tuning and enforce some preconditions to speed up the training process. A human study evaluating the system in a real-world setting demonstrates promising results, indicating high usability and effective task completion. This RL-based approach offers a scalable and interpretable alternative for designing interaction managers in multimodal human-robot collaborations.

</details>


### [139] [JAMB: Joint Action-Motion Diffusion for Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25322)
*Chuyang Xiao,Peilin Meng,David Held*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: JAMB：一种将双臂动作与未来3D点轨迹联合去噪的扩散策略，在共享Transformer中让动作与轨迹假设互相修正，在RoboTwin 2.0 16项任务上平均成功率83.4%，较最强基线提升23.9个百分点，并在真实机器人3项任务上分别超越纯动作与辅助几何预测方法50.0与21.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂协同操作困难，因为任一臂的运动都会改变共享3D场景并影响另一臂。然而多数扩散策略在生成动作时未显式建模这种未来几何后果；已有的预测式变体也仅将未来状态用作辅助监督或固定条件，无法在去噪过程中与动作相互影响。

Method: 提出JAMB：一个扩散策略，在共享Transformer内联合去噪双臂动作与未来3D点轨迹，使动作与轨迹假设在去噪全程共同演化、互相提供信息与修正。并将多模态表征统一到共享时空坐标系中，以支持联合去噪时的几何感知交互。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0多种双臂操作任务与真实机器人上与纯动作策略及不同未来预测方法（涵盖不同状态表示与学习目标）对比：16项仿真任务平均成功率83.4%，超最强基线23.9个百分点；3项真实任务较纯动作、辅助几何预测方法分别高出50.0与21.2个百分点；对杂乱场景与分布外背景的泛化更强。

Conclusion: 结果表明联合动作—运动建模框架对协同双臂操作有效，显式建模未来几何后果并在去噪中与动作交互可显著提升性能与泛化能力。

Abstract: Coordinated bimanual manipulation is challenging because the motion of either arm can alter the shared 3D scene and thereby affect the other arm. Yet most diffusion policies generate actions without explicitly modeling these future geometric consequences, while predictive variants typically use future state only as auxiliary supervision or fixed conditioning. We address this limitation by proposing JAMB, a diffusion policy that jointly denoises bimanual actions and future 3D point tracks. By allowing action and track hypotheses to evolve together within a shared Transformer, each can inform and refine the other throughout denoising. We further ground multimodal representations in a shared spatiotemporal coordinate system to facilitate geometry-aware interaction during joint denoising. We evaluate JAMB on diverse bimanual manipulation tasks in RoboTwin 2.0 and on a real-world robot, comparing it with action-only policies and alternative future-prediction approaches spanning different state representations and learning objectives. Across 16 simulation tasks, JAMB achieves an average success rate of 83.4%, outperforming the strongest baseline by 23.9 percentage points. On three real-world tasks, it outperforms the action-only and auxiliary geometry prediction methods by 50.0 and 21.2 percentage points, respectively. Beyond these performance gains, JAMB shows stronger generalization to cluttered scenes and out-of-distribution backgrounds than the evaluated baselines. Together, these results demonstrate the effectiveness of our joint action-motion modeling framework for coordinated bimanual manipulation. Our project website is available at https://jam-bimanual.github.io/

</details>


### [140] [GINIO: A Geometric SO(3)-Equivariant Interface for Neural Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25338)
*Chankyo Kim,Minghan Zhu,Tzu-Yuan Lin,Avantika Rattan,Maani Ghaffari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GINIO——一种SO(3)等变的神经惯性里程计接口，保证运动测量均值按向量、协方差按二阶张量随IMU坐标系旋转一致变换，并引入Last-Frame Alignment(LFA)实现高效的传感器系学习；在TLIO、NanoBench、Fetch上显著提升精度与鲁棒性，谱协方差将协方差等变误差降低三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经惯性里程计多将网络作为滤波器内的学习测量，但这些测量应能在任意IMU安装约定下保持一致的几何变换律：其均值应作向量变换，协方差应作二阶张量的同余变换。然而现有方法（如EqNIO等）在任意旋转下缺乏严格的等变保证，导致跨安装/重安装场景性能严重退化。

Method: 提出GINIO——面向任意IMU测量帧旋转的几何SO(3)等变神经惯性里程计接口。给定标定后的IMU窗口，预测满足张量变换律的运动测量与不确定性。引入Last-Frame Alignment(LFA)：一种确定性预处理，可证与SO(3)等变预测器的世界系训练等价，从而支持高效的传感器系学习。连接式估计器跟踪IMU偏置等传感器局部状态，将冗余量估计与学习测量强制的几何律分离。该接口可实例化于滤波器连接NIO、AirIO式循环空中预测、EqNIO式全SO(3)规范化及ResNet式时序骨干。

Result: 在TLIO上，GINIO达2.018 m ID/SO(3) ATE，而EqNIO退化至76.389 m，且FLOPs减少11.6倍；在NanoBench上，AirIO式实例在无外部姿态输入下将ATE从5.579 m降至1.430 m，ResNet式实例达0.581 m ATE优于ResNet1D的0.645 m；在Fetch上，GINIO在无重训练条件下将未见物理重安装ATE从8.15 m降至0.50 m。不确定性方面，谱协方差相比对角头将协方差等变误差降低超过三个数量级。

Conclusion: GINIO通过SO(3)等变接口与LFA预处理，在不牺牲效率的前提下为神经惯性里程计提供严格的几何一致性保证，并在多数据集上显著提升精度、泛化性与不确定性建模质量，鲁棒性超出精确坐标系保证的范畴。

Abstract: Neural inertial odometry increasingly uses networks as learned measurements inside filtering pipelines. Such measurements should transform consistently under arbitrary IMU mounting conventions: their mean must transform as a vector, and their covariance must transform congruently as a second-order tensor. We present GINIO, a geometric SO(3)-equivariant interface for neural inertial odometry under arbitrary rotations of the IMU measurement frame. Given calibrated IMU windows, our framework predicts a motion measurement and uncertainty obeying these tensorial laws. To support efficient sensor-frame learning, we introduce Last-Frame Alignment (LFA), a deterministic preprocessing step that is provably equivalent to world-frame training for SO(3)-equivariant predictors. The connected estimator tracks sensor-local states such as IMU bias, separating nuisance estimation from the geometric law enforced by the learned measurement. We instantiate the same interface in filter-connected NIO, AirIO-style recurrent aerial prediction, EqNIO-style full-SO(3) canonicalization, and ResNet-style temporal backbones. On TLIO, GINIO achieves 2.018 m ID/SO(3) ATE while EqNIO degrades to 76.389 m, using 11.6x fewer FLOPs. On NanoBench, our AirIO-style instantiation improves ATE from 5.579 m to 1.430 m without external attitude input, and our ResNet-style instantiation reaches 0.581 m ATE versus 0.645 m for ResNet1D. On Fetch, GINIO empirically reduces unseen physical-remount ATE from 8.15 m to 0.50 m without retraining, demonstrating robustness beyond the exact coordinate-frame guarantee. For uncertainty, spectral covariance reduces covariance-equivariance error by over three orders of magnitude compared with a diagonal head.

</details>


### [141] [Learning from Humans for Proactive Assistance in Human-Robot Collaborative Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25351)
*Elvin Yang,Christoforos Mavrogiannis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PROACT：通过人类协作行为预测与柔性全身控制结合，提升人-机器人协作搬运的效率与用户体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去的工作往往将"高效移动物体"和"顺应人类输入"分开处理，导致机器人要么高效但抗拒用户输入，要么配合用户却依赖持续引导。

Method: PROACT框架：利用大规模真实人类双人搬运演示数据集训练的Transformer模型，预测未来物体运动，并将其融入柔性全身控制中实现前瞻性（anticipation）控制。

Result: 在9自由度移动机械臂的108次真实试验中，相比仅柔性控制与MPC基线，平均交互功分别降低59.2%和20.4%，平均完成时间分别缩短12.9%和6.9%。

Conclusion: 在障碍约束下的协作搬运中，整合人类协作行为预测与柔性控制，可同时提升效率、减少用户负担并保持物理响应性。

Abstract: We focus on human-robot collaborative transport, a challenging task of broad relevance spanning logistics, manufacturing, and the home, in which a user and a robot work together to relocate a large or heavy object. To act as an effective partner, the robot should reduce the user's effort by contributing to efficient relocation of the object while remaining physically responsive to them. Prior work often addresses these capabilities separately, producing robots that may move the object efficiently but resist user input, or accommodate the user but depend on continuous guidance. Our key insight is that obstacle-constrained collaborative transport requires integrating predictions of human collaborative behavior with compliant robot control. To this end, we introduce PROACT, a framework for human-robot collaborative transport that incorporates anticipation into compliant whole-body control through a learned model of human collaborative behavior. Trained on a large-scale, real-world dataset of dyadic human transport demonstrations, our transformer architecture distills collaborative behavior into predictions of future object motion. Across 108 real-world trials with a 9-DoF mobile manipulator, PROACT reduces mean interaction work by 59.2\% and 20.4\%, and mean completion time by 12.9\% and 6.9\%, relative to compliance-only and MPC baselines, respectively. Footage from our experiments can be found at https://youtu.be/qAGvQfVPjbk.

</details>


### [142] [HOTICE: Whole-Body Humanoid Object Transportation in Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25363)
*Toan Nguyen,Weiduo Yuan,Siheng Zhao,Yue Wang,Daniel Seita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HOTICE：面向人形机器人杂乱环境中搬运物体的全身学习框架，通过解耦势场、双智能体RL与专家到通才蒸馏，在MuJoCo与Unitree G1上实现稳健的物体搬运与sim2real部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在真实人类环境中搬运物体是基础能力，但当机器人本体与所携载荷周边自由空间均被杂物约束时，现有方法难以应对。核心挑战在于：机器人与其搬运物体都需避障，且全身loco-manipulation动作空间巨大、杂乱场景多样导致泛化困难。

Method: 1）提出Humanoid-Object Decoupled Potential Fields（人形-物体解耦势场），联合编码机器人与所携物体的避障引导，实现二者的协同、障碍感知运动；2）针对全身loco-manipulation的大动作空间，设计双智能体强化学习架构，解耦上、下半身控制，并通过共享状态观测与奖励保持全身协调；3）采用专家到通才（specialist-to-generalist）蒸馏策略，将带特权信息的教师策略蒸馏为单一可部署的学生策略，以泛化到多样杂乱场景。

Result: 在MuJoCo仿真与真实Unitree G1人形机器人上评估，HOTICE在多种杂乱场景中实现有效且鲁棒的物体搬运，可处理不同形状物体；能可靠协调全身运动与感知物体的避障，有效泛化到未见过的杂乱环境，并在sim2real部署中取得强性能。

Conclusion: HOTICE验证了将解耦势场、双智能体RL与教师-学生蒸馏结合，可有效解决杂乱环境中人形机器人全身协同搬运难题，并具备良好的场景泛化与sim2real迁移能力。

Abstract: Object transportation is a fundamental capability for humanoid robots operating in real-world, human-centric environments, yet existing methods struggle when clutter constrains free space around both the robot and its carried payload. We present HOTICE, a whole-body humanoid learning framework for transporting objects through such cluttered environments. First, we introduce Humanoid-Object Decoupled Potential Fields, which jointly encode collision-avoidance guidance for the robot and the carried object, enabling coordinated, obstacle-aware motion for both. Second, to address the large action space inherent to whole-body loco-manipulation, we design a dual-agent reinforcement learning architecture that decouples upper- and lower-body control while preserving whole-body coordination via shared state observations and rewards. To train a policy that generalizes across diverse cluttered scenes, we further employ a specialist-to-generalist distillation strategy, in which privileged teacher policies are distilled into a single deployable student policy. We evaluate HOTICE in MuJoCo simulation and on a real Unitree G1 humanoid, demonstrating effective and robust object transportation across cluttered scenarios for objects of varying shapes. Our results show that HOTICE reliably coordinates whole-body motion and object-aware collision avoidance, generalizing effectively to previously unseen cluttered environments while achieving strong performance in sim2real deployment.

</details>


### [143] [Capability-Aware Arbitration for Semantic Intent-Based Shared Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25369)
*Zhaoda Du,Michael Bowman,Xiaoli Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出能力感知共享控制框架，用VLM推断人类意图置信度、用VLA策略在线估计执行能力置信度，经贝叶斯滤波与sigmoid映射进行非线性权限分配，任务成功率92%显著优于基线，缓解过度辅助问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共享控制多依据"推断人类意图的置信度"分配机器人权限，隐含假设自主执行可靠。当该假设不成立时，高意图置信度会导致过度辅助（over-helping），损害共享控制性能。因此需要将机器人自身执行能力纳入权限分配考量。

Method: 1) 视觉语言模型（VLM）推断人类意图并提供语义意图置信度；2) 视觉语言动作（VLA）策略生成自主动作，并基于随机动作轨迹的离散度与局部不稳定性在线估计VLA能力置信度；3) 设计非线性仲裁策略，通过sigmoid映射将经贝叶斯滤波的语义意图置信度与VLA能力置信度结合，自适应调整机器人权限。评估结合VLM/VLA置信度评估与12名被试的拾放及双向堆叠实验，涵盖分布内与分布外条件。

Result: 所提方法任务成功率最高（92%），优于手动遥操作（83%）、仅意图仲裁（44%）和固定等权融合（10%）；在控制友好度和权限加权分歧指标上也优于两个共享控制基线（更高友好度、更低分歧）。

Conclusion: 将VLA能力置信度纳入权限分配可有效缓解过度辅助并提升共享控制性能，验证了能力感知仲裁相对于意图驱动仲裁与固定权重融合的优越性。

Abstract: Shared control often allocates robot authority based on confidence in inferred human intent, assuming reliable autonomous execution. When this assumption fails, high intent confidence can cause over-helping. We present a capability-aware shared-control framework in which a vision-language model (VLM) infers human intent and provides semantic-intent confidence, while a vision-language-action (VLA) policy generates autonomous actions. VLA capability confidence is estimated online from the dispersion and local instability of stochastic action trajectories. We design a nonlinear arbitration policy that combines Bayesian-filtered semantic-intent confidence with VLA capability confidence through a sigmoid mapping to adapt robot authority. Our evaluation combined VLM/VLA confidence assessment with a study involving 12 participants performing pick-and-place and bidirectional stacking under in-distribution and out-of-distribution conditions. The proposed method achieved the highest task success rate (92%), compared with manual teleoperation (83%), intent-only arbitration (44%), and fixed equal-weight blending (10%). It also achieved higher control friendliness and lower authority-weighted disagreement than both shared-control baselines. These results demonstrate the benefit of incorporating VLA capability into authority allocation to mitigate over-helping and improve shared-control performance.

</details>


### [144] [Angular momentum analysis on Karate roundhouse kicks: a longitudinal case study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25374)
*Jan C. L. Lau,Christian Mele,Jonathan Feng-Shun Lin,Katja Mombaur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文通过为期一年的纵向研究，引入两种基于角动量的新指标，分析空手道回旋踢中无目标可收腿后腿踢的角动量管理机制，比较稳定与不稳定踢腿的差异，并考察相关变量随时间的变化，旨在为动态人体运动稳定性分析及机器人与外骨骼的平滑步态提供参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实步态不仅限于直线行走，还包含剧烈往复旋转，其内在力学机制尚不明确。空手道回旋踢可作为动态往复旋转的极端案例，研究其角动量管理有助于理解动态人体运动稳定性，并指导机器人和外骨骼实现更平滑的步态。

Method: 以第一作者为空手道学员开展一年纵向研究，另招募一名空手道教练作为专家进行单次测试以作比较。研究聚焦无目标可收腿后腿回旋踢，提出两种基于角动量的新指标，并分析角动量相关变量。

Result: 不稳定踢腿在击打前具有更高的总角动量峰值，击打后制动峰值更小。所提出的角动量指标能够识别部分不稳定踢腿的原因，但由于不稳定可能源于不同原因，稳定与不稳定踢腿之间未能形成清晰区分。

Conclusion: 所提指标可作为性能指标用于分析其他类型的动态运动；若要将其作为稳定性判据，建议同时考虑其与时间、运动学及压力中心的协调关系。

Abstract: Human gait in reality extends beyond straight-line walking, with some situations even requiring drastic back-and-forth rotations. A smooth and stable execution may seem intuitive, but the underlying mechanics remains unknown. The Karate roundhouse kick may be an extreme case of exhibiting dynamic back-and-forth rotations, but investigating the angular momentum (AM) management can potentially inform stability analysis in dynamic human motions and smoother gait in robots and exoskeletons. This paper introduces two new AM-based measures and analyzes AM-related variables to study the target-less retractable back-leg Karate roundhouse kick. The purpose is to understand the underlying AM management, analyze the differences between stable and unstable kicks, and investigate how these variables and measures change over time with improvement. A one-year longitudinal study was conducted with the first author as a Karate student, and a Karate instructor was also recruited for one session as an expert, whose data is used for comparison. Results show that unstable kicks have higher peak total AM before Strike and smaller braking peak after Strike. The proposed AM-based measures are able to identify the cause of some unstable kicks, though no clear distinction could be made between stable and unstable kicks since kicks can be unstable for different reasons. Nonetheless, the proposed measures can be applied as performance measures to analyze other types of dynamic motions. To incorporate them as stability criteria, however, it is recommended to also consider their coordination with time, kinematics, and center of pressure.

</details>


### [145] [PARTE: Plane-Assisted Robust Transformation Estimation for Point Cloud Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25375)
*Abolfazl Babanazari,Carson Cramer,Tyler Summers,Carlos Nieto,Kaveh Fathian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PARTE（Plane-Assisted Robust Transformation Estimation）全局点云配准方法，将平面结构作为补充配准证据而非需要抑制的干扰，通过Plane Context Histogram（PCH）描述子与两级匹配获取可靠平面对应，并与点对应在置信度加权兼容图中联合剔除离群点后再估计刚性变换。在6个室内外基准、8097对配准对上，PARTE总体成功率优于13种方法且运行时间低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局点云配准在重叠有限、几何重复及传感器噪声导致对应集被离群点主导时仍具挑战性。平面区域对传统点描述子尤为困难，常在匹配前被抑制或丢弃，从而损失了有价值的配准信息。

Method: PARTE提取平面块，并用新型Plane Context Histogram（PCH）描述子编码每个平面块周围的几何；采用两级匹配流程识别可靠平面对应；将候选点对应与平面对应在置信度加权兼容图中联合进行离群点剔除，随后估计刚性变换。当无可用平面对应时，方法自然退化为纯点配准。提供带Python绑定的开源C++实现。

Result: 在涵盖密集RGB-D与稀疏LiDAR的6个室内外基准上，对8097对配准对进行评估，PARTE在13种标准及最先进方法中取得最高总体成功率，同时保持较低运行时间。

Conclusion: 将平面结构作为补充配准证据而非抑制对象，并通过PCH描述子与点-面联合兼容图进行鲁棒估计，可有效提升全局点云配准在困难场景下的成功率与效率。

Abstract: Global point-cloud registration remains challenging when limited overlap, repetitive geometry, and sensor noise produce correspondence sets dominated by outliers. Planar regions are particularly difficult for conventional point descriptors and are therefore often suppressed or discarded before matching. We present PARTE (Plane-Assisted Robust Transformation Estimation), a global registration method that instead treats planar structure as complementary registration evidence. PARTE extracts planar patches and represents them using our novel Plane Context Histogram (PCH), a descriptor that encodes the geometry surrounding each patch, while a two-level matching procedure identifies reliable plane correspondences. Candidate point and plane correspondences are combined in a confidence-weighted compatibility graph for joint outlier rejection, followed by rigid transformation estimation. When no usable plane correspondences are available, PARTE naturally reduces to point-only registration. We evaluate PARTE on 8,097 registration pairs across six indoor and outdoor benchmarks spanning dense RGB-D and sparse LiDAR measurements. Evaluations show PARTE achieves the highest overall success rate against 13 standard and state-of-the-art methods while maintaining low runtime. An open-source C++ implementation with Python bindings is provided at https://parte.pages.dev.

</details>


### [146] [VLAQuantBench: Closed-Loop Evaluation of Post-Training Quantization for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25376)
*Jiuyi Xu,Qing Jin,Meida Chen,Song Wang,Yang Sui,Yangming Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VLAQuantBench基准（409次运行、94,574个仿真回合），系统研究VLA模型后训练量化中层级范围、数值格式与校准的交互作用，发现精度分配是"配方依赖"的而非通用层级敏感性规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练量化可降低视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的显存需求，但精度选择必须同时考虑层级范围、数值格式与校准三者之间的交互，而现有工作缺乏受控、可复现的系统评估。

Method: 构建VLAQuantBench受控评测：在LIBERO上测试四种模型，X-VLA额外在三个仿真基准族上评测；采用无校准W4A4 round-to-nearest量化；对比不同动作头子集层数（126→167层）、两回合校准（smoothing-and-clipping）、以及针对单个28,672参数输出投影的保护策略；结合固定观测回放、真实kernel与物理机器人测量。

Result: 无校准W4A4下，π0.5动作头子集从126层扩展到167层使成功率由7.0%升至70.5%，固定观测回放证实对应的数值恢复；两回合校准消除了所测子集中的严重联合失效，但同一平滑-裁剪配方会降低π0成功率，且无法恢复OpenVLA-OFT端到端性能；对OpenVLA-OFT，仅保护一个28,672参数输出投影即可恢复接近基线的成功率，其余441个可量化线性层在LIBERO-Long保持W3或在四个套件上使用八位激活；任务聚类区间支持上述失败与恢复的显著对比。

Conclusion: 量化精度选择取决于具体配方，存在配方依赖的交互关系；应给出具体的精度分配方案，而非套用通用的层级敏感性规则。代码、配置与回合记录已开源。

Abstract: Post-training quantization reduces the memory requirements of vision-language-action (VLA) models, but precision selection must account for the interaction between layer scope, numerical format, and calibration. We introduce \textbf{VLAQuantBench}, a controlled evaluation with 409 runs and 94,574 simulation episodes: four models on LIBERO, with X-VLA additionally evaluated on three simulation benchmark families. Under uncalibrated W4A4 round-to-nearest quantization, expanding a $π_{0.5}$ action-head subset from 126 to 167 layers raises success from 7.0\% to 70.5\%. Fixed-observation replay confirms a corresponding numerical recovery. Two-episode calibration removes the severe joint failures in the tested subsets, whereas the same smoothing-and-clipping recipe lowers $π_0$ success and does not recover OpenVLA-OFT end-to-end. For OpenVLA-OFT, protecting one 28,672-parameter output projection instead restores near-baseline success: the remaining 441 eligible linear layers retain W3 on LIBERO-Long or eight-bit activations across all four suites. Task-clustered intervals support the large failure and recovery contrasts. These results establish recipe-dependent interactions and identify concrete precision assignments, rather than universal layer-sensitivity rules. Real-kernel and physical-robot measurements complement the accuracy analysis. Code, configurations, and episode records are publicly available at https://github.com/jiuyixu25/VLAQuantBench.

</details>


### [147] [Norm2Tex: Augmenting Visuo-Tactile Simulations with Texture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25398)
*Seongjin Bien,Débora Oliveira Makowski,Roberto Calandra,Florian Walter,Wolfram Burgard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Norm2Tex：一种即插即用方法，利用法线贴图纹理为视觉触觉传感器仿真补充高频表面细节，缩小仿真与真实触觉数据的域差异，并在材质分类与强化学习任务中验证了仿真到真实的迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模数据集对通用机器人控制策略至关重要，但真实触觉数据采集成本高、耗时长，因此需要触觉仿真。然而现有触觉仿真器只捕捉整体接触几何，忽略纹理等细节，导致仿真与真实触觉数据之间存在显著的域差异（domain shift）。

Method: Norm2Tex 是一种插件式方法：在触觉仿真器渲染管线之前，修改目标物体的深度图，将法线贴图纹理中的高频表面细节注入视觉触觉传感器仿真中，从而可无缝集成到不同触觉仿真器。评估采用材质分类任务与强化学习任务来检验仿真到真实（sim-to-real）迁移。

Result: 实验表明，Norm2Tex 能在跨域条件下保留与材质相关的触觉信息，提升纹理识别性能，并在真实世界中产生依赖材质的控制行为。

Conclusion: 通过为触觉仿真补充高频纹理细节，Norm2Tex 有效缓解了仿真与真实触觉数据间的域差异，验证了其在纹理感知与基于触觉的控制策略 sim-to-real 迁移中的有效性。

Abstract: Large-scale datasets are essential for training generalist robot control policies. Collecting real-world tactile data is costly and time-consuming, motivating the use of tactile simulations. However, current tactile simulators capture only overall contact geometry and miss fine details like texture. This results in a significant domain shift between simulated and real tactile data. To address this gap, we introduce Norm2Tex, a plug-in method that augments simulations of vision-based tactile sensors with high-frequency surface details from normal map textures. By modifying the target object's depth map before a tactile simulator's rendering pipeline, Norm2Tex seamlessly integrates into different tactile simulators. We also evaluate sim-to-real transfer using material classification and a reinforcement learning task. Our results show that Norm2Tex preserves material-dependent tactile information across domains, improving texture recognition and producing material-dependent control behavior in the real world.

</details>


### [148] [Effects of Assistance Delay on Joint Mechanics and Energetics in Biological Torque Control of a Hip Exoskeleton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25417)
*Jimin An,Ryan Lee,Jingshu Peng,Eni Halilaj,Inseung Kang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 生物力矩控制（将估计的人体关节力矩直接映射为外骨骼助力）在平地行走、上坡与下坡三种生物力学差异显著的任务中均能降低净代谢率与关节正功，但助力延迟对关节做功影响显著、对净代谢率无显著影响，且延迟响应不存在任务依赖性；负功主导的下坡行走中代谢反而略升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物力矩控制是一种任务无关的助力策略，但其固定"状态-力矩"映射能否在生物力学特性迥异的任务（平地、上坡、下坡）中同样有效尚不明确；同时助力延迟如何影响不同任务下的助力效果亦缺乏系统研究。

Method: 8名受试者完成零力矩基线条件及5种主动助力条件，延迟范围40–320 ms，分别在平地行走（LG）、上坡（RA）与下坡（RD）三种任务下测试髋关节外骨骼；测定净代谢率、生物髋关节正功与下肢总正功，并比较延迟效应与任务依赖性。

Result: 跨任务与各主动延迟下，助力使净代谢率降低5.24%、生物髋关节正功降低5.86%、下肢总正功降低1.68%（均p < 0.05）。延迟显著影响两项关节做功指标（均p < 0.001），但不影响净代谢率：机械卸载随延迟增加而普遍下降，而代谢收益相对稳定。相对零力矩条件，LG净代谢率降低9.75%、RA降低7.20%，但RD升高1.23%。未检测到延迟响应的任务依赖性差异。

Conclusion: 生物力矩映射应依据目标结果与被助力关节的机械角色进行评估；以正功为主的助力策略若不加以改进，可能无法推广到负功主导的运动任务。

Abstract: Biological torque control directly maps an estimated human joint moment to exoskeleton assistance, providing a task-agnostic strategy for supporting diverse locomotor activities. However, it remains unclear whether a fixed state-to-torque mapping provides effective assistance across biomechanically distinct tasks. We examined how assistance delay affected hip exoskeleton performance during level-ground (LG), ramp-ascent (RA), and ramp-descent (RD) walking. Eight participants completed a zero-torque baseline condition and five active assistance conditions with delays ranging from 40 to 320 ms. Across tasks and active delays, assistance reduced net metabolic rate by 5.24%, positive biological hip joint work by 5.86%, and total lower-limb positive joint work by 1.68% (all p < 0.05). Assistance delay affected both joint-work outcomes (both p < 0.001) but not net metabolic rate. Mechanical unloading generally decreased with increasing delay, whereas metabolic benefits remained comparatively stable. Relative to the zero-torque condition, net metabolic rate decreased by 9.75% during LG and 7.20% during RA but increased by 1.23% during RD. We did not detect task-dependent differences in the delay response. Our findings indicate that biological torque mappings should be evaluated based on the target outcome and mechanical role of the assisted joint, and that predominantly positive-power assistance may not generalize to negative-work-dominant locomotion without modification.

</details>


### [149] [REDACT: Robust Perceptive Locomotion under Unseen Visual Corruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25450)
*Natapat Kirdwichai,Tobias Driskell-Poole,Andrei Sontea,Jadu Dash,Muhammad Burhan Hafez,Danesh Tarapore*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REDACT：一种师生框架，融合改进的视觉编码器、持续特征掩码与共识门控，在仅用干净仿真深度训练的情况下，实现对未建模损坏的鲁棒深度条件运动控制，并在真实环境中零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度条件运动策略在观测偏离训练分布时（遮挡、无效回波、传感器噪声、视觉干扰）会产生不可预测行为。现有合成传感器增广虽针对特定退化，但无法为训练中未覆盖的损坏类型定义行为，因此需要弥补训练时覆盖不足的问题。

Method: 提出REDACT（Retaining Evidence Despite Artifacts for Continued Traversal）师生框架：包含改进的视觉编码器架构、持续特征掩码（persistent feature masking），以及新的共识门控算法；门控仅基于干净观测做近似保形校准（approximate conformal calibration），无需事先知道损坏类型。训练仅使用干净仿真深度。

Result: 在未见损坏下仍能保留有用视觉信息，穿越成功率高于现有跑酷基线；跨损坏族的深度增广评估显示REDACT在增广覆盖缺失处提升鲁棒性；真实世界试验实现向结构化与森林环境的零样本迁移，且场景内容陌生。

Conclusion: 通过训练时覆盖替代需预先知道损坏类型的增广，REDACT可在未建模损坏下保持有效深度信息，并具备跨仿真到真实的泛化能力。

Abstract: Depth-conditioned locomotion policies have demonstrated impressive agile maneuvers, but can be steered to unpredictable actions when observations are outside their training distribution. Occlusion, invalid returns, sensor noise, and visual distractors can shift deployment observations away from nominal simulated depth. While synthetic sensor augmentation targets specified degradations, it does not by itself define behavior under corruption families omitted from training. To address gaps in training-time coverage, we present REDACT (Retaining Evidence Despite Artifacts for Continued Traversal), a teacher-student framework combining an improved visual encoder architecture, persistent feature masking, and a novel consensus-gating algorithm to retain useful depth information under unmodeled corruption. The gate uses approximate conformal calibration on clean observations alone, requiring no prior knowledge of the corruption type. Trained on clean simulated depth, REDACT retains useful visual information under unseen corruption, supporting higher traversal success than existing parkour baselines. Evaluation of depth augmentation across corruption families further shows that REDACT improves robustness where augmentation coverage is missing. Real-world trials demonstrate zero-shot transfer to structured and forested environments with unfamiliar scene content.

</details>


### [150] [A bioinspired internal model-based online estimator for planar pursuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25470)
*Tengyue Liu,Xincheng Li,Sofia Morales Ferreira,Kevin Galloway,Udit Halder*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仿生内部模型估计器，利用部分传感观测与已知自运动重建相对运动状态，将其建模为带相对运动学约束的优化问题，用极大值原理推导最优条件，并以移动时域方式在线求解，数值与硬件实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿生反馈控制（追逐、跟踪、集群运动）常以交互个体间的相对构型表达，但实际机载传感器往往无法直接提供反馈控制所需的全部量，因此必须估计未观测状态。

Method: 将状态重建表述为优化问题：以相对运动学作为约束，最小化内部模型输出与机载传感器测量之间的不一致；用庞特里亚金极大值原理推导必要最优性条件；采用前向-后向算法数值求解，并用移动时域（滚动时域）形式实现在线估计。

Result: 在数值实验中与经典状态估计器进行了对比评估；并在机器人硬件上通过两种追逐策略验证了所提框架的实时可行性。

Conclusion: 该仿生内部模型估计器可从部分观测中有效重建相对状态，性能优于/可比经典估计器，且可实时部署于机器人平台，为仿生反馈控制提供可行的感知估计方案。

Abstract: Bioinspired feedback controls for pursuit, tracking, and collective motion are often expressed in terms of the relative configuration between interacting agents. In practice, however, onboard sensors may not directly provide all quantities required for feedback control, necessitating estimation of unobserved quantities. This paper develops a bioinspired internal model-based estimator for reconstructing those quantities from partial sensory observations and known self-motion. State reconstruction is posed as an optimization problem that treats the relative kinematics as constraints and minimizes the disagreement between the internal model outputs and measurements from onboard sensors. Pontryagin's Maximum Principle is used to derive the necessary optimality conditions. A forward-backward algorithm is used to provide a numerical solution and a moving horizon formulation is employed for online implementation. The estimator is evaluated numerically against classical state estimators. Real-time implementation of the proposed framework on robotic hardware is demonstrated through two pursuit strategies.

</details>


### [151] [Brace Yourself: Task-Conditioned Environmental Bracing for Forceful Humanoid Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25486)
*Zongyuan Zhang,Christopher Lehnert,Will N. Browne,Jonathan M. Roberts*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出支撑手策略SHS，使类人机器人单手撑环境、另手施力，无需人类动作数据或在线全身轨迹规划，将可用接触力从13.5N提升至60N并改善力跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类人机器人在执行施力操作时，交互力会干扰全身平衡，导致可施加的力受限，因此需要一种机制在施力时维持平衡。

Method: 提出支撑手策略（SHS）：机器人一手撑住环境提供支撑，另一手执行施力操作；优化任务条件下的支撑构型，用以引导两个同步的强化学习策略；不使用人类运动数据，也不进行在线全身轨迹规划。在Unitree G1上验证。

Result: 可用接触力最高达60N，而无环境支撑时最大持续力为13.5N；相比任务无关的支撑构型，力跟踪性能显著提升；同一策略无需重新训练即可泛化到不同任务区域。

Conclusion: SHS是一种简单机制，可显著扩展类人机器人的施力操作能力。

Abstract: Forceful manipulation is challenging for humanoid robots because interaction forces can disturb whole-body balance. We introduce the Supporting Hand Strategy (SHS), which enables a humanoid to brace against the environment with one hand while performing forceful manipulation with the other. SHS optimises a task-conditioned support configuration that guides two synchronous reinforcement-learning policies, without human motion data or online whole-body trajectory planning. On a Unitree G1, SHS achieved usable contact forces up to 60 N, compared with a maximum sustained force of 13.5 N without environmental bracing, while substantially improving force tracking over a task-independent support configuration. The same policies generalised to different task regions without retraining. SHS therefore provides a simple mechanism for substantially extending humanoid forceful-manipulation capability.

</details>


### [152] [RoboMP-DINOv2: Prompts, Not Filters for Robust Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25506)
*Han Qi,Heng Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboMP-DINOv2，将分割掩码作为空间提示而非可见性过滤器，结合 DINOv2 密集特征实现全场景视觉编码，并引入掩码区域颜色随机化（MCR）提升外观鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作策略需要在视觉变化下泛化，同时保留与动作相关的场景上下文。通用视觉编码器未针对视觉运动控制定制，而物体中心方法常用分割掩码作为硬过滤器，丢弃了潜在有用的上下文信息。

Method: 从完整观测中提取密集 DINOv2 特征；在掩码位置注入学习到的区域特定嵌入；对提示与非提示 token 联合上下文化以进行动作预测；并采用掩码区域颜色随机化（MCR）增强外观鲁棒性，得到 RoboMP-DINOv2-MCR。

Result: 在七种模拟操作设置中，空间偏移下成功率 60.7%、场景杂乱下 59.7%，而基于 DINOv2 的 Diffusion Policy 分别为 50.7% 和 41.0%。在未见物体颜色下，RoboMP-DINOv2-MCR 达 72.5%，最强颜色随机化基线仅 35.1%。

Conclusion: 将掩码视为空间提示而非可见性过滤器，可在提升视觉鲁棒性的同时保留行为相关的场景信息；额外实验与表征分析验证了该设计的有效性。

Abstract: Robot manipulation policies must generalize across visual shifts while preserving scene context relevant to action. General-purpose vision encoders are not tailored to visuomotor control, while object-centric approaches often use segmentation masks as hard filters that discard potentially useful context. We propose RoboMP-DINOv2 (Robotics Mask-Prompted DINOv2), a full-scene vision encoder that treats masks as spatial prompts rather than visibility filters. It extracts dense DINOv2 features from the full observation, injects learned region-specific embeddings at masked locations, and jointly contextualizes prompted and unprompted tokens for action prediction. We further introduce masked-region color randomization (MCR) to improve appearance robustness, yielding RoboMP-DINOv2-MCR. Across seven simulated manipulation settings, RoboMP-DINOv2 achieves 60.7% success under spatial shifts and 59.7% under scene clutter, compared with 50.7% and 41.0% for a DINOv2-based Diffusion Policy. Under unseen object colors, RoboMP-DINOv2-MCR achieves 72.5% success versus 35.1% for the strongest color-randomized baseline. Additional experiments and representation analyses show improved robustness while preserving behaviorally relevant scene information. Code is available at https://github.com/han20192019/RoboMP_DINOv2.

</details>


### [153] [A Deployment Study of Identity-Gated Drone Gesture Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25511)
*Diyari Mohammed Salih,Ilyes Chaabeni,Naima Ait Oufroukh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 IGate，一种身份门控的无人机手势控制栈，仅当验证到已注册操作员时才接受手势指令；系统用20帧人脸少样本注册，在线人脸验证+跟踪，手势由RBF-SVM分类手部关键点，并以分层有限状态机管理模式；在DJI Tello EDU上完成离线与飞行测试（270次试验，149次实飞）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视觉的手势控制会接受视野内任意手部的指令，在共享室内空间中存在安全隐患，因此需要引入身份验证机制，仅允许已注册操作员控制设备。

Method: 构建 IGate 身份门控控制栈：1）用20帧初始人脸帧做少样本注册，无需用户特定预训练；验证时将当前人脸裁剪的嵌入与注册模板做余弦相似度比较，人脸跟踪采用比例校正；2）手势控制通过RBF-SVM对提取的手部关键点进行分类，模型在自建数据集上训练；3）用分层有限状态机处理模式选择、默认与回退行为。在 DJI Tello EDU 上对各组件进行离线与实飞评估。

Result: 共270次试验（149次实飞）。人脸验证离线等错误率0.32%，实飞时升至19.3%。在悬停锁定条件下，RBF-SVM手势分类器优于几何规则（准确率0.850 vs. 0.651），其中82%的性能差距来自深度通道。所有日志与复现脚本将公开。

Conclusion: 身份门控可有效将手势控制限制在已注册操作员范围内；少样本人脸验证在离线条件下表现优异，但实飞中受运动/姿态变化影响明显退化。RBF-SVM手部关键点分类显著优于手工几何规则，且深度信息是主要增益来源。

Abstract: Vision-based gesture control accepts commands from any hand in the camera field of view, which is unsafe in shared indoor spaces. This paper presents IGate, an identity-gated control stack that includes gesture control and face tracking, in which commands are admitted only when an enrolled operator is verified. The system performs few-shot user enrolment from 20 initial face frames, without prior user-specific training: verification compares an embedding of the current face crop against the enrolled template by cosine similarity, while face tracking uses proportional correction. Gesture control is achieved by classifying extracted hand landmarks using an RBF-SVM trained on a custom dataset. Additionally, a hierarchical finite-state machine handles mode selection, default, and fallback behaviours. The approach is tested on a DJI Tello EDU, each component evaluated offline and in-flight across 270 trials (149 flown). Face verification yields a 0.32% offline equal error rate versus 19.3% in-flight. Under hover-locked conditions, the RBF-SVM gesture classifier outperforms the geometric rule (0.850 vs. 0.651 accuracy), with 82% of this gap stemming from the depth channel. All logs and reproduction scripts will be released.

</details>


### [154] [Digital Twin-Driven VR Teleoperation with Multi-View Spatial Perception for Surgical Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25527)
*Chang Liu,Chenhao Yu,Honghao Zhao,Hao Ding,Haochen Wei,Adnan Munawar,Mathias Unberath,Peter Kazanzides*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于数字孪生的VR遥操作平台，用无标记感知重建手术环境并在HMD中多视角渲染，配合VR手柄控制，在dVRK用户研究中显著优于HoloLens 2混合现实基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RMIS平台采用固定控制台，仅提供单一内窥镜视角，深度感知受限；已有的HMD便携方案依赖视频透视，同样受限于单视角，且多数系统采用手势跟踪导致控制鲁棒性与稳定性不足。

Method: 构建数字孪生驱动的VR遥操作平台：通过无标记感知生成手术环境数字孪生并流式传输至HMD进行显示，提供多视角渲染与自然运动视差线索，解耦手部姿态与器械严格对齐；采用VR手柄替代手势跟踪以扩大遥操作工作空间并提升控制鲁棒性与稳定性；在dVRK上进行15人用户研究，与HoloLens 2混合现实基线对比。

Result: 相比最先进的HoloLens 2混合现实基线，路径长度减少86%，抖动（jerk）减少95%；在所有条件下深度感知置信度与或优于传统固定控制台。

Conclusion: 数字孪生VR平台可克服视频透视的单视角与深度感知局限，配合手柄控制实现更稳定、高效的遥操作，具备替代传统控制台的潜力。

Abstract: Current robot-assisted minimally-invasive surgery (RMIS) platforms provide a fixed console for the surgeon to view stereo endoscopic images and teleoperate instruments inside the patient. Several researchers have proposed the use of a head-mounted display (HMD) as a portable console, with video pass-through rendering of the endoscope images which, like the fixed console, restricts the operator to a single endoscopic viewpoint and limits depth perception. We present a digital twin-driven virtual reality (VR) teleoperation platform, where the digital twin is created from markerless perception of the surgical environment and streamed for display on the HMD. This overcomes the limitations of video pass-through by providing multi-view rendering and natural motion-parallax cues, enabling decoupling of the user's hand posture from strict instrument alignment. The system utilizes VR hand controllers to increase the teleoperation workspace and to improve the robustness and stability of instrument control compared to the hand tracking approach adopted by most prior systems. A 15-participant user study on the da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK) shows that our VR platform significantly outperforms a state-of-the-art HoloLens 2 mixed reality baseline, reducing path length by 86% and jerk by 95%, while achieving depth perception confidence comparable to or exceeding the traditional console across all conditions.

</details>


### [155] [HABILIS Brain 0: Geometry-Change Supervision for Vision-Language-Action and Residual Flow Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25558)
*Jinu Pahk,Jesoon Kang,Taegeon Park,Jisu An,Soo Min Kimm,Jaejoon Kim,Byoung-Tak Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GC-VLA，通过预测多视角未来-当前几何变化令牌进行几何监督，四阶段训练并在LIBERO上达95.20%，结合GCRF后达99.55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作策略虽受益于几何监督，但仅用当前帧几何无法显式刻画操作过程中的变化；动机是学习一个与本体无关的视觉接口，可先在机器人与第一视角视频上预训练，再对齐机器人特定动作。

Method: GC-VLA从当前观测预测多视角未来-当前几何变化令牌；离线帧对定义名义0.5秒预测时域，未来观测仅用于构造训练目标。Stage 1训练几何变化视觉语言模型GC-VLM；Stage 2引入连续ActionExpert，对齐机器人动作并在VLM接口处停止动作流梯度；Stage 3允许这些梯度与ActionExpert联合更新可训练VLM组件；Stage 4冻结GC-VLA并应用几何条件残差流GCRF，采用二元干预路由器与从闭环反馈学习的有界残差速度策略。

Result: GC-VLA在LIBERO上成功率95.20%，GC-VLA结合GCRF达99.55%；推理仅用当前观测与学到的GC表示，无需执行离线目标编码器。

Conclusion: 几何变化监督为VLA提供显式动态几何线索，分阶段训练可有效对齐动作，闭环残差策略进一步提升成功率，且推理高效。

Abstract: Vision-language-action policies benefit from geometric supervision, but current-frame geometry alone does not explicitly describe the changes associated with manipulation. This design is motivated by the goal of learning an embodiment-agnostic visual interface that can be pretrained across robot and egocentric video before robot-specific action alignment. We introduce Geometry-Change VLA (GC-VLA), which learns to predict multiview future-current geometry-change tokens from current observations. Offline frame pairs define a nominal 0.5-second prediction horizon; future observations are used only to construct training targets. Stage 1 trains a geometry-change vision-language model (GC-VLM). Stage 2 introduces a continuous ActionExpert and aligns it with robot actions while stopping action-flow gradients at the VLM interface. Stage 3 enables these gradients to update the trainable VLM components jointly with the ActionExpert. Stage 4 freezes GC-VLA and applies Geometry-Conditioned Residual Flow (GCRF), using a binary intervention router and a single bounded residual velocity policy learned from closed-loop feedback. GC-VLA achieves 95.20% success on LIBERO, and GC-VLA with GCRF achieves 99.55%. Inference uses current observations and the learned GC representation without executing the offline target encoders.

</details>


### [156] [IndustrialVLA-Bench: A Traceable Multi-Axis Evaluation of Open Robot Policy Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25562)
*Yiqi Wang,Zhifeng Rao,Jiaqi Zhang,Xiaoyang Li,Zhangkai Wu,Yiqun Duan,Mingkai Zheng,Fei Wang,Shan You,Taotao Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 IndustrialVLA-Bench，一个证据感知的统一评测框架，在相同报告规范下比较 6 个 VLA 与 WAM 系统，分开评估干净能力、非语言鲁棒性、指令敏感性与执行成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA（视觉-语言-动作模型）与 WAM（世界-动作模型）面向相同操作任务，却常在不同评测协议下汇报，导致两者在能力、鲁棒性、语言敏感性和部署成本上的权衡不清晰，缺乏可比较、可追溯的证据。

Method: 构建 IndustrialVLA-Bench，对 6 个已发布系统采用统一报告模式，分四项评测：LIBERO 上的干净能力、LIBERO-Plus 上的非语言鲁棒性、LIBERO-Para 上的指令敏感性，以及观测到的执行成本。任务分数为固定 checkpoint 与推理配置下、三个不同随机种子的完整评测聚合；并按证据等级区分 protocol-faithful、near-reproduction 与 pending-verification。

Result: 六个系统的干净 LIBERO 平均分仅相差 1.58 分，而鲁棒性与同义改写汇总分差分别达 14.62 分和 31.08 分；仅限三个 protocol-faithful 系统比较时，对应差值仍为 1.36、14.62 和 23.10 分，说明该诊断性差异不依赖较弱证据层级。同时报告推理延迟、峰值内存、运行模式与各系统证据状态。

Conclusion: 论文不主张任一范式的普遍优越性，而是通过 IndustrialVLA-Bench 提供可追溯证据，在共享实用标准下比较已发布机器人策略；仅 protocol-faithful 条目支持严格比较。代码与评测记录已开源。

Abstract: Open robot policies increasingly follow two paradigms: vision-language-action models (VLAs) directly map observations and instructions to actions, whereas world-action models (WAMs) incorporate learned video or world dynamics into policy learning or action generation. Although both target the same manipulation tasks and represent alternative design choices, they are commonly reported under different evaluation protocols, leaving their capability, robustness, language sensitivity, and deployment-cost trade-offs unclear. We present IndustrialVLA-Bench, an evidence-aware evaluation of six released VLA and WAM systems under a unified reporting schema. It separately evaluates clean capability on LIBERO, non-language robustness on LIBERO-Plus, instruction sensitivity on LIBERO-Para, and observed execution cost. Reported task scores aggregate three complete evaluations with distinct random seeds under a fixed checkpoint and inference configuration. Across all six systems, clean LIBERO averages differ by only 1.58 points, whereas robustness and paraphrase summaries span 14.62 and 31.08 points. Restricting every comparison to the three protocol-faithful systems preserves the effect (1.36, 14.62 and 23.10 points), so the diagnostic separation reported here does not depend on the weaker evidence tiers. We additionally report observed inference latency, peak memory, runtime mode, and an evidence status for every system. Protocol-faithful, near-reproduction, and pending-verification entries remain visibly separated; only protocol-faithful entries support strict comparisons. Rather than claiming universal superiority of either paradigm, IndustrialVLA-Bench provides traceable evidence for comparing released robot policies on shared practical criteria. Code and evaluation records are available at https://github.com/xiaoqi-7/IndustrialVLA-Bench.

</details>


### [157] [Recording Hand-Held Laparoscopic Instrument Motion in the Operating Room: Magnetometer-Free Fusion of Inertial, Range and Visual Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25577)
*Jiyul Lee,Dongho Yee,Juahn Oh,Jinseok Lee,Yechan Seo,Seong Jeong,Minsung Kim,Seonho Shim,Younghoon Noh,Hyuk Choi,Youngbin Kong,Hyoun-Joong Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种可夹装于常规腹腔镜器械上的器械状态记录器，在套管针远端运动中心约束下，用误差状态卡尔曼滤波融合六轴IMU、ToF测距仪与无标记相机；以U-Net分割器械轴轮廓获取航向角替代磁力计。相对Franka Research 3机械臂，平移误差1.21mm RMS、相对旋转误差0.34° RMS，航向角误差0.200°（加速度计+磁力计为3.58°），连续轨迹绝对尖端误差1.22mm（编程）/3.04mm（遥操作）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微创手术多使用手持腹腔镜器械，但目前仅保留内窥镜视频，体现手术技能、可用于技能评估与机器人学习的器械运动信息被丢弃。仅凭视频估计位姿误差达毫米至厘米级；器械上安装的IMU又无法依赖磁力计，因为实测中磁场随器械位姿和不同场次而变化。

Method: 设计可夹装于常规器械（不改造进入患者体内的部分）的器械状态记录器，融合六轴IMU、飞行时间（ToF）测距仪与无标记相机；在套管针远端运动中心（RCM）约束下采用误差状态卡尔曼滤波；用U-Net分割轴杆轮廓获得航向，替代磁力计。

Result: 相对Franka Research 3机械臂且未与其对齐：300次平移试验位移误差1.21mm RMS，180次旋转试验相对旋转误差0.34° RMS；航向角误差0.200°，而仅用加速度计+磁力计为3.58°。连续轨迹实时跟踪显示，经三个滤波参数事后调优后，绝对尖端误差为1.22mm（编程轨迹）与3.04mm（遥操作），完整融合优于任何传感器子集。

Conclusion: 由于估计器不使用磁测量，其精度不依赖未受干扰的磁场。该夹装设备可在常规手持腹腔镜手术中记录度量级尖端轨迹，并显示器械进入患者体内后被遮挡的插入深度与姿态。

Abstract: Most minimally invasive procedures are still performed with hand-held laparoscopic instruments, yet only the endoscopic video is retained; the instrument motion that expresses surgical skill, and that could support skill assessment and robot learning, is lost. Pose from video alone remains millimeters to centimeters off, and an instrument-mounted inertial measurement unit (IMU) cannot rely on its magnetometer, whose field changed with tool pose and between sessions in our measurements. We present a surgical instrument-state logger that clips onto a conventional instrument without modifying the part that enters the patient and fuses a six-axis IMU and a time-of-flight (ToF) rangefinder with a markerless camera in an error-state Kalman filter under the remote center of motion (RCM) of the trocar. Heading comes from the shaft silhouette, segmented by a U-Net, in place of the magnetometer: the rotation-angle error is 0.200°, against 3.58° from the accelerometer and magnetometer alone. Against a Franka Research 3 manipulator, and without alignment to it, the displacement error over 300 translation trials was 1.21mm RMS and the relative-rotation error over 180 rotation trials 0.34° RMS. On continuous trajectories, tracked and displayed in real time, the absolute tip error was 1.22mm (programmed) and 3.04mm (teleoperated) after post-hoc tuning of three filter parameters, and the full fusion beat every sensor subset. Because the estimator uses no magnetic measurement, its accuracy does not rely on an undisturbed field. The same clip-on device could thus record metric tip trajectories during routine hand-held laparoscopy, while displaying the insertion depth and attitude that are hidden once the instrument is inside the patient.

</details>


### [158] [CableVLA: Simulation-Privileged Global-Local Representation Learning for Cable Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25606)
*Zhifei Teng,Bo Feng,Xiang Zou,Jinpeng Xiao,Min Li,Zhouping Yin,Yiqun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CableVLA：端到端多模态视觉-语言-动作框架，将仿真特权监督转化为可部署的线缆拓扑与触觉表示，在345次MuJoCo评估中把成功率从62.6%提升到84.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线缆布线需要全局线缆拓扑与局部接触变化的协同控制，现有方法难以同时获取可部署的拓扑表征与触觉接触动态。

Method: 提出CableVLA框架，包含TopoHead（将节点级物理及当前/未来线缆拓扑信息蒸馏为因果视觉上下文供动作专家使用）与TacSense（帧分支与taxel分支互补，从电阻阵列学习接触动态，仿真器提供的运动学与接触事件作为超出实测力图的监督）；接触门控激活力触觉残差，对冻结的拓扑条件策略的下一8步手臂与夹爪动作进行细化。

Result: 345次MuJoCo评估中成功率由π0.5-V视觉基线的62.6%提升至84.9%；TacSense在相近参数量下相较CNN-LSTM基线在滑动过渡识别上显著提升，且在冻结编码器探针下优势保持；另通过拓扑预测、57项触觉评估、策略适应、跨仿真器与真机对比评估表征质量与零样本迁移。

Conclusion: 仿真特权监督可有效蒸馏为可部署的拓扑与触觉表示，接触门控的力触觉残差能提升拓扑条件策略的下游控制性能。

Abstract: Cable routing requires coordinated control of global cable topology and changing local contacts. We present CableVLA, an end-to-end multimodal vision-language-action framework that converts simulation-privileged supervision into deployable cable-topology and tactile representations. TopoHead distills node-level physics and current and future cable-topology information into causal visual context for the action expert. TacSense uses complementary frame and taxel branches to learn contact dynamics from resistive arrays, with simulator-derived kinematics and contact events providing supervision beyond the measured force map. A contact gate activates force-tactile residuals that refine the next 8 arm-and-gripper actions of a frozen topology-conditioned policy. Across 345 MuJoCo evaluations, CableVLA improves success from 62.6% for the $π_{0.5}$-V visual baseline to 84.9%. TacSense achieves pronounced gains in slip-transition recognition over a CNN-LSTM baseline with a similar parameter count, and this advantage persists under frozen-encoder probes. Topology prediction and 57-task tactile evaluations assess representation quality, while policy adaptation studies evaluate downstream control performance. Cross-simulator and real-robot comparisons further examine zero-shot policy transfer under changes in dynamics and sensing.

</details>


### [159] [A Deployable Four-Finger Payload for Teleoperated Free-Flying Manipulation with Astrobee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25614)
*William Su,Jordan Kam,Yunosuke Nakamura,Yixiao Wang,Jianshu Zhou,Masayoshi Tomizuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文面向舱内自由飞行器，提出双臂遥操作流程与可展开四指抓取载荷的概念设计，并在零重力ISS仿真中完成刚性货包的十次搬运试验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来低地球轨道栖息地需要在有人与无人阶段执行货物搬运、维护等日常任务，需要可部署的抓取与遥操作系统。

Method: 抓取载荷通过线性导轨上的四个独立驱动手指提供17个操作自由度；VR界面将地面操作者手部动作映射到手指对、手指间距映射到导轨、手腕运动映射到Astrobee平移。在基于MuJoCo定制的零重力ISS仿真器中开展遥操作。

Result: 完成十次重复的刚性ISS货物转运袋搬运试验，测量任务成功率、连续接触保持、完成时间与货物运动。

Conclusion: 初步验证了该双臂遥操作流程与四指抓取载荷设计在零重力舱内货物搬运任务中的可行性。

Abstract: This article presents a bimanual teleoperation pipeline and conceptual design of a deployable four-finger payload for intra-vehicular free-flyers. Future habitats in low-Earth orbit (LEO) will require systems to perform mundane tasks like cargo handling and maintenance during crewed and uncrewed periods. The gripper payload provides 17 manipulation degrees-of-freedom (DoF) through four independently actuated fingers on a linear rail system. To control it, a virtual reality (VR) device interface maps the human ground operator's hand motions to the finger pairs, their separation to the rail, and common wrist motion to Astrobee translation. We present the preliminary results of teleoperating Astrobee in a custom zero-gravity MuJoCo-based International Space Station (ISS) simulator through ten repeated trials of transporting a rigid ISS Cargo Transfer Bag (CTB). We measure task success, continuous contact retention, completion time, and cargo motion.

</details>


### [160] [Relative Contact Velocity-Controlled Hand-Object Mechanism for Dexterous Tool Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25619)
*Sunyu Wang,Jean Oh,Nancy S. Pollard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出手–物统一机构（HOM）建模，实现多指手完整工具操作流程的运动规划与接触估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦抓取或单一操作阶段，缺乏让通用多指手完成"拾取—装载—挥动"完整工具操作流程的统一方法；需建立与手部运动学和几何无关的普适建模框架。

Method: 受人类工具操作与机械设计原理启发，将手、物体及广义接触坐标系建模为统一的手–物机构（HOM），其子装配体定义为手指间的相对接触速度与接触力约束；据此构建基于最小二乘与互补滤波器的轻量、物理可解释的运动规划与接触估计框架。

Result: 在仿真中通过遥操作对五种不同机械手进行评估，五只手均完成完整工具操作流程，并能从相同、简单的参考轨迹生成灵巧行为；框架在手、工具和任务间表现出良好适应性。

Conclusion: HOM 的运动学与几何基础赋予框架跨手、跨工具、跨任务的通用性，为多指手完整工具操作提供了统一且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: This work investigates how to enable general multi-finger robotic hands to perform the complete tool manipulation process, which entails picking up a tool, loading it into a suitable pose, and then wielding it. Inspired by human tool manipulation and mechanical design principles, we model the hand and the tool as a unified hand-object mechanism (HOM) composed of sub-assemblies. Specifically, we define a HOM as consisting of the hand, the object, and the generalized contact frames, allowing the HOM's motions to be expressed with the same set of Cartesian-space relative contact velocities, irrespective of the hand's kinematics and geometry. Then, we define a HOM's sub-assemblies as relative contact velocity and contact force constraints between fingers. Building on these definitions, we developed a lightweight and physically interpretable motion planning and contact estimation framework using least squares and a complementary filter. We evaluated our framework in simulation by teleoperating five different robotic hands. The results show that our framework enabled all five hands to execute the complete tool manipulation process, achieving dexterous behaviors even from identical, simple reference trajectories. Furthermore, the results showcase our framework's adaptability to different hands, tools, and tasks, enabled by its kinematic and geometric foundation.

</details>


### [161] [From Instrument-Mounted Demonstrations to In-Vivo Execution: Learning Bimanual Laparoscopic Appendectomy Without Robot-Collected Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25625)
*Dongho Yee,Juahn Oh,Jinseok Lee,Jiyul Lee,Yechan Seo,Seong Jeong,Minsung Kim,Seonho Shim,Younghoon Noh,Hyuk Choi,Youngbin Kong,Kyu Eun Lee,Hyoun-Joong Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一套端到端流程，将外科医生手持腹腔镜器械的运动记录下来，用于训练手术机器人策略，并在活体兔上完成离体与在体验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微创手术多由手持腹腔镜器械完成，术者器械运动学信息在手术结束后丢失，仅保留内窥镜视频；缺乏机器人示教数据成为手术机器人学习的主要瓶颈。

Method: 1) 器械状态记录器：安装于标准腹腔镜器械杆上，仅用惯性、飞行时间与霍尔传感器恢复位姿与钳口状态，无需外部相机或跟踪器；2) 数据管线：以机器人为真值测量各传感器通道延迟并做时间对齐，生成观测-动作对；3) 策略学习：以微调 DINOv3 为骨干的扩散策略，先在由离体兔阑尾深度图重建的物理仿真器中通过闭环 rollout 选型，再用 4 只活体兔的 849 条在体示教重训；4) 部署：在另外 4 只活体兔上带电外科操作执行，由外科医生选择手术阶段。

Result: 在 4 只活体兔中，3 只由策略完成了阑尾切除术；验证了仅凭术者自身器械记录的示教即可训练、选型并部署双臂手术策略。机器人仅作为传感器标定的时间基准与执行器，不采集任何示教数据。

Conclusion: 术者自持器械的运动采集可替代机器人示教，为手术机器人学习提供可行数据来源；两套示教数据集已公开以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Most minimally invasive surgery is still performed with hand-held laparoscopic instruments, and the surgeon's instrument kinematics are lost when the operation ends; only the endoscope video is kept. This paper presents an end-to-end pipeline that captures this motion in the operating room and uses it to train a surgical robot policy, validated on live animals. We introduce a surgical instrument-state logger that mounts on the shaft of a standard laparoscopic instrument and recovers its pose and jaw state from an inertial sensor, a time-of-flight sensor and a Hall sensor, with no external camera or tracker. A data pipeline measures the latency of every sensor channel against a robot ground truth and aligns the channels before forming observation-action pairs. On these demonstrations we train a diffusion policy with a fine-tuned DINOv3 backbone, selecting its design by closed-loop rollouts in a physics simulator reconstructed from depth maps of an ex-vivo rabbit appendix. The policy is then retrained on 849 in-vivo demonstrations from four live rabbits and deployed on four additional live rabbits with electrosurgery armed. With the surgeon selecting the surgical phase, the policy completed the appendectomy in three of the four animals. The results show that demonstrations recorded from a surgeon's own instruments are sufficient to train, select and deploy a bimanual surgical policy in vivo. The robot serves only as the timing reference for sensor calibration and as the executor, and collects no demonstrations. Both demonstration corpora are released to support future surgical robot learning research.

</details>


### [162] [MachEmbodied-U0: Unified Understanding and Generation Model for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25627)
*Haoran Wen,Wenfu Wang,Kunsong Shi,Jingke Wang,Wancheng Feng,Yiren Zhang,Yueran Zhao,Xuancheng Zhang,Nanfei Ye,Xingru Chen,Zhaohong Sun,Chengmin Yang,Zikang Yu,Penghao Bi,Jia Shi,Yu Liu,Kun Zhan,Yan Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出统一具身基础模型ME-U0，通过Mixture-of-Transformers连接理解与生成专家，利用子任务预测与可供性定位引导视觉动态与动作联合生成，在仿真与真实操控任务中取得优异性能并具备零样本能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人控制需模型同时理解任务意图、定位交互位置、刻画场景演化并生成精确动作。现有视觉-语言-动作模型语义先验强但缺乏显式场景动态建模；世界-动作模型虽耦合视觉预测与控制，却未显式暴露细粒度操作所需的语义与空间结构。

Method: ME-U0采用Mixture-of-Transformers架构连接理解与生成专家；以子任务预测和可供性定位引导基于流匹配的视觉动态与动作联合生成；视觉动态涵盖未来RGB、深度、表面法线与光流，为外观、几何与运动提供互补监督；并提出多速率旋转位置编码（MRPE）对齐视觉动态与细粒度控制。预训练数据约4,200小时机器人及第一视角演示数据。

Result: 仅用各下游基准原生监督，在RoboDojo仿真基准平均得分17.66；LIBERO与LIBERO-Plus平均成功率分别为99.0%与82.5%；真实机器人操作任务验证有效；无下游监督时展现零样本子任务预测、可供性定位与视觉动态能力。

Conclusion: ME-U0在仿真与真实世界中兼具有竞争力的下游控制性能与可迁移的任务定位及视觉动态能力，实现理解与生成的统一。

Abstract: General-purpose robot control requires models to understand task intent, identify where to interact, capture how the scene evolves, and generate precise actions. Vision-language-action models provide strong semantic priors but typically do not explicitly model scene dynamics, while world-action models couple visual prediction with control without necessarily exposing the task-relevant semantic and spatial structure needed for fine-grained manipulation. We present MachEmbodied-U0 (ME-U0), a unified embodied foundation model connecting understanding and generation experts through a Mixture-of-Transformers architecture. Subtask prediction and affordance grounding guide joint visual-dynamics and action generation via flow matching. Visual dynamics encompass future RGB, depth, surface normals, and optical flow, providing complementary supervision for appearance, geometry, and motion. Multi-rate Rotary Position Encoding (MRPE) aligns visual dynamics with fine-grained control. We pretrain ME-U0 on approximately 4,200 hours of curated demonstrations from robotic datasets and egocentric datasets. Using only the supervision natively available in each downstream benchmark, ME-U0 achieves an average score of 17.66 on the RoboDojo simulation benchmark and average success rates of 99.0\% and 82.5\% on LIBERO and LIBERO-Plus, respectively. We additionally validate ME-U0 on real-world robotic manipulation tasks, demonstrating its effectiveness beyond simulation. Without corresponding downstream supervision, ME-U0 further demonstrates zero-shot subtask prediction, affordance grounding, and visual dynamics on simulated and real-world observations. Overall, ME-U0 combines competitive downstream control performance with transferable task-grounding and visual-dynamics capabilities across simulation and the real world.

</details>


### [163] [PAKT: Physically-Aligned Kinesthetic Teaching for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25630)
*Lars Johannsmeier,Yashraj Narang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PAKT是一个用于强化学习中的动觉示教框架，通过导纳控制与参考生成器保证采集轨迹符合机器人运动学限制，并配合高频控制栈，在插装与工业装配基准上相比HIL-SERL基线减少23%-48%循环时间、62%-86%累计干预次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界强化学习系统在接触密集型工业操作中仍难以满足微米级精度、99%以上成功率和人类水平循环时间的要求。虽然离策略算法可通过示范和干预提升性能，但关键瓶颈在于缺乏直观的示教接口，使其既能便捷采集人类引导，又能遵守物理系统与策略的约束条件。

Method: PAKT采用动觉示教（而非遥操作）作为交互方式：操作者通过导纳控制施加人力，将人类作用力映射为机器人运动；下游的参考生成器施加与策略执行时相同的运动学限制（如速度、加速度、加加速度），从而保证采集到的轨迹在可复现范围内。为支撑该示教接口，PAKT增加高性能控制栈，将低频RL动作映射为高频力矩指令，由参考生成器与随后的阻抗控制器组成，在保持阻抗控制器跟踪性能的同时改善接触处理并产生更平滑的策略动作。

Result: 在四个插装与工业装配基准（含数据中心计算托盘任务）的已报告运行中，端到端系统相比HIL-SERL基线将循环时间缩短23%-48%，累计干预次数减少62%-86%。

Conclusion: PAKT通过结合工业界广泛使用的动觉示教、符合运动学约束的参考生成以及高频控制栈，为真实世界接触密集型工业操作提供了一种直观且可执行的强化学习示教与执行方案，显著提升了效率并降低了人工干预需求。

Abstract: Real-world reinforcement learning (RL) systems still struggle with the demands of contact-rich industrial manipulation, including micrometer-level precision, success rates above 99%, and human-level cycle times. Although off-policy algorithms can improve performance by leveraging demonstrations and interventions, a key bottleneck is the lack of an intuitive interface for collecting such guidance while complying with constraints of the physical system and the policy. We propose PAKT, a framework for kinesthetic teaching in RL. As opposed to teleoperation approaches, PAKT relies on kinesthetic guidance, which is widely used in industry. However, a critical weakness of kinesthetic guidance is the possibility for the operator to move the robot along trajectories (e.g., velocities, accelerations, jerk) that the robot and/or policy cannot physically reproduce. Using PAKT, operators guide the robot through admittance control, which maps human-applied forces to motion. The downstream reference generator applies the same kinematic limits used during policy execution, keeping the collected trajectories within these limits. To support this teaching interface with an appropriate execution layer, PAKT adds a high-performance control stack that maps low-frequency RL actions to high-frequency torque commands. It consists of a reference generator and subsequent impedance controller, where the reference generator preserves the tracking performance of the impedance controller while improving contact handling and producing smoother policy actions. Across the reported runs on four insertion and industrial assembly benchmarks, including a data center compute tray, the end-to-end system reduces cycle time by 23%-48% and cumulative intervention count by 62%-86% relative to the HIL-SERL baseline. Project website: https://pakt-website.github.io/pakt-website}{https://pakt-website.github.io/pakt-website

</details>


### [164] [DynaForge: Planning-Guided Residual Learning for Dynamic Manipulation Demonstration Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25631)
*Yiyang Jin,Yu Zheng,Xiao He,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DynaForge：一种规划引导的动态操作演示生成框架，通过学习残差修正并结合隐式课程，将动态任务演示生成成功率从41.30%提升至78.37%，训练出的策略在仿真与真实任务中显著优于DOMINO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态物体操作对真实环境中的机器人至关重要，但高质量演示生成方法有限。面向静态任务的方法难以迁移到动态场景；规划类方法在接触附近易失败；DOMINO式回放简化了动态交互，限制了策略学习所需的经验。

Method: DynaForge采用规划引导框架学习动态操作的残差修正。它按任务阶段结合低频全局规划与高频以物体为中心的逆运动学，并施加残差策略以在动态交互中修正动作。此外，引入隐式课程：将条件匹配的rollout分组，并选择混合成功组，使残差强化学习聚焦于不断变化的胜任力前沿。

Result: 在Can和Bottle任务上，DynaForge在相同标称环境步数预算下使用0.73倍于vanilla GRPO的优化器步数，并取得更高的最终成功率。在九个仿真任务上，平均演示生成成功率从规划先验的41.30%提升至78.37%。每任务800条演示时，用DynaForge数据训练的DP3策略平均成功率为49.11%，而DOMINO数据仅为7.07%。在三个真实动态任务上，DynaForge训练的策略成功率为30-60%，DOMINO训练的策略为0-10%，展示了sim-to-real迁移能力。

Conclusion: DynaForge通过规划引导的残差修正与隐式课程，有效提升了动态操作演示生成的质量与效率，并显著增强了下游策略在仿真和真实场景中的表现与sim-to-real迁移能力。

Abstract: Dynamic object manipulation is essential for robots operating in real-world environments, yet methods for generating high-quality demonstrations remain limited. Methods designed for static tasks do not readily transfer to dynamic settings. Among dynamic demonstration generators, planning-based methods can fail near contact, while DOMINO-style replay simplifies dynamic interactions and may limit the experience available for policy learning. We present DynaForge, a planning-guided framework that learns residual corrections for dynamic manipulation demonstration generation. DynaForge combines low-frequency global planning with high-frequency object-centric inverse kinematics across task phases, and applies a residual policy to correct actions during dynamic interaction. An implicit curriculum groups rollouts under matched conditions and selects mixed-success groups, focusing residual reinforcement learning on the evolving competence frontier. On Can and Bottle, it uses 0.73x as many optimizer steps as vanilla GRPO at the same nominal environment-step budget, with higher observed final success rates. Across nine simulation tasks, DynaForge increases mean demonstration-generation success from 41.30% of the planning prior to 78.37%. With 800 demonstrations per task, DP3 policies trained on DynaForge data achieve 49.11% mean success, compared with 7.07% for DOMINO data. On three real-world dynamic tasks, DynaForge-trained policies achieve 30-60% success, compared with 0-10% for DOMINO-trained policies, showing the ability of DynaForge for sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [165] [RoboFollow: Unveiling the Instruction Following Mirage in Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25636)
*Chang Guo,Yukun Xie,Bohan Tan,Zheng Chang,Zhaokai Yin,Qianli Ma,Yingqiao Wang,Chao Liang,Zhipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboFollow诊断基准，揭示具身智能体"高成功率≠强指令跟随能力"的假象，根源在于"低场景熵"结构特性，并证明现有缓解手段无法弥合该鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体成功率虽高，但真实指令跟随能力远弱于数值所示。作者将这一错觉归因于"低场景熵"：当视觉场景仅对应单一有效任务时，语言变得冗余，策略可在几乎不使用语言的情况下获得高分。因此需要一个能诊断真实指令跟随能力的基准。

Method: 提出RoboFollow诊断基准，遵循三原则：(1)高场景熵：每个训练场景支持多个运动学上不同的任务分支，使视觉不足、必须依赖语言；(2)分层诊断协议：L0–L3四级，逐步扰动视觉布局与语义，检验等价指令行为一致性及不同指令行为可区分性（涵盖空间关系、属性、轨迹约束、逻辑）；(3)混淆受控诊断：简化交互物体、限定动作于已训练集合，并分阶段报告Intent与Execution分数，将理解与运动执行分离。

Result: 对九个VLA与WAM策略的评测显示，即便达到较强的L0表现，在该微调设定下也无法可靠迁移到L1–L3。包括更强VLM骨干、QA协同训练、LangForce、Classifier-Free Guidance在内的代表性缓解手段均未能弥合该差距。

Conclusion: RoboFollow揭示真实指令跟随是当前具身智能体关键且被忽视的瓶颈。代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: Modern embodied agents achieve impressive success rates, yet their actual instruction-following ability is far weaker than these numbers suggest. We trace this illusion to a structural property we term low scene entropy: when a visual scene admits only one valid task, language becomes redundant and a policy can score highly while barely using it. We introduce RoboFollow, a diagnostic benchmark with three principles: (1) High Scene Entropy: each training scene supports multiple kinematically distinct task branches, making vision alone insufficient and forcing reliance on language. (2) Hierarchical Diagnostic Protocol: a four-level protocol (L0--L3) progressively perturbs visual layout and semantics, probing whether equivalent instructions yield consistent behavior and distinct ones yield discriminable behavior across spatial relations, attributes, trajectory constraints, and logic. (3) Confound-Controlled Diagnosis: we simplify interaction objects, restrict actions to the trained repertoire and report stage-wise Intent and Execution scores, isolating comprehension from motor execution. Evaluation of nine VLA and WAM policies shows that strong L0 performance, where attained, does not reliably transfer to L1--L3 under our fine-tuning setup. Representative mitigations, including stronger VLM backbones, QA co-training, LangForce, and Classifier-Free Guidance, all fail to close this gap. RoboFollow exposes genuine instruction following as a critical, overlooked bottleneck. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/AutoLab-SAI-SJTU/RoboFollow and https://huggingface.co/datasets/AutoLab-SJTU/robofollow-data.

</details>


### [166] [A Reconfigurable Bidirectional Cable-Driven Hip Exoskeleton with Swappable Bench/Backpack Dual-configuration Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25639)
*YuanLong Ji,Shuhan Xiang,Qihan Ye,Xingbang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可重构双向线驱髋关节外骨骼平台，可在台式固定与背包式无缆驱动间快速切换，并共享同一无缆可穿戴髋部接口，兼具实验室康复评估与移动助行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验室康复评估与系统开发需要充足的驱动与计算资源，而移动助行则要求无缆便携。现有平台难以在同一可复用硬件上同时满足这两种需求，如何将二者集成于一个平台是核心设计挑战。

Method: 设计可重构双向线驱动髋关节外骨骼平台，模块化调整驱动配置、末端执行器传感路径与底层控制接口；末端执行器（不含线缆与驱动单元）重0.405 kg，集成编码器与力矩传感器。开展台式导纳式运动跟踪实验、背包式开环力矩跟踪实验，并招募3名健康受试者佩戴myoMOTION进行人体穿戴实验。

Result: 验证了台式导纳运动跟踪与背包式开环力矩跟踪能力；完成台式↔背包式运动就绪切换30次试验，耗时30.1±16.3 s；构建小规模多模态可穿戴外骨骼步态数据集，包含台式跑步机8 min记录与背包式户外行走11 min记录。

Conclusion: 通过统一可穿戴结构、驱动接口与传感路径，该平台使同一髋关节外骨骼可分别在台式与背包式配置下验证，为跨场景的迭代开发与应用提供可复用硬件基础。

Abstract: Hip exoskeletons provide an important hardware basis for lower-limb rehabilitation and locomotor assistance. Laboratory rehabilitation assessment and system development require substantial actuation and computing resources, whereas mobile assistance requires untethered portability. Integrating both capabilities within one reusable platform remains a central design challenge. This paper presents a reconfigurable bidirectional cable-driven hip exoskeleton platform that rapidly switches between bench-mounted and backpack-mounted actuation while sharing one cable-free wearable hip interface. The platform modularly adapts the actuation configuration, end-effector sensing path, and low-level control interface. Each cable-driven end-effector weighs 0.405 kg, excluding the cable and actuation unit, and integrates an encoder and a torque sensor; experiments validated bench-mounted admittance-based motion tracking capability and backpack-mounted open-loop torque tracking. Human-worn experiments with three healthy participants used myoMOTION to evaluate the platform's wearable-side hip-motion sensing capability, verified bench-to-backpack and backpack-to-bench motion-ready switching across 30 trials in $30.1\pm16.3$ s, and formed a small-scale multimodal wearable-exoskeleton gait dataset for sensing validation and data-driven algorithm development, comprising 8 min bench-mounted treadmill records and 11 min backpack-mounted outdoor walking records. These results show that, by unifying the wearable structure, actuation interface, and sensing path, the proposed platform enables validation of the same hip exoskeleton in both bench-mounted and backpack-mounted configurations, providing reusable hardware for iterative development and applications across scenarios.

</details>


### [167] [Contact-Stable Deformable Tissue Simulation Using Implicit Integration and Live-Pose Grasp Constraints for Laparoscopic Surgery Robot Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25642)
*Juahn Oh,Dongho Yee,Jinseok Lee,Jiyul Lee,Yechan Seo,Seong Jeong,Minsung Kim,Seonho Shim,Younghoon Noh,Hyuk Choi,Youngbin Kong,Hyoun-Joong Kon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个由固定视角RGB-D手术野记录重建可变形组织的闭环手术机器人仿真器，核心为保持仿真稳定性的数值与接触设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环评估手术机器人需要能够变形、可抓取抬升并还原真实解剖结构的组织，而现有仿真环境难以同时满足这些条件。

Method: 从固定视角RGB-D记录重建组织，去除器械后合成、封闭为水密体并四面体化（流程统一应用于两个物种三份标本）；将器官置于双臂仿真单元中，两台Franka FR3通过6 mm套管操作电动器械；采用隐式积分、仿真网格与碰撞网格分离、数值保护机制，以及在活体组织位姿下捕获的抓取约束。

Result: 13个器官、146,061个四面体且无反转单元；45次重复抓取抬升中，摩擦抓取15次失败，约束抓取各成功13/15；位移组织上静息位姿约束产生最大17.8 mm单步跳变，活体位姿捕获可消除；前表面深度误差1.33–1.41 mm，器官轮廓IoU 0.80；五次视频复现抓取抬升的地标位移RMSE为11.8 mm，静态预测为14.2 mm。

Conclusion: 该环境定位于闭环可行性、安全性、接触与策略筛选，不声称具备生物保真度。

Abstract: Closed-loop evaluation of surgical robots requires tissue that deforms, can be grasped and lifted, and reproduces the anatomy in which the robot will operate. We present a simulator in which this tissue is reconstructed from a fixed-view RGB-D recording of the surgical field, composited to remove the instruments, closed into watertight volumes and tetrahedralised; the pipeline was applied unchanged to three specimens of two species (thirteen organs, 146,061 tetrahedra, no inverted elements). For one specimen, the organs are placed in a bimanual cell in which two Franka FR3 arms operate motorised instruments through 6 mm trocars. The core contribution is the numerical and contact design that keeps this cell stable: implicit integration, simulation meshes separate from collision meshes, numerical guards, and a grasp constraint captured at the live tissue pose. In 45 repeated grasp-lifts, a friction grasp held the tissue in 0 of 15 trials and each constraint grasp in 13 of 15; on displaced tissue, a rest-pose constraint produced one-step snaps of up to 17.8 mm, which live-pose capture eliminates. Against the recording, front-surface depth error is 1.33 to 1.41 mm, organ silhouette IoU is 0.80, and in five grasp-lifts reproduced from video the landmark displacement RMSE is 11.8 mm against 14.2 mm for a static prediction. Biofidelity is not claimed; the environment is intended for closed-loop feasibility, safety, contact and policy screening.

</details>


### [168] [Skill Sequence Planning for Collaborative Multi-Robot Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25649)
*Xi Wang,Bo Fu,Carol C. Menassa,Vineet R. Kamat,Min Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种技能序列规划方法，使异构多功能机器人团队利用可复用的预编程技能（抓取、钻孔、紧固等）协作完成建筑装配任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在建筑流程自动化方面潜力巨大，但行业采用受限，部分原因是将机器人适配到多样化任务所需的编程工作量大。方法旨在减少为每种任务变体单独编程机器人的需求。

Method: 中央控制器将建筑的数字化表示转换为施工关系图，表示施工实体、其状态及父子关系。系统据此选择下一个施工目标，为机器人团队中有能力的成员生成符号化技能序列，并生成无碰撞的几何运动规划。随着施工状态变化，符号规划问题被动态重新生成。交互式数字孪生向人类协作者展示规划的技能序列与机器人状态，供其在执行前审查和批准。

Result: 通过一个建筑装配案例研究进行评估，验证了该方法的效果（摘要未给出具体量化指标）。

Conclusion: 通过减少为每种任务变体单独编程机器人的需求，该方法支持协作机器人团队在建筑中更灵活地部署。

Abstract: Robots have significant potential to automate construction processes. However, their industry adoption remains limited, partly because of the programming effort required to adapt robots to diverse tasks. This paper presents a skill sequence planning method that enables a heterogeneous team of multi-functional robots to collaboratively perform construction assembly work using reusable, preprogrammed skills such as grasping, drilling, and fastening. A central controller transforms the digital representation of the building into a construction relationship graph that represents construction entities, their states, and their parent-child relationships. Based on this representation, the system selects the next construction target, generates a symbolic sequence of skills for capable members of the robot team, and produces collision-free geometric motion plans for skill execution. The symbolic planning problem is dynamically regenerated as the construction state changes. An interactive digital twin presents the planned skill sequence and robot states to human co-workers for review and approval before execution. The method is evaluated through a construction assembly case study. By reducing the need to program robots separately for each task variation, the proposed approach supports more flexible deployment of collaborative robot teams in construction.

</details>


### [169] [PhyVisGen: Physically and Visually High-Fidelity Robotic Manipulation Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25653)
*Yu Zheng,Qiyu Feng,Yixin Wu,Baoquan Yang,Yixuan Zhou,Bingyang Hu,Kemeng Huang,Guansheng Yang,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO
