### [241] [Multiform Longest Edge Bisection of Tetrahedra via Sextuple Permutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26522)
*Agustin Trujillo,Jose Pablo Suarez,Tania Moreno-García*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 该论文提出在六元组空间 R6 中重新表述四面体的最长边二分（LEB），将四面体用边长平方表示，使细分方程线性化并摆脱坐标数据结构；通过 Multiform LEB（MLEB）刻画多值最长边选择，引入二分模式（sextuple 置换序列）；证明共享同一 LEB 模式的六元组集合在 R6 中构成凸区域，并证明 R1+ 族与 Liu-Joe 族的无限细分树可坍缩为具有八个状态的有限有向图，且两族共享同一图、仅初始状态不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维 LEB 的核心困难在于四面体可能具有多条最长边，使细分规则本质上是多值的；传统基于坐标的数据结构处理这种情况较为复杂。作者因此希望在纯“边长平方”表示下建立线性、组合化的 LEB 分析框架，为深度迭代细分提供高效工具。

Method: 1）将四面体表示为其六条边长平方构成的 sextuple（R6 中的点），在该表示下 LEB 细分方程完全线性，避免坐标结构。2）针对多最长边情形提出 Multiform Longest Edge Bisection（MLEB），系统枚举所有可允许的最长边选择。3）用“二分模式”（bisection patterns）编码多值行为，即控制细分过程的 sextuple 置换序列。4）证明同一 LEB 模式对应的 sextuple 集合为 R6 中的凸区域。5）对 R1+ 族与 Liu-Joe 族等结构重要四面体族，分析其无限细分树，将其压缩为有限有向图。

Result: 共享同一 LEB 模式的 sextuple 集合构成 R6 中的凸区域；对于 R1+ 族和 Liu-Joe 族，无限细分树坍缩为仅有八个状态的有限有向图；且这两个族由同一有向图支配，区别仅在于初始状态不同。

Conclusion: 该有向图表述给出了细分过程的统一组合描述，并为深层迭代 LEB 分析提供了高效计算框架。

Abstract: We introduce a new formulation of the Longest Edge Bisection (LEB) of tetrahedra entirely in sextuple space R6, where tetrahedra are represented by the squares of their edge lengths. This representation renders the LEB refinement equations fully linear and eliminates the need for coordinate-based data structures.
  A central difficulty in three-dimensional LEB arises when a tetrahedron possesses multiple longest edges, making the refinement rule intrinsically multivalued. We formalize this phenomenon through the notion of Multiform Longest Edge Bisection (MLEB), which systematically explores all admissible longest-edge choices. To encode this multivalued behavior, we introduce the concept of bisection patterns, defined as sequences of sextuple permutations governing the refinement process.
  We prove that the set of sextuples sharing a common LEB pattern forms a convex region in R6. For structurally significant families of tetrahedra, including the R1+ family and the Liu-Joe family, we show that the infinite refinement tree collapses into a finite directed graph with eight states. Remarkably, both families are governed by the same graph, differing only in their initial state.
  This directed-graph formulation provides a unified combinatorial description of the refinement process and offers an efficient computational framework for deep iterative LEB analysis.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [242] [MIISO: Modal Integrators for Isogeometric Stabilization of Outliers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25087)
*Sreeram Shankar*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出MIISO——一族指数Rosenbrock-Krylov时间积分器，在不修改空间离散的前提下，于时间积分层面消除等几何高阶离散谱顶端的非物理离群模态，保持物理模态无耗散，二阶至四阶无条件A稳定且全局收敛，支持非线性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶等几何离散会在离散谱顶端产生少量非物理离群模态（outlier modes），这些模态会污染波传播与结构动力学问题的数值解。现有补救措施（无离群基、质量集中、子空间投影等）均需修改空间离散，带来额外代价或改变原离散精度特性。作者希望在保持空间离散不变的前提下，仅在时间积分层面抑制离群模态。

Method: 提出MIISO（Modal Integrators for Isogeometric Stabilization of Outliers），一族指数Rosenbrock-Krylov积分器：先识别离群模态；再构造时间积分格式，使离群模态被湮灭，而物理模态不引入数值耗散。积分器通过Krylov近似实现无矩阵（matrix-free）计算，兼具效率与可扩展性；机制不限于等几何分析，可推广至任何可刻画谱病态的方法。

Result: 该积分器族无条件A稳定，在二阶、三阶、四阶全局收敛，并可推广至非线性问题。数值实验在多种等几何问题上验证了离群模态与物理模态的预期行为，与其他时间积分方法相比：MIISO达到与已有空间离群模态去除方法相当的效果，但不改变离散格式；同时再现理论收敛阶，并在相同精度下计算成本低于generalized-alpha方法。

Conclusion: MIISO通过时间积分层面的模态选择性处理，无需修改空间离散即可有效抑制等几何高阶离散的离群模态污染，兼顾精度、稳定性与效率，且具有向其他谱病态方法推广的潜力。

Abstract: High-order isogeometric discretizations produce a small number of non-physical outlier modes at the top of the discrete spectrum. These modes can pollute the numerical solution in wave propagation and structural dynamics. Most existing remedies modify the spatial discretization through outlier-free bases, mass lumping, subspace projection, and other methods. We introduce MIISO (Modal Integrators for Isogeometric Stabilization of Outliers), a family of exponential Rosenbrock-Krylov integrators that suppress outlier modes temporally while leaving the spatial discretization unmodified. The method identifies the outliers and modifies the time-integration scheme to annihilate them while leaving physical modes without dissipation. The integrators are evaluated matrix-free through Krylov approximation, making them efficient and scalable. While developed for isogeometric analysis, the mechanism extends to any method in which a spectral pathology can be characterized. The family is unconditionally A-stable and globally convergent at orders two, three, and four, and it extends to nonlinear problems. Numerical experiments confirm the intended outlier and physical mode behavior on a variety of isogeometric problems compared to other time integration methods, and show that MIISO matches previous spatial outlier removal without changing the discretization. They also recover the predicted convergence orders and show lower cost than generalized-alpha at matched accuracy.

</details>


### [243] [An unconditionally stable second-order Robin partitioned method for fluid-poroelastic structure interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25350)
*Wenlong He,Thomas Wick,Xiaohe Yue,Jiwei Zhang,Haibiao Zheng*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该文研究非定常流体-多孔弹性结构相互作用（FPSI）问题，通过Robin型界面条件将Stokes方程与全动态Biot系统解耦，提出一阶与二阶Robin分区格式，证明无条件稳定性，并用数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FPSI问题中流体域与多孔弹性域强耦合，传统整体求解计算量大、难以灵活处理复杂构型；需要构造可将两子系统独立求解的稳定分区算法。

Method: 将物理耦合条件等价重写为Robin型界面条件，引入辅助界面变量作为两子系统共享的Robin数据，把耦合问题分解为流体与多孔弹性子问题。空间上采用有限元法（FEM）离散，时间上分别采用后向Euler格式与BDF2格式并结合二阶界面外推。利用离散动态关系推导辅助界面变量的更新公式，得到全离散一阶、二阶Robin分区格式，每步独立求解两子问题。

Result: 严格证明了两种格式的无条件稳定性；数值实验验证了时间收敛阶，考察了Robin参数的影响；经典压力波基准问题验证了方法精度，非线性移动域仿真展示了方法对更复杂FPSI构型的适用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的Robin分区格式实现了FPSI问题的解耦求解，具备无条件稳定性与预期收敛阶，能准确处理基准问题并适用于复杂移动域配置。

Abstract: We study an unsteady fluid-poroelastic structure interaction (FPSI) problem, in which the free fluid region is governed by the incompressible Stokes equations and the poroelastic region by the fully dynamic Biot system. The original physical coupling conditions are equivalently reformulated as Robin-type interface conditions and an auxiliary interface variable is introduced as common Robin data shared by the two subsystems. This reformulation decomposes the coupled FPSI problem into fluid and poroelastic subproblems posed on their respective subdomains. The problem is discretized in space by the finite element method (FEM), while the backward Euler scheme and the BDF2 scheme combined with the second-order interface extrapolation are employed for the temporal discretization. By exploiting the resulting discrete dynamic relations, we derive an update formula for the auxiliary interface variable and obtain fully discrete first- and second-order Robin partitioned schemes in which the two subproblems are solved independently at each time step. The unconditional stability of both schemes is rigorously established. Numerical experiments verify the temporal convergence orders of the proposed schemes and investigate the influence of the Robin parameter. A classical pressure-wave benchmark verifies the accuracy of the proposed methods, while supplementary nonlinear moving-domain simulations illustrate their applicability to more complex FPSI configurations.

</details>


### [244] [Three-dimensional blind deconvolution by CP-parameterized kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25394)
*Fatoumata Sanogo,Stefan Kindermann*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究3D盲反卷积中核$k$与图像$u$的联合重建问题，采用核的半参数CP分解与图像的变分TV惩罚，结合交替最小化、正性投影与核归一化等步骤，并讨论运动模糊歧义，在3D MRI、高光谱图像及灰度视频上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 盲反卷积中同时估计核与图像是病态问题，尤其3D体数据维度高、非唯一性严重。作者希望借助核的低秩结构先验与图像的TV正则，提升3D场景下的重建稳定性，并厘清运动对象视频中核的非唯一性（运动模糊歧义）问题。

Method: 对核$k$施加半参数CP（CANDECOMP/PARAFAC）分解以刻画其低秩结构，对图像$u$施加变分TV惩罚；采用交替最小化框架，图像步用TV正则求解，核步用CP分解更新，并引入正性投影、核归一化以及因果支撑投影等约束步骤。

Result: 在3D MRI图像、高光谱图像以及一段灰度测试视频上的多个数值实验表明，该方法能有效完成核与图像的盲反卷积重建；同时分析了运动模糊导致的核非唯一性现象及其影响。

Conclusion: 将核的CP低秩分解与图像TV正则相结合，并辅以投影与归一化约束的交替最小化策略，可有效处理3D盲反卷积；但运动模糊歧义会带来核的非唯一性，需要额外约束加以缓解。

Abstract: We consider the problem of reconstructing a kernel $k$ and image $u$ in a blind deconvolution problem from convolutional data $g = k*u$. We particularly focus on the 3D case, i.e., on volumetric images. The problem is approached by imposing a semiparametric CP decomposition for the kernel and a variational TV penalty for the image. We also discuss a motion blur ambiguity effect, i.e., a nonuniqueness issue of the kernel in the blind deconvolution problem that may appear in certain videos of moving objects. The blind deconvolution algorithm uses an alternating minimization approach with TV regularization for $u$ and a CP decomposition step for the kernel. These are implemented with positivity projection, kernel normalization, and\textbackslash or causal support projection steps. Several numerical examples for 3D MRI images, hyperspectral images, and a grayscale test video show the effectiveness of the method.

</details>


### [245] [Convergence of the original BGN method for mean curvature flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25402)
*Yifei Li*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明原始BGN方法在时间步长τ=h²时，其曲线平均曲率流的数值解在流形距离下以h²阶收敛。核心创新是引入后向误差分析，构造满足BGN方法且缺陷阶提升至h⁴（切向部分h⁶）的近似流，进而利用演化曲面有限元技术证明H¹超收敛，并确认τ=O(h²)是τ与h的正确关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始Barrett--Garcke--Nürnberg (BGN)方法虽广泛用于平均曲率流的数值求解，但其在流形距离度量下的收敛阶及时间步长与空间步长的正确匹配关系尚未被严格证明，缺乏理论保证。

Method: 引入源于常微分方程数值分析的后向误差分析：通过对离散速度进行空间与时间展开，构造一个近似流，使其插值以改进的缺陷（h⁴阶，切向部分h⁶阶）满足BGN方法；该构造同时识别出BGN方法的切向速度，并据此证明τ=O(h²)是τ与h的正确关系；随后结合演化曲面有限元方法证明数值解对该近似流的H¹超收敛。

Result: 证明了原始BGN方法在流形距离下以h²阶收敛（取τ=h²）；改进了缺陷阶至h⁴（切向h⁶）；得到数值解对近似流的H¹超收敛阶为h⁴，从而导出流形距离下的收敛性；数值实验验证了所预测的缺陷阶。

Conclusion: BGN方法在τ=h²时于流形距离度量下以h²阶收敛；后向误差分析结合演化曲面有限元技术是分析该类方法的有效工具，并明确了时间步长与空间步长应满足τ=O(h²)。

Abstract: We prove that the original Barrett--Garcke--Nürnberg (BGN) method for mean curvature flow of curves converges in the manifold distance with order $h^2$ for the time step $τ=h^2$. The key new technique is backward error analysis, which stems from the numerical analysis of ordinary differential equations. By combining the expansions of the discrete velocity in space and time, we construct an approximation flow whose interpolation satisfies the BGN method with an improved defect, of order $h^4$ with tangential part of order $h^6$. This construction also identifies the tangential velocity of the BGN method and shows that $τ=O(h^2)$ is the correct relation between $τ$ and $h$. With the improved defect, we then prove $H^1$ superconvergence of order $h^4$ of the numerical solution to the approximation flow using techniques of evolving surface finite element methods, which yields the convergence in the manifold distance. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted orders of defects.

</details>


### [246] [High-Order Variable-Scaled Energetic Variational Neural Networks for Phase-Field Gradient Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25489)
*Xiaobo Jing,Leiyi Dong,Jia Zhao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出高阶变尺度能量变分神经网络（VS-EVNN）方法，用于相场梯度流，通过尺度化网络输入并结合高阶多阶段变分外推与自适应时间步，提升了求解精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基线EVNN在相场梯度流求解中精度受限，需要一种能更好归一化能量梯度系数、并结合高阶时间离散格式的方法来提高解误差表现。

Method: 每个离散阶段采用更新状态网络，其空间输入以界面尺度为中心并做除法尺度化，而采样能量与运动惩罚项相对基线EVNN保持不变；该重尺度由能量坐标变换归一化梯度系数所驱动。结合高阶多阶段变分外推格式与启发式自适应时间步控制器，每阶段作为网络最小化问题求解，早期阶段作为求积网格上的固定锚点，从而得到多阶段变分格式的神经实现，并给出守恒质量的Cahn--Hilliard流动率加权版本。

Result: 在一维和二维Allen--Cahn与Cahn--Hilliard流动（后者在质量守恒度量下）上测试：在报告优化设置下，尺度化网络比未尺度化EVNN基线取得更低解误差，高阶格式在测试算例中提高了精度。

Conclusion: 变尺度表示结合高阶多阶段变分格式可有效提升相场梯度流神经求解器的精度，且质量守恒版本适用于Cahn--Hilliard流动。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a high-order variable-scaled energetic variational neural network (VS-EVNN) method for phase-field gradient flows. Each discretization stage uses an updated-state network whose spatial inputs are centered and divided by the interface scale, while the sampled energy and movement penalties remain unchanged relative to the baseline EVNN formulation. This rescaling is motivated by a coordinate change in the energy that normalizes the gradient coefficient of the transformed functional. We combine the scaled representation with high-order, multi-stage variational extrapolation schemes and a heuristic adaptive time-stepping controller. Each stage is solved as a network minimization problem, in which the earlier stages serve as fixed anchors on the quadrature grid, yielding a neural realization of these multi-stage variational schemes, including a mobility-weighted, mass-conserving version for Cahn--Hilliard flows. The method is tested on one- and two-dimensional Allen--Cahn and Cahn--Hilliard flows, the latter in the mass-conserving metric. Under the reported optimization settings, the scaled networks yield lower solution errors than the unscaled EVNN baseline, and the higher-order schemes improve the accuracy in the test examples.

</details>


### [247] [AKAPINN: Adaptive Kolmogorov-Arnold Physics-Informed Neural Networks for approximating solutions to quasilinear partial differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25507)
*B. Veena S. N. Rao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文对无网格KAN-PINN求解非线性应变限制偏微分方程开展受控对比研究，发现训练损失与相对L2误差之间存在指标倒置现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非线性应变限制偏微分方程，评估无网格KAN-PINN的求解性能，并考察不同材料参数组合下的训练行为与精度表现。

Method: 在固定计算流程下进行三组不同材料参数对(α, β)的训练：采用精确硬边界ansatz、鲁棒的pseudo-Huber残差损失，以及多阶段Adam-to-L-BFGS优化器调度。

Result: 分析揭示出根本性的指标倒置：训练损失反映的是与算子相关的残差尺度及本构刚度，而算子无关的相对L2误差表明，中间参数区间反而获得更优的求解精度与更强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 训练损失不宜作为该类问题的精度代理指标，应以算子无关的相对L2误差评估模型性能；中间参数区间在精度与泛化上更具优势。

Abstract: A controlled comparative study of a mesh-free Kolmogorov--Arnold Physics-Informed Neural Network (KAN-PINN) applied to nonlinear strain-limiting partial differential equations is presented. Three distinct training runs across varying material-parameter pairs $(α, β)$ are evaluated within a fixed computational pipeline comprising an exact hard boundary ansatz, a robust pseudo-Huber residual loss, and a multi-stage Adam-to-L-BFGS optimizer schedule. A fundamental metric inversion is revealed by the analysis: while training loss reflects operator-specific residual scaling and constitutive stiffness, the operator-independent relative $L_2$ error demonstrates that intermediate parameter regimes achieve superior solution accuracy and enhanced generalization.

</details>


### [248] [On a Conjecture Related to Eigenvalue Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25552)
*Lei-Hong Zhang,Ren-Cang Li*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明了Li关于Hadamard乘积特征值扰动界猜想中,当单位不变范数取Frobenius范数时成立,且最优常数c=1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Li在特征值扰动界研究中提出了三个涉及Hadamard乘积的最小化问题,其中第三个问题探究非奇异矩阵W下的极小值与置换矩阵P下的极小值之间的关系,并猜想存在与矩阵规模无关的反向不等式。Romeo和Tilli指出若该界对任意G成立,则范数须与Frobenius范数一致等价,这促使作者在Frobenius范数下直接验证该猜想。

Method: 作者针对Frobenius范数情形,对任意复矩阵G建立等式,证明左端非奇异W极小值与右端置换矩阵P极小值相等,从而给出最优常数c=1。

Result: 证明了当单位不变范数为Frobenius范数时,对任意复矩阵G有 \min_{W nonsingular}\|W∘G\|_F\|W^{-1}∘G^T\|_F = \min_{P permutation}\|P∘G\|_F^2。

Conclusion: 相应的特征值扰动界猜想在Frobenius范数下成立,且最优常数为c=1,证实了Li猜想的紧性。

Abstract: In connection with eigenvalue perturbation bounds, Li [Linear Algebra Appl., 278 (1998), pp. 317-326] formulated three minimization problems involving Hadamard products. The third asks how to relate $$ \min_{W\ \mathrm{nonsingular}}\|W\circ G\|_\mathrm{ui}\,\|W^{-1}\circ G^\mathrm{T}\|_\mathrm{ui} \quad\mbox{and}\quad \min_{P\ \mathrm{permutation}}\|P\circ G\|_\mathrm{ui}^2, $$ where $\|\cdot\|_\mathrm{ui}$ denotes a unitarily invariant norm and $\circ$ denotes the Hadamard product, i.e., entrywise multiplication. The first quantity never exceeds the second, and a reverse inequality with a constant independent of the matrix size was conjectured. Romeo and Tilli [Linear Algebra Appl., 326 (2001), pp. 161-172] showed that if such a bound holds for every $G$, then $\|\cdot\|_\mathrm{ui}$ must be uniformly equivalent to the Frobenius norm $\|\cdot\|_\mathrm{F}$. We prove that the two quantities coincide when $\|\cdot\|_\mathrm{ui}=\|\cdot\|_\mathrm{F}$: for every complex matrix $G$, $$ \min_{W\ \mathrm{nonsingular}} \|W\circ G\|_\mathrm{F}\,\|W^{-1}\circ G^\mathrm{T}\|_\mathrm{F} =\min_{P\ \mathrm{permutation}}\|P\circ G\|_\mathrm{F}^2. $$ The associated conjecture therefore holds with the optimal constant $c=1$.

</details>


### [249] [The Scott-Vogelius element is inf-sup stable on Freudenthal meshes for $k \geq 4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25690)
*Siqi Ding,Pingbing Ming,Haijun Yu,Quanfan Zhu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明Scott-Vogelius元在Freudenthal网格上对任意速度次数k≥4均inf-sup稳定，解决了Farrell、Mitchell和Scott于2024年提出的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Scott-Vogelius元是Stokes问题的经典无散度混合有限元，但三维情形理论尚不完整：Zhang于2011年只证明了k≥6时的inf-sup稳定性，而数值证据显示k=4,5时也成立，这一猜想悬而未决。

Method: 强化Zhang的方法，通过局部片（local patches）上的显式构造进行证明，不依赖计算机验证。

Result: 证明了Scott-Vogelius元在Freudenthal网格上对每个速度次数k≥4均满足inf-sup稳定性，从而解决了Farrell-Mitchell-Scott 2024年提出的猜想。

Conclusion: 该工作完善了Scott-Vogelius元在三维Freudenthal网格上的理论框架，将稳定性结论从k≥6推广至k≥4。

Abstract: The Scott-Vogelius element is a classical divergence-free mixed finite element for the Stokes problem that has attracted decades of research attention, yet its theoretical framework remains incomplete. In two dimensions, the inf-sup stability on Freudenthal and other regular meshes has been rigorously established. In three dimensions, Zhang established inf-sup stability on Freudenthal meshes for $k\ge6$ in 2011, while numerical evidence indicates that inf-sup stability remains true for $k=4,5$. In this paper, we strengthen Zhang's approach and prove that the Scott--Vogelius element is inf-sup stable on Freudenthal meshes for every velocity degree $k\ge 4$, thereby resolving a conjecture proposed by Farrell, Mitchell, and Scott in 2024. The proof proceeds by explicit constructions on local patches and does not rely on computer verification.

</details>


### [250] [Uniform approximation of spectra of linear second order differential operators via discrete sine transform based discretisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25796)
*Oliver Křenek,Vít Průša,Rebecca Tozzi,Martin Vejvoda*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究有界区间上正则Sturm–Liouville算子及任意平面区域上Laplace算子（均带零Dirichlet边界条件）的离散化格式，目标是使N×N离散矩阵的N个特征值尽可能逼近连续算子的前N个特征值。数值实验表明传统离散化因高指标特征值逼近不佳而失败；新提出的基于离散正弦变换（DST）的格式能复现高指标特征值的渐近行为，实现一致谱逼近，且可应用于非矩形区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离散化格式虽能较好逼近低指标特征值，但对高指标特征值逼近很差，导致整体谱逼近不均匀；因此需要设计一种能使N×N矩阵的N个特征值一致逼近连续算子前N个特征值的离散化方案。

Method: 针对零Dirichlet边界条件下的正则Sturm–Liouville算子（有界区间）与Laplace算子（任意平面区域），通过数值实验考察多种传统离散化格式，并提出一种基于离散正弦变换（DST）的离散化格式，其构造使得高指标特征值的渐近行为得以复现。

Result: 数值实验证实传统离散化无法实现目标，其失败源于高指标特征值逼近差、谱逼近不均匀；所提出的DST离散化格式成功复现高指标特征值的渐近行为，实现了均匀（一致）谱逼近，并可应用于非矩形区域，克服了许多基于Fourier变换方法仅适用于矩形区域的限制。

Conclusion: 基于离散正弦变换的离散化格式通过匹配高指标特征值的渐近行为，实现了对连续算子前N个特征值的一致逼近，且适用于任意平面区域，优于传统离散化与多数Fourier变换方法。

Abstract: We study various discretisation schemes for the regular Sturm--Liouville operator on a bounded interval and for the Laplace operator on an arbitrary planar domain, in both cases subject to zero Dirichlet boundary conditions. The objective is to identify a discretisation scheme such that the corresponding discretised operator---a matrix of size $N \times N$---produces $N$ eigenvalues that approximate as closely as possible the first $N$ eigenvalues of the corresponding operator at the continuous level. By means of numerical experiments we examine several conventional discretisation schemes, and we corroborate the known fact that the conventional discretisations fail to achieve the objective, with the failure attributable to the poor approximation of high-index eigenvalues, and, as a result, to the non-uniform spectrum approximation. In contrast, the newly proposed discrete sine transform based discretisation scheme is designed in such a way that it replicates the asymptotic behaviour of high-index eigenvalues, thereby providing the sought uniform spectrum approximation. Of equal significance is the fact that the proposed discrete sine transform based scheme can be, unlike many Fourier transform based methods, applied to non-rectangular domains.

</details>


### [251] [Anisotropic Kernel-based Multilevel Interpolation of High-Dimensional Functions on Sparse Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25828)
*Rüdiger Kempf*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 基于低维多层算子的节点表示，本文给出TPML高维插值的显式可计算形式，证明混合正则Sobolev与连续模误差估计并刻画最优各向异性权重范围，提出三种权重策略，十维实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TPML虽结合Smolyak稀疏网格与核多层插值（每层核尺度与填充距离耦合以保证条件数一致有界），但其原始表述仅隐式依赖目标函数，无法进行数值求值与实现。

Method: 利用近期给出的低维多层算子节点表示，推导高维插值的显式可计算表示；据此在混合正则Sobolev范数与连续模下建立误差估计，推广已有L2理论，并刻画能达到最优收敛率（相对自由度）的各向异性权重全范围。

Result: 获得显式高维插值表示；给出混合正则Sobolev与连续性范数误差估计；刻画最优各向异性权重范围；导出三种显式权重策略，分别均衡精度、自由度与性价比；十维数值实验验证理论。

Conclusion: TPML由此从隐式框架变为可计算且具完备误差与权重理论的散点高维逼近方法，三种权重策略为实际参数选择提供依据。

Abstract: The tensor product multilevel method (TPML) approximates high-dimensional functions from scattered data on general bounded domains by combining Smolyak's sparse grid construction with the kernel-based multilevel interpolation, coupling the kernel scale to the fill distance at each level so that condition numbers stay uniformly bounded. Its original formulation depended only implicitly on the target function, which precluded numerical evaluation. Using a recent nodal representation of the low-dimensional multilevel operators, we derive an explicit, computable representation of the high-dimensional interpolant. On this basis we prove error estimates in mixed-regularity Sobolev and continuity norms, extending the existing $ L_2 $-theory, and we characterise the full range of anisotropy weights that attain the optimal rate against the number of degrees of freedom. Three explicit weight strategies follow, equilibrating the accuracy, the degrees of freedom, or the cost-benefit ratio. Numerical experiments in up to ten dimensions illustrate the theory.

</details>


### [252] [A Multirate Transient Simulation Technique for Circuits described by Integro-Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25854)
*Hans Georg Brachtendorf,Kai Bittner*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文讨论射频电路仿真中的多速率偏微分方程（PDE）技术及其数值求解方法，并指出在混合集总/分布器件与脉冲波形场景下的局限，进而提出结合等效复基带（ECB）方法的耦合系统/电路仿真需发展新的积分微分方程数值技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射频电路仿真需要高效且精确的数值方法。多速率PDE技术虽强大，但在混合集总/分布器件且波形呈脉冲状时，现有数值技术（包络调制HB、多步有限差分、样条/小波）均存在缺陷；同时通信系统采用ECB方法建模，信号与冲激响应通过Hilbert变换以基带形式描述，导致耦合系统/电路仿真需要新的数值技术。

Method: 采用多速率偏微分方程技术，将底层常微分代数方程（DAE）重构为PDE，并沿PDE的特征曲线获得DAE解。数值求解技术包括包络调制谐波平衡（HB）、多步有限差分（FD）以及样条/小波方法。通信系统则通过等效复基带（ECB）方法建模，利用Hilbert变换以基带对应形式描述信号与冲激响应。

Result: 指出现有各数值技术在混合集总/分布器件及脉冲状波形情况下均存在不足；ECB方法下信号与冲激响应以基带形式表示，使得耦合系统/电路仿真面临积分微分方程求解的新需求。

Conclusion: 多速率PDE技术是射频电路仿真的有力手段，但现有数值方法在混合器件与脉冲波形场景下有局限；结合ECB建模的耦合系统/电路仿真需要发展新的积分微分方程数值求解技术。

Abstract: The multirate partial differential equation (PDE) technique is considered as one of the most powerful methods for the simulation of radio frequency (RF) circuits. The method reformulates the underlying ordinary differential algebraic equation (DAE) by a PDE. The DAE solution is obtained along a characteristic curve of the PDE. Numerical solution techniques comprise (envelope modulated) Harmonic Balance (HB), multistep finite difference (FD) and spline/wavelet techniques. All techniques have disadvantages when mixed lumped/distributed devices occur and the waveforms exhibit pulse shaped behavior. Furthermore, communication systems are modeled and simulated by the Equivalent Complex Baseband (ECB) approach. Signals and impulse responses are described
  by their baseband counterparts, employing the Hilbert transformation. A coupled system/circuit simulation requires therefore novel numerical techniques for the solution of integro-differential equations.

</details>


### [253] [The inverse elastic scattering problem in the cavity with impedance boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25858)
*Shiyu Xu,Huiling Zheng,Fang Zeng*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 研究二维阻抗边界空腔中的弹性逆散射问题，证明内部散射场满足互易关系，并证明空腔形状与边界阻抗参数可由内部曲线上的散射场唯一确定；数值上先用线性采样法重建形状，再结合位势理论与最小二乘法恢复阻抗参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对带阻抗边界条件的二维空腔弹性逆散射问题，关注能否从内部测量数据同时确定空腔几何形状与边界阻抗参数，即解的唯一性及相应的数值反演方法。

Method: 理论上建立空腔内部散射场的互易关系，并据此给出形状与阻抗参数的唯一性证明；数值上采用线性采样法重建空腔形状，再结合位势理论与最小二乘法反演边界阻抗参数。

Result: 证明了内部散射场的互易关系，并证明空腔形状与边界阻抗参数可由内部曲线上的散射场测量唯一确定；数值实验验证了所提方法在形状重建与阻抗参数反演上的可行性与有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为阻抗边界空腔的弹性逆散射提供了唯一性理论与一套可行的两步数值反演方案，并通过数值实验验证其有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the elastic inverse scattering problem in a two-dimensional cavity with an impedance boundary condition. We prove that the scattered fields inside the cavity satisfy a reciprocity relation, and prove that both the shape of cavity and the impedance parameter on the boundary can be uniquely determined from the scattered fields measured on an interior curve. Numerically, we first employ the linear sampling method to reconstruct the shape, and then recover the impedance parameter by combining potential theory with the least squares method. Several numerical experiments are presented to demonstrate the feasibility and effectiveness of our methods.

</details>


### [254] [Enhanced attenuation modelling for multispectral computed tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25926)
*Jürgen Jeschke,Dirk Lorenz*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文重新审视多光谱CT物质分解建模，利用已知物质吸收谱推导新的唯一性条件，并提出三步重建模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多光谱CT物质分解问题在建模与求解上仍缺乏充分的理论保证，尤其是解的唯一性问题；作者希望借助已知的物质吸收谱信息改善建模，从而获得可先验验证的唯一性条件。

Method: 利用不同物质已知的吸收谱，重新对多光谱CT物质分解问题进行建模，据此推导新的唯一性条件，并提出一个三步重建模型。唯一性判据仅依赖能段灵敏度函数的特征与物质衰减谱，因此可在测量前计算。

Result: 给出了新的唯一性条件，并提出对应的三步重建模型；唯一性判据可在采集数据前先验计算。

Conclusion: 通过引入已知吸收谱，可在测量前对物质分解的可解性进行先验判断，并为多光谱CT物质分解提供新的重建框架。

Abstract: We revisit the modelling of the material decomposition problem in multispectral computed tomography and propose to leverage the known absorption spectra of different materials. With this we both derive new uniqueness conditions for the material decomposition and also offer a three-step reconstruction model. Our uniqueness criterion only requires certain characteristics of the bin sensitivity functions and the material attenuation spectra and hence, can be computed a-priori before measurements are taken.

</details>


### [255] [A structure-preserving staggered semi-implicit four-split finite volume scheme for continuum mechanics on unstructured meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25935)
*M. Dumbser,W. Boscheri,M. Tavelli,A. Thomann*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种半隐式保结构有限体积离散方法，用于统一的一阶双曲连续介质力学模型，通过四分裂格式消除声速、剪切和热波速带来的时间步限制，仅受材料速度约束，并在非结构网格上保持关键结构性质与渐近保持性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一的流体与固体双曲模型包含输运、粘性、热传导和弹性变形等多种机制，多个特征波速共存导致全显式离散面临严重的时间步限制。需要一种克服该困难且能保持方程结构性质的数值方法。

Method: 构造四分裂半隐式格式，将控制方程分解为对流、温度、机械和压力四个子系统；仅对流子系统显式推进，其余子系统按顺序隐式处理。同时在三角形非结构网格上采用相容的顶点交错空间离散，使失真场和热冲量对应的无旋内蕴约束在松弛源项线性或缺失时得到尊重。

Result: 方法的时间步限制仅依赖材料速度，与声波、剪切和热波速无关。格式在低马赫数极限及GPR模型的刚性松弛极限下具有渐近保持性，可恢复经典Navier-Stokes-Fourier方程。涵盖流体与固体的数值实验验证了精度、鲁棒性和多尺度能力。

Conclusion: 该半隐式保结构有限体积方法为统一双曲连续介质力学模型提供了高效、稳健且多尺度的数值求解框架，兼顾结构保真与渐近保持特性。

Abstract: We present a new semi-implicit structure-preserving (SP) finite volume discretization for a unified first-order hyperbolic model of continuum mechanics. This framework provides a common mathematical description for fluids and solids and incorporates transport, viscous effects, heat conduction, and elastic deformations within a single system of hyperbolic partial differential equations. The coexistence of several physical mechanisms gives rise to multiple characteristic wave speeds, resulting in severe time-step restrictions for fully explicit discretizations. To overcome this difficulty, we develop a four-split scheme in which the governing equations are decomposed into convective, temperature, mechanical, and pressure subsystems. The convective subsystem is the only one that is advanced explicitly in time, while the remaining subsystems are treated implicitly in a sequential manner. As a consequence, the time step restriction of the proposed method depends only on the material velocity and is independent of the acoustic, shear, and thermal wave speeds that characterize the model.
  The scheme is designed to preserve key structural properties of the underlying equations on unstructured grids. In particular, a compatible vertex-staggered spatial discretization on triangles allows the curl-free involutions associated with the distortion field and thermal impulse to be respected whenever the relaxation source terms in the governing PDE are linear or absent. At the same time, the scheme is asymptotic preserving as it is consistent with the low Mach number limit and with the stiff relaxation limits of the GPR model that recover the classical Navier-Stokes-Fourier equations in fluid mechanics. A series of numerical experiments covering both fluids and solids demonstrates the accuracy, robustness, and multi-scale capabilities of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [256] [A posteriori error estimates for variational inequalities of the second kind in an abstract framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26001)
*Lothar Banz,Miriam Schönauer,Andreas Schröder*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一个抽象框架，用于推导第二类变分不等式（变分不等式）的可靠后验误差估计量，并在一定条件下证明其（局部）有效性，应用于理想化摩擦接触问题和Mosolov型模型问题并给出数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对第二类变分不等式缺乏通用、可靠的a posteriori误差估计方法的问题，作者希望构建一个统一、可推广的抽象框架，以支持可靠且（局部）有效的误差估计。

Method: 基于等价的混合形式（mixed formulation），通过估计残差（residuum），并将Lagrange乘子替换为其（可能经过后处理的）离散对应物，从而推导后验误差估计量。该框架应用于hp有限元离散框架下的理想化摩擦接触问题与Mosolov型模型问题。

Result: 所提出的估计量具有可靠性；在特定条件下可获得（局部）有效性；数值实验表明该理论结果具有广泛的适用性。

Conclusion: 该抽象框架为第二类变分不等式的后验误差估计提供了统一且可扩展的理论工具，并在地化接触及Mosolov型问题中得到了验证。

Abstract: In this paper, an abstract framework is introduced for the derivation of reliable a posteriori error estimators for variational inequalities of the second kind. Under certain conditions (local) efficiency of the error estimators can also be derived. The framework is based on the estimation of a residuum which results from an equivalent mixed formulation and from the replacement of the Lagrange multiplier by its (possibly post-processed) discrete counterpart. The approach is applied to an idealized frictional contact problem and a Mosolov type model problem in an $hp$-finite element discretization setup. Several numerical experiments demonstrate the broad applicability of the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [257] [An Iterative Active Subspace Approach for Model Order Reduction of Parametric Systems with High-Dimensional Parameter Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26009)
*Chenzi Wang,Peizhi Yu,Lihong Feng,Peter Benner,Wenshuai Lu,Zheng You*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代主动子空间（IAS）方法，用于高维参数空间下的参数化模型降阶，以缓解精度与降阶模型规模之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维参数空间下的快速建模需求增长，高保真有限元模型代价高；参数化模型降阶（pMOR）受"维数灾难"限制；已有主动子空间（AS）方法在精度与降阶模型紧凑性之间难以平衡，模型往往不够小或不够准。

Method: 提出迭代主动子空间（IAS）方法，通过迭代方式构造主动子空间，用于参数化模型降阶，在精度与降阶模型规模之间取得更好的平衡。

Result: 在一定程度上解决了精度与降阶模型规模之间的权衡问题，相比原始主动子空间方法获得显著的计算增益。

Conclusion: IAS 为高维参数空间下的 pMOR 提供了一种有效途径，兼顾精度与紧凑性并提升计算效率。

Abstract: The increasing complexity in design and manufacturing has driven the need for advanced techniques for fast modeling problems with large-dimensional parameter spaces. Avoiding high-fidelity finite element models while achieving fast and accurate simulations in such contexts is challenging. Parametric model order reduction (pMOR) has drawn significant attention in recent years. Nevertheless, the curse of dimensionality in parameter spaces has severely limited its effectiveness. The active subspace (AS) approach has been successfully applied to pMOR for systems with many parameters. However, the balance between accuracy and compactness of the reduced model remains problematic for such systems with high-dimensional parameter spaces. It often results in models that are either not small enough or not accurate enough. In this paper, we propose an iterative active subspace (IAS) approach for parametric model order reduction, which, to some extent, addresses the trade-off between accuracy and reduced model size and achieves substantial computational gains compared to the original active subspace method.

</details>


### [258] [Troesch's equation with charge separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26075)
*Mihai Halic*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文将经典Troesch两点边值问题推广以纳入电荷分离效应：w''=L sinh(Lw)，w(0)=W_0<0<W_1=w(1)，并给出显式近似解及其精度验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Troesch问题未考虑电荷分离，而物理考量要求纳入该效应。推广后解在两端均出现边界层，与经典情形（仅一端边界层）不同，因此需要新的求解方法。

Method: 求解依赖于一类超定一阶边值问题的结果，据此构造显式近似解；并采用多种方式验证其精度。展示这类超定一阶边值问题本身也是文章的第二目标。

Result: 得到了推广方程的显式近似解，并在多种验证方式下确认了其精度；同时建立/给出了关于一类超定一阶边值问题的结果。

Conclusion: 推广后的Troesch问题解具有双端边界层结构，所提显式近似解精度良好；文中关于超定一阶边值问题的结果具有独立价值。

Abstract: We consider the following generalization of Troesch's classical two-point-BVP in order to take into account charge separation, which is required by physical considerations: $w''=L\sinh(Lw), w(0)=W_0<0<W_1=w(1)$. In contrast with the classical case, the solution of this generalized equation possesses boundary layer at both ends. We determine an explicit, approximate solution and verify its precision in several ways.
  The analytic solution hinges on a result concerning a class of overdetermined first order BVPs. Presenting this matter constitutes the second objective of the article.

</details>


### [259] [An implicit locking-free B-spline Material Point Method for large strain geotechnical modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26104)
*Mian Xie,Pedro Navas,Susana López-Querol*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种无锁定的隐式B样条物质点法（B-spline MPM），有效抑制应力振荡，并成功用于大变形岩土问题的数值模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统物质点法（MPM）因公式与有限元法相似且质点可独立于网格运动而广泛用于大变形岩土问题，但存在细胞穿越噪声和体积锁定等不稳定性，导致应力场强烈振荡，需要一种能控制应力振荡且易于实现的方法。

Method: 提出隐式无锁定B样条MPM；采用一种新颖且直接的B样条形函数实现流程，避免复杂的质点搜索算法，实现从原始MPM到B样条MPM的无缝转换；并提出易于实现、便于复现的体积锁定缓解策略。

Result: 通过三个数值算例验证：颗粒柱坍塌实验、边坡破坏以及大贯入基础。结果与文献实验及既有数值研究吻合良好，且相较其他方法给出更清晰的应力场。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够将应力振荡控制在可忽略水平，适用于大应变岩土问题建模，且实现简单、可复现，在模拟中具有优于现有方法的应力场表现。

Abstract: The Material Point Method (MPM) has drawn great attention in the numerical modelling of large deformation, geotechnical problems. The popularity of MPM is mainly because its formulation shares significant similarities with the Finite Element Method. In MPM, the iteration points can move independently from the mesh, allowing for the resolution of large deformation problems. However, because of this, the original MPM formulation suffers from the well-known cell-crossing noise and volumetric-locking instabilities, resulting in a strongly oscillated stress field. A novel implicit locking-free B-spline MPM that controls stress oscillations to a negligible level is proposed in this paper. A novel, but very straightforward B-spline shape function implementation procedure, avoids the need for a complex material point searching algorithm, providing seamless transformation from the original MPM to this robust B-spline MPM, aiming at modelling large-strain geotechnical problems. The newly proposed volumetric locking mitigation strategy is also very easy to implement, which facilitates the reproducibility of this research. The proposed method is validated against three numerical studies: granular column collapse experiment, slope failure and footing with large penetration. The proposed numerical method agrees well with experiments reported in the literature and previous numerical studies. Also, these numerical examples show that the proposed method provides a more prominent stress field than other available methodologies.

</details>


### [260] [Compact Finite-Average-Moment Schemes with Single-Step Oscillation Elimination for Hyperbolic Conservation Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26194)
*Chuan Fan,Kailiang Wu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出有限平均矩(FAM)框架，仅演化P¹矩状态，通过紧致线性矩重构实现最高六阶精度，并将非线性稳定化整合为单次无导数振荡消除(OE)过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶激波捕捉格式因宽模板、逐级非线性权重和昂贵的局部特征分解而存在严重计算瓶颈，需在保持高阶精度的同时降低演化自由度与计算成本。

Method: 构造紧致有限平均矩框架：空间形式精度与演化自由度解耦，仅演化单元平均值与缩放一阶矩(P¹)；采用紧致、多项式精确的线性矩重构达到k=3~6阶；非线性稳定化整合为仅在最终Runge-Kutta级之后施加的单次无导数OE过程，单元平均值严格守恒，对一阶矩施加显式指数修正；重构在守恒变量上逐分量进行。

Result: 傅里叶分析表明线性骨架具k阶精度、强伪模态阻尼及(k+1)阶单元平均超收敛；标量方程与Euler方程数值实验验证了光滑精度与稳健无振荡激波分辨；完整运行墙钟时间显著低于MR-WENO、HWENO-U与OE-HWENO。

Conclusion: FAM框架以少量演化自由度实现高阶紧致线性重构，通过单次OE稳定化兼顾守恒性、精度与激波捕捉能力，并在计算效率上优于现有高阶WENO类方法。

Abstract: High-order shock-capturing schemes often face severe computational bottlenecks due to wide stencils, stagewise nonlinear weights, and expensive local characteristic decompositions. To address this, we propose a compact finite-average-moment (FAM) framework for hyperbolic conservation laws that completely decouples the formal spatial order from the number of evolved local degrees of freedom. By evolving only a $\mathbb{P}^1$ moment state (i.e., cell averages and scaled first-order moments), we achieve up to sixth-order accuracy ($k=3, 4, 5, 6$) via compact, polynomially exact \emph{linear} moment reconstructions. A key algorithmic innovation is consolidating the nonlinear stabilization into a single, derivative-free oscillation-elimination (OE) procedure applied only after the final Runge--Kutta stage. This OE procedure exactly preserves cell averages while applying an explicit exponential correction to the first-order moments, avoiding repeated stagewise nonlinear weights or limiting. Fourier analysis of the linear backbone demonstrates $k$th-order accuracy, strong spurious mode damping, and $(k+1)$th-order cell-average superconvergence. Extensive numerical experiments on scalar laws and the Euler equations verify the expected smooth accuracy and robust, nonoscillatory shock resolution. Notably, by performing componentwise reconstruction directly in conservative variables and streamlining stabilization, the FAM schemes deliver significantly lower complete-run wall-clock times compared to the multi-resolution weighted essentially non-oscillatory (MR-WENO), unified-stencil Hermite WENO (HWENO-U), and OE-HWENO methods.

</details>


### [261] [Runge--Kutta-Aligned Oscillation Elimination: Restoring Superconvergence for Fully Discrete Shock-Capturing DG Schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26195)
*Manting Peng,Zhuoyun Li,Kailiang Wu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种与Runge--Kutta (RK) 对齐的消振荡 (OE) DG 框架，使全离散非线性稳定格式恢复超收敛性质，并在一、二维线性守恒律中严格证明了 $(k+2)$ 阶超收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性稳定对双曲守恒律的DG离散不可或缺，但通常会破坏超收敛分析所需的精细误差结构，导致现有理论多局限于无振荡控制的线性或半离散格式。本文旨在弥合这一理论与实际全离散非线性稳定格式之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出RK对齐的OE-DG框架：将OE过程中的伪时间步与累计RK阶段系数同步，从而激活标准OEDG无法获得的低阶界面误差抵消机制；构造阶段对齐的修正函数以补偿非线性OE源项；发现保持出流边平均的二维投影算子以封闭离散位移估计；并给出设计此类具超收敛性质的OE型DG格式的一般指导原则。

Result: 严格证明该全离散格式对一、二维线性守恒律达到 $(k+2)$ 阶超收敛（相对于精确解的定制投影），同时保留原方法的无振荡激波捕捉能力；数值实验验证了预测的超收敛阶，并表明RK对齐保持了OEDG对强间断问题的无参数鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RK对齐的OE-DG框架成功在保持非线性稳定与激波捕捉能力的同时恢复超收敛性质，为设计具超收敛性的OE型DG格式提供了理论原则与构造工具。

Abstract: Nonlinear stabilization is indispensable for discontinuous Galerkin (DG) discretizations of hyperbolic conservation laws, yet it typically disrupts the delicate error structure required for superconvergence analysis. Consequently, existing theory has largely been restricted to linear or semi-discrete schemes lacking oscillation control. This paper bridges this gap by proposing a Runge--Kutta (RK) aligned oscillation-eliminating (OE) DG framework that restores the superconvergence properties. By synchronizing the pseudo-time step in the OE procedure with the cumulative RK stage coefficients, we unlock a cancellation mechanism for low-order interface errors that is inaccessible to standard OEDG formulations. We rigorously prove that this fully discrete scheme achieves $(k+2)$-th order superconvergence to a tailored projection of the exact solution for linear conservation laws in both one and two dimensions, while maintaining the non-oscillatory shock-capturing capabilities of the original method. Moreover, we establish a general guiding principle for designing a class of OE-type DG schemes that exhibit such superconvergence. Key theoretical innovations include the construction of stage-aligned correction functions to compensate for nonlinear OE sources and the discovery of a two-dimensional projection operator that preserves outflow-edge averages, a property essential to close the discrete shift estimates. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted superconvergence rates and demonstrate that RK alignment preserves the parameter-free robustness of OEDG for problems with strong discontinuities.

</details>


### [262] [Sharp Approximation on the Sphere by Linearized Shallow Networks with Analytic Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26276)
*Jia Li,Tong Mao,Jinchao Xu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究单位球面上解析激活函数的线性化浅层网络的谱行为与逼近性质，推导了超球系数的渐近公式，并给出具有Sharp Sobolev逼近速率与系数ℓ²控制的逼近定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Mhaskar关于解析带函数球面逼近工作的启发，需要理解tanh、logistic sigmoid等固定解析激活函数所张成的线性浅层网络在球面上表达函数的谱机制，特别是超球系数的高阶渐近、指数衰减尺度与符号振荡，并据此建立Sharp逼近率。

Method: 针对相关奇点为虚轴上共轭单极点的实解析函数，用留数显式推导其超球系数的渐近公式，得到在高阶子序列上的匹配下界，并确定指数衰减尺度与符号振荡；对tanh、logistic sigmoid及有理激活直接适用，对arctan通过求导处理。随后在拟均匀点{θ_j*}上构造线性组合f_n(η)=Σ a_j σ(θ_j*·η)，分析H^r到H^s的逼近误差与系数规范化ℓ²界。

Result: 给出了超球系数的渐近公式及其匹配下界，识别了指数衰减尺度与符号振荡；对满足谱兼容条件的f∈H^r(S^d)，得到Sharp Sobolev速率‖f−f_n‖_{H^s}≲n^{-(r-s)/d}‖f‖_{H^r}；同时对谱兼容解析目标函数给出解析收敛率，并提供系数的显式规范化ℓ²界。

Conclusion: 分析将超球系数非零阶处的谱渐近与下界、Sobolev范数逼近以及固定解析激活函数的系数控制统一起来，为球面上线性浅层网络的逼近能力提供了谱理论刻画。

Abstract: We study the spectral behavior and approximation properties of linearized shallow networks with analytic activation functions on the unit sphere $\mathbb S^d$. Motivated by earlier spherical approximation results for analytic zonal functions \cite{mhaskar1999approximation}, we first derive an asymptotic formula, expressed explicitly in terms of the residues, for the ultraspherical coefficients of a real-analytic function whose relevant singularities are a conjugate pair of simple poles on the imaginary axis. In particular, the formula gives a matching lower bound along the relevant high-degree subsequences and identifies the exponential decay scale and sign oscillation. It applies directly to $\tanh$, the logistic sigmoid, and the rational activations considered here, while the corresponding estimate for $\arctan$ is obtained through differentiation.
  For such activation functions and quasi-uniform points $\{θ_j^\ast\}_{j=1}^n \subset \mathbb S^d$, functions $f \in \mathcal H^r(\mathbb S^d)$ satisfying the appropriate spectral compatibility condition can be approximated by a linear combination $$f_n(η) = \sum_{j=1}^n a_j σ(θ_j^\ast \cdot η),$$ with the sharp Sobolev rate $$\|f - f_n\|_{\mathcal H^s(\mathbb S^d)} \lesssim n^{-\frac{r-s}{d}} \|f\|_{\mathcal H^r(\mathbb S^d)}.$$ The approximation theorem places the $\mathcal H^s$ norm on the left-hand side and simultaneously provides an explicit normalized $\ell^2$ bound on the coefficients. For spectrally compatible analytic target functions, the same construction further gives the stated analytic convergence rate. Thus, the analysis connects spectral asymptotics and lower bounds at the degrees where the ultraspherical coefficients do not vanish with Sobolev-norm approximation and coefficient control for fixed analytic activation functions.

</details>


### [263] [Spiral Endgames for Computing Singular Endpoints of Homotopy Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26370)
*Emma R. Cobian,Jonathan D. Hauenstein,Charles W. Wampler*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种螺旋终局方法，用对数螺线采样点并以截断Puiseux级数插值逼近奇异解端点，统一了Cauchy（圆采样）与幂级数（线性采样）两种极端情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同伦路径终点为奇异解时，预测-校正路径跟踪可能在端点处失败。已有终局方法中，圆采样（Cauchy终局）数值条件好但需要多次重抽样、更新效率低；线性采样（幂级数终局）更新效率高但数值条件差。需要一种能兼顾二者优点的采样方案。

Method: 不在路径上跟踪至终点，而是在入射路径上采集非奇异点，用截断Puiseux级数插值逼近端点。沿复平面上的对数螺线采集样本点，构造螺旋终局；该螺线在极限情形下分别退化为圆采样（Cauchy终局）与线性采样（幂级数终局），从而将二者统一为同一框架的两个极端。文中对各方法的数值条件进行了分析与比较。

Result: 通过数值条件分析与比较，以及对复解析系统奇异解的计算实例，验证了螺旋采样方法相对Cauchy终局圆采样方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 螺旋终局在Cauchy终局的优良数值条件与幂级数终局的高更新效率之间取得平衡，是计算复解析系统奇异解的一种有效新方法。

Abstract: We develop a new endgame for numerically computing singular solutions to complex analytic systems by means of a homotopy path whose endpoint is the solution to be computed. When the solution is singular, prediction-correction path tracking may fail at the endpoint. Instead of tracking the path all the way to its end, endgames sample nonsingular points on the incoming path and approximate the endpoint by interpolating the samples with a truncated Puiseux series. By collecting sample points along a logarithmic spiral in the complex plane, the proposed spiral endgame balances between the superior numerical conditioning of a circular sample (known as the Cauchy endgame) and the update efficiency of a linear sample (known as the power series endgame), these being tied together as two extremes of the new approach. The numerical conditioning of the methods are analyzed and compared, and computational examples illustrate the effectiveness of the new spiral sampling method compared with the circular sampling method of the Cauchy endgame.

</details>


### [264] [Fast Karhunen-Loève Expansions via FFT-Accelerated Toeplitz Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26459)
*Nils Wildt,Wolfgang Nowak*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 利用Toeplitz结构与FFT循环嵌入，将KLE特征求解的协方差矩阵-向量乘加速至O(N log N)，避免稠密矩阵组装，存储从O(N²)降至O(2^d N)，单线程加速从中位数18×（N=4096）提升至183×（N=2^15），并可推广到非可分核、d维及任意域上的分片常数场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gaussian随机场在随机PDE、不确定性量化和地统计模拟中应用广泛，常通过截断KLE获得。但KLE需计算N×N协方差矩阵的主导特征对（N为网格单元总数），Krylov求解器虽只需矩阵-向量积，但稠密存储需O(N²)内存、每次积需O(N²)时间，在大规模问题上不可行。

Method: 针对等距网格上的平稳核，协方差矩阵呈（分块）Toeplitz结构，利用基于FFT的循环嵌入（circulant embedding）在O(N log N)时间内完成矩阵-向量积，且无需组装稠密矩阵。将该构造推广至非可分核与d维情形（使用分块Toeplitz矩阵），并扩展至张量网格子集所表示的任意域上的分片常数场。

Result: 在单线程匹配对比中，特征求解的中位加速比从N=4096时的18×增长到N=2^15时的183×。计算收益随问题规模增大而提升，存储需求从O(N²)降至O(2^d N)，使得标准形式下原本不可行的大规模离散场计算成为可能。

Conclusion: FFT循环嵌入的无矩阵KLE方法显著降低计算与存储开销，且适用于非可分核、多维及任意域分片常数场，随规模增大收益更明显。

Abstract: Gaussian random fields are a versatile tool used in the fields of stochastic PDEs, uncertainty quantification, and geostatistical simulation. One way to obtain them is to use a truncated Karhunen--Loève expansion (KLE). Computing the expansion requires the leading eigenpairs of an $N \times N$ covariance matrix, where $N$ is the total number of grid cells. These are usually computed with a Krylov eigensolver, which relies on the covariance operator only within matrix--vector products. Stored densely, the matrix takes $\mathcal{O}(N^{2})$ memory and each product $\mathcal{O}(N^{2})$ time. For a stationary kernel on an equispaced grid, the covariance matrix becomes (block-) Toeplitz and the product evaluates in $\mathcal{O}(N\log N)$ time using FFT-based circulant embedding, without the need to assemble the dense matrix. In a matched single-threaded comparison, the median speedup of the eigensolve grows from $18 \times$ at $N = 4096$ to $183 \times$ at $N = 2^{15}$. This makes it possible to compute discretized fields that would otherwise be infeasible to compute in the standard formulation. We show that the same construction carries over to non-separable kernels as well as $d$ dimensions, using block-Toeplitz matrices. We extend it to piecewise-constant fields on arbitrary domains given as subsets of a tensor grid. Computational savings grow with problem size, and storage drops from $\mathcal{O}(N^{2})$ to $\mathcal{O}(2^{d}N)$.

</details>


### [265] [Linear Robin-type Domain Decomposition scheme for Mixed Formulation of Richards' Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26478)
*Åsmund v. B. Synnevåg,Wietse M. Boon,Florin A. Radu,Sarah E. Gasda*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出求解Richards方程混合形式的线性Robin型区域分解格式(mLRDD)，采用非重叠分解隔离不同材料，Robin型界面条件耦合子域，混合有限元离散保证局部质量守恒，严格证明收敛性，2D/3D数值算例验证效率与稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变饱和多孔介质渗流中，强非均质性(多种材料组成多块/多层)使Richards方程混合形式求解困难。需将各材料隔离以在子域内获得近均质条件，从而高效稳健求解。

Method: 对Richards方程混合形式施加非重叠区域分解，隔离各材料得到近均质子域；时间上采用后向Euler离散，L-scheme线性化；推导与混合形式一致的Robin型界面条件耦合子域；空间上采用混合有限元保证局部质量守恒并提供与Robin条件一致的流量变量。

Result: 严格证明了格式的收敛性；2D/3D非均质多域数值算例表明格式高效且稳健，并数值验证了理论收敛结果。

Conclusion: 所提线性Robin型区域分解格式(mLRDD)适用于强非均质多孔介质中Richards方程混合形式的求解，兼顾局部质量守恒、收敛性与数值效率。

Abstract: In this work, we present a linear Robin-type domain decomposition scheme for solving the mixed formulation of Richards' equation (mLRDD-scheme), governing flow in variably saturated porous media. Assuming a highly heterogeneous porous medium consisting of multiple blocks/layers of distinct materials, we apply non-overlapping domain decomposition to isolate individual materials, yielding near-homogeneous conditions within each subdomain. Richards' equation is discretized in time by backward Euler and linearized using the L-scheme. A Robin-type interface condition, consistent with the mixed formulation, is then derived to couple the subdomains. For spatial discretization, mixed finite elements are employed, ensuring local mass conservation and providing the flux variables consistent with the Robin condition. The convergence of the proposed scheme is rigorously proved. Heterogeneous, multidomain numerical examples in 2D/3D are presented to demonstrate the efficiency and robustness of the scheme, along with numerical validation of the theoretical convergence results.

</details>


### [266] [Error Analysis for Solving Elliptic Interface Problems with Randomized Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26534)
*Jun Hu,Sidi Wu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文对随机神经网络（RaNNs）求解椭圆界面问题进行了统一误差分析，从Barron函数积分表示、泛化界到优化保证，给出界面不连续下的先验L2误差估计，并用数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 椭圆界面问题解在界面处存在不连续（低正则性），传统数值方法误差分析困难；随机神经网络虽在求解PDE中表现良好，但缺乏针对界面问题的统一误差理论，尤其是同时满足PDE、边界与界面条件的复合损失的定量保证。

Method: 1) 使用标准tanh激活函数推导Barron函数的积分表示，得到适用于任意支撑在立方体上、密度严格为正的隐层参数分布的C^2逼近界；2) 对同时施加PDE、边界和界面条件的复合损失建立O(n^{-1})的定量泛化界，n为训练样本数；3) 结合RaNN的优化保证，推导界面不连续情形下神经网络解的先验L2误差估计。

Result: 得到C^2逼近界与复合损失的O(n^{-1})泛化界，并据此给出界面不连续下神经网络解的先验L2误差估计；数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为随机神经网络求解椭圆界面问题提供了统一的误差分析框架，将逼近、泛化与优化误差整合为先验L2误差估计，适用于界面不连续情形。

Abstract: This paper presents a unified error analysis of randomized neural networks (RaNNs) for elliptic interface problems. We first derive an integral representation of Barron functions using the standard $\tanh$ activation, which yields a $C^2$-approximation bound valid for any hidden-parameter distribution supported on a cube with strictly positive density. We then establish a quantitative $\mathcal{O}(n^{-1})$ generalization bound for the composite loss that enforces the partial differential equation, boundary, and interface conditions simultaneously, where $n$ denotes the number of training samples. Combining these results with the optimization guarantees for RaNNs, we obtain an a priori $L^2$ error estimate for the neural network solution in the presence of interface discontinuities. Numerical experiments are presented to validate the theoretical results.

</details>


### [267] [NEPv Approach for Optimization on Stiefel Manifold with the $(2,1)$-norm Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26675)
*Ren-Cang Li,Li Wang,Lei-Hong Zhang,Zhaojun Bai*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出统一的NEPv框架，用于高效求解Stiefel流形上带(2,1)-范数正则的优化问题，适用于PCA、LDA、OCCA等机器学习目标的行稀疏投影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行稀疏投影在机器学习（如特征选择）中用于挑选最相关特征。将PCA、LDA、OCCA等目标与矩阵(2,1)-范数正则结合可获高质量行稀疏投影，但该正则非光滑，导致Stiefel流形上的优化问题难以高效求解。

Method: 建立统一的NEPv（非线性特征值问题）框架，处理Stiefel流形上带(2,1)-范数正则的优化；并分析该正则项的作用，将框架推广到数据科学中多种常见学习目标与(2,1)-范数正则的组合。

Result: 通过数值实验展示NEPv方法的有效性，并揭示实际应用中合适的正则化参数应具备的性质。

Conclusion: NEPv框架为Stiefel流形上带(2,1)-范数正则的优化提供了统一且高效的求解途径，具有广泛适用性，并为正则化参数选择提供指导。

Abstract: Row-sparse projection provides a useful tool in machine learning (ML) when it comes to, for example, feature selection, aiming to choose most relevant features for various ML objectives. One way to seek a high quality row-sparse projection is to combine an ML objective, such as the ones for PCA, LDA, and OCCA, with the matrix $(2,1)$-norm regularization which is nonsmooth. Such combinations result in challenging optimization problems on the Stiefel manifold that need to be solved efficiently. In this paper, a unifying NEPv framework is established to efficiently deal with optimization on the Stiefel manifold with the $(2,1)$-norm regularization. The effect of the $(2,1)$-norm regularization is also investigated. The wide applicability of the framework is demonstrated through the combinations of common learning objectives in today's data science applications with the $(2,1)$-norm regularization. Numerical experiments are presented to illustrate the use of the NEPv approach and to gain insights as to what a proper regularizing parameter should have in real-world applications.

</details>


### [268] [Fully Discrete Multi-Entropy Stability of High-Order Schemes for Compressible MHD: A Weak-to-Strong Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26791)
*Huihui Cao,Kailiang Wu,Caiyou Yuan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文建立了理想可压缩MHD方程在一般多面体网格上任意高阶有限体积与DG近似的全离散弱到强(W2S)多熵稳定性理论，使单次数值更新同时满足任意给定凸Harten熵对族的离散熵不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理熵仅在密度与压强为正时才有定义，因此核心困难在于协调离散熵稳定性与正性保持、磁场散度约束以及非守恒Godunov-Powell耦合之间的冲突。

Method: 基于Wu与Shu的可证正性保持MHD框架，对有限体积与DG方法的单元平均值演化开展弱多熵分析；通过凸分解与相对熵给出一般多面体网格上弱稳定的两个充分准则；利用相容磁修正与局部无散近似抵消非守恒Godunov-Powell耦合并提供离散消去；再通过W2S提升将正性与熵限制统一为单元逐点缩放限制器，并采用变步长强稳定性保持多步法获得高阶时间精度。

Result: 数值实验验证了理论结论，并展示了计算鲁棒性；所构造的限制器在实现强多熵稳定性的同时严格保持单元平均值与局部无散磁场。

Conclusion: 该框架为高阶MHD近似中统一物理容许性、磁场散度控制与多熵稳定性提供了严格的全离散基础。

Abstract: We establish a fully discrete weak-to-strong (W2S) multi-entropy stability theory for arbitrarily high-order finite-volume and discontinuous Galerkin (DG) approximations of the ideal compressible magnetohydrodynamics (MHD) equations on general polytopal meshes, whereby a single numerical update simultaneously satisfies discrete entropy inequalities for any prescribed finite family of convex Harten entropy pairs. Because physical entropies are defined only for positive density and pressure, the central analytical difficulty lies in reconciling discrete entropy stability with positivity preservation, the magnetic divergence constraint, and the nonconservative Godunov--Powell coupling.
  Building upon the provably positivity-preserving MHD framework of Wu and Shu, we develop a weak multi-entropy analysis of the common cell-average evolution of finite-volume and DG methods. Through convex decomposition and relative entropy, we establish two sufficient criteria for this weak stability general polytopal meshes. Compatible magnetic corrections and locally divergence-free approximations cooperatively offset the nonconservative Godunov--Powell coupling and provide the discrete cancellations essential to both positivity-preservation and entropy stability. A W2S lifting then unifies positivity and entropy limiting into a single cellwise scaling limiter, achieving strong multi-entropy stability while strictly preserving cell averages and the locally divergence-free magnetic field. High-order temporal accuracy follows from variable-step strong-stability-preserving multistep methods. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical findings and demonstrate computational robustness. This framework provides a rigorous, fully discrete foundation for unifying physical admissibility, magnetic divergence control, and multi-entropy stability in high-order MHD approximations.
